To capture threat actors early in the reconnaissance phase, we developed two machine learning frameworks to detect LDAP reconnaissance: an ML classifier to predict malicious LDAP queries and an ML-based data-mining method to extract malicious query signatures. By correlating LDAP queries with endpoint detections, the first framework uses weak supervision to label a massive dataset and classify LDAP queries as malicious or benign. For immediate deployment, a second technique was developed on top of this approach to employ a rigorous statistical hypothesis-testing framework for mining novel, malicious LDAP signatures. While this weakly supervised approach is limited compared with manual human labeling, it is more practical for this use case because it leverages large-scale automated corpus construction, reducing costs and time. Ultimately, both the LDAP classifier and the ML-based LDAP signature mining method achieved performance benchmarks, with the classifier achieving up to a 65\% True Positive Rate (TPR) on the holdout set while limiting false positives, and mined signatures demonstrating 81.48\% field precision with CrowdStrike's Managed Detection and Response team.

</details>


### [38] [Towards Improved Anomaly Detection for Cloud Cybersecurity via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28923)
*Manu Nandan,TJ Jaymes,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的自监督学习方法，应用于AWS CloudTrail日志，用于检测云环境中的可疑安全事件。该方法为每个事件生成异常评分，并能动态适应组织变化而无需周期性重新训练。在五个组织的实验中，该方法将告警量从传统方法的数千条/小时降至约1条/小时，但研究仅评估了被标记事件，无法估计假阴性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云基础设施的广泛使用使得安全威胁检测成为必要，而基于启发式或异常检测的自动化方法由于静态特性容易产生高误报率，亟需一种能动态适应且告警量更低的检测方法。

Method: 采用图神经网络的自监督学习方法，对AWS CloudTrail日志中的事件进行建模，为每个事件生成异常评分，实现无需周期性重新训练的动态自适应检测。

Result: 在五个组织的实验中，该模型在几乎所有情况下生成的告警量远低于基于领域专家规则的基线方法，将告警量从传统方法的数千条/小时降低至约1条/小时。

Conclusion: 该研究有效降低了云安全告警量，但仅为实际部署研究而非完全验证的检测系统，无法估计假阴性率，未来需扩展到其他云环境。

Abstract: Detecting security threats in an organization's cloud computing environment has become necessary due to the increased reliance on cloud infrastructure. Logging of all cloud computing events enables investigation into any incidents after they are detected. Automated detection of threats using the logs based on heuristics or anomaly detection could result in a high false positive rate due to its relatively static nature. In this article, we present an industrial case study of a self-supervised learning method using graph neural networks applied to AWS CloudTrail logs to surface suspicious events for analyst review. The model produces an anomaly score for each event and dynamically adapts to changes in the organization without requiring periodic retraining. Based on our experiments across five organizations, the proposed model produced substantially fewer alerts than a domain expert rule-based baseline in almost all cases, reducing alert volumes to approximately 1 per hour from thousands generated by traditional methods. We note that this evaluation covers only flagged events, and false negatives cannot be estimated from the current data; findings should therefore be interpreted as a practical deployment study offering insights into real-world constraints rather than a fully validated detection system. We discuss these limitations and the requirements for extending the approach to other cloud environments as future work.

</details>


### [39] [Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925)
*Ananto Nayan Bala,Faisal Muhammad Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于WildChat的多智能体路由基准，包含3,000个提示和12个智能体目录，采用集合级评估指标和成本约束分析。实验表明监督路由方法显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM方法，其中微调编码器在无约束条件下表现最佳，线性多标签模型是最强实用基线，加权路由层可进一步提升成本约束下的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示下的工具和智能体路由本质上是集合预测问题——单个查询可能需要多个智能体，而过选会增加执行成本。现有研究缺乏标准化的多智能体路由评估基准和协议，无法系统研究准确性与成本之间的权衡。

Method: 从WildChat衍生出3,000个提示的基准数据集，在固定12智能体目录下使用AI辅助启发式标注和可控重平衡。评估协议包含集合级指标（精确率、召回率、F1、Jaccard、精确匹配）、延迟、面向执行的覆盖模拟，以及基于序数智能体成本层级的有约束加权路由设置。对比方法包括最近邻匹配、线性多标签分类、依赖感知基线、微调编码器、确定性的加权后评分（WAR）和零样本LLM基线。

Result: 监督路由方法显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由。微调编码器在无约束集合准确率上表现最强，线性多标签模型是最强实用基线。在有约束设定下，加权路由层在强监督评分器基础上提升了效用，其中Encoder+WAR增益最大。

Conclusion: 该基准和评估协议支持在固定目录多智能体路由中可重复地研究准确性与成本权衡。

Abstract: Tool and agent routing from natural-language prompts is naturally a set-valued prediction problem: a single query may require multiple agents, while over-selection increases execution cost. The benchmark introduced here is derived from WildChat and contains 3,000 prompts over a fixed 12-agent catalog, with AI-assisted heuristic labels under a fixed schema and controlled rebalancing for multi-label evaluation. The evaluation protocol combines set-level metrics (Precision, Recall, F1, Jaccard, and Exact Match), latency, an execution-oriented capability-coverage simulation, and a constrained weighted-routing setting based on ordinal agent-cost tiers. Compared methods include nearest-neighbor matching, linear multilabel classification, dependency-aware baselines, a fine-tuned encoder, deterministic weighted post-scoring via Weighted Agent Routing (WAR), and a zero-shot LLM baseline. Results show that supervised routers substantially outperform nearest-neighbor and zero-shot LLM routing. The fine-tuned encoder achieves the strongest unconstrained set accuracy, while the linear multilabel model provides the strongest practical baseline. In the constrained setting, the weighted routing layer improves utility when applied on top of strong supervised scorers, with the largest gain observed for Encoder+WAR. Overall, the benchmark and evaluation protocol support reproducible study of accuracy-cost trade-offs in fixed-catalog multi-agent routing.

</details>


### [40] [DLR: Zero-Inference-Cost Latent Residuals for Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28932)
*Dong Wang,Wenwu Tang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DLR（复制潜在残差）的插件方法，通过在低秩预训练中引入固定结构化残差来增强性能，不增加可学习参数，训练后可折叠回低秩骨架，在60M至7B参数的LLaMA模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型预训练成本极高，低秩预训练虽能减少参数和FLOPs，但质量通常落后于全秩训练，需要在不增加部署开销的前提下提升低秩预训练质量。

Method: DLR在标准低秩输出Bz上添加固定结构化残差 (alpha/sqrt(K)) * Expand_K(z)，其中K=ceil(d_out/r)，将每个潜在坐标复制K次；alpha固定，无额外可学习参数；训练后通过闭式解B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T吸收到上投影中，保持部署时参数、FLOPs和内存与低秩骨干一致。

Result: 在60M至7B参数的LLaMA模型上，DLR在大多数设置下提升了C4验证集困惑度，在130M及以上规模效果最明显；折叠后的检查点可干净地迁移到标准监督微调基准上。

Conclusion: DLR是一种有效的无参数、可折叠的插件方法，能够在不增加部署开销的前提下提升低秩预训练质量，且具有良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models have driven recent progress in language and multimodal AI, yet pre-training them at scale is prohibitively expensive. Low-rank pre-training, which factorizes each weight matrix into a rank-r product to reduce both parameters and FLOPs, is a promising response but typically lags full-rank training in quality. We propose Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR), a training-only, parameter-free, foldable plug-in for low-rank pre-training. DLR augments the standard low-rank output Bz with a fixed structured residual alpha/sqrt(K) * Expand_K(z) that replicates each latent coordinate K = ceil(d_out/r) times across the output. With alpha fixed, DLR adds zero learnable parameters per layer; after training, it is absorbed into the up-projection in closed form, B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T, so deployment parameter count, FLOPs and memory match the underlying low-rank backbone exactly. Across LLaMA models from 60M to 7B parameters, DLR strengthens low-rank pre-training on C4 validation perplexity in most settings, with the clearest gains at 130M and above; folded checkpoints transfer cleanly to supervised fine-tuning on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [41] [ReGuide: From Test-Time Guidance to Self-Improving Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28939)
*Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGuide是一种自改进框架，通过相位条件引导（PCG）生成可纠偏的轨迹数据，并将其吸收回策略中进行微调或重训练，从而有效解决行为克隆扩散策略中的协变量偏移问题，在多个机器人任务上取得1.3-7.7倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆扩散策略虽表达能力强大，但容易受到协变量偏移（covariate shift）的影响：微小状态偏差会累积导致任务失败。现有方法要么通过专家修正或合成增强来扩大训练分布（成本高或依赖假设），要么在测试时用学习模型引导冻结策略（丢弃了纠偏轨迹），缺乏对引导数据的复用。

Method: 提出ReGuide框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 相位条件引导（PCG）：构建相位特定的潜在目标，仅在偏离但可恢复区域施加引导，通过估计干净动作使动力学模型匹配训练分布；2) 两种吸收策略：ReGuide-FT（当前检查点微调）和ReGuide-FS（在增广数据集上从头训练），两者可组合迭代。成功的引导轨迹作为可复用的在线策略恢复数据被吸收回策略中。

Result: 在Robomimic的Can、Square、Transport和Tool Hang四个任务上，ReGuide将基础策略成功率提升1.3-7.7倍，在测试时引导设置中优于LPB，消融实验表明性能提升来源于引导恢复数据而非额外轨迹本身。

Conclusion: ReGuide通过将引导生成的纠偏轨迹作为可复用的自改进数据，有效缓解了扩散策略的协变量偏移问题，且无需依赖专家修正或合成数据假设，为行为克隆策略的测试时纠偏提供了新的数据高效范式。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned diffusion policies are expressive but remain vulnerable to covariate shift: small deviations from demonstrated states can compound into task failure. Existing methods address this either by expanding the training distribution through expert corrections or synthetic augmentation, or by steering a frozen policy at test time with guidance from a learned model. The former can be expensive or assumption-dependent, while the latter discards the corrected trajectories after execution. We introduce ReGuide, a self-improving framework that treats guided rollouts as reusable on-policy recovery data. ReGuide first uses Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG) to generate corrective rollouts: it constructs phase-specific latent targets, applies guidance only in the drifted-but-recoverable regime, and guides through the estimated clean action to match the dynamics model's training distribution. Successful guided rollouts are then absorbed back into the policy through ReGuide-FT, which fine-tunes the current checkpoint, or ReGuide-FS, which retrains from scratch on the augmented dataset; the two can also be composed and iterated. On Robomimic Can, Square, Transport, and Tool Hang, ReGuide improves base-policy success by $1.3$--$7.7\times$, outperforms LPB in the test-time-only setting, and matched-data ablations show that the gains come from guided recovery data rather than additional rollouts alone.

</details>


### [42] [Machine-learnable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28947)
*Veit Elser,Manish Krishan Lal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化定义来描述大型离散集合的三个特性：元素易识别、易生成、且这些任务易从样例中学习。将该形式化特化到二进制字符串集合，并基于有界复杂度的布尔自编码器定义了"机器可学习性"。实验使用布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器，证明了罗夏墨迹图案（镜像半幅可反转对比度）以及更"狂野"的集合（其元素仅近似被可接受的自编码器固定）的机器可学习性。对于后者，展示了一种简单迭代方法，使狂野集合逐步演化为真正机器可学习的集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为大型离散集合建立形式化定义，使其具备元素易识别、易生成、易从样例学习这三个特性，并基于此定义机器可学习性。

Method: 将形式化特化到二进制字符串集合，基于有界复杂度的布尔自编码器定义机器可学习性；使用布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器；对罗夏墨迹图案和更狂野的集合进行实验；对狂野集合采用简单迭代使其演化为机器可学习集合。

Result: 证明了罗夏墨迹图案（含镜像半幅对比度反转情况）的机器可学习性；证明了更狂野集合（元素仅近似固定）的机器可学习性；展示了一种迭代方法可将狂野集合演化为机器可学习集合。

Conclusion: 所提出的形式化定义和基于布尔自编码器的机器可学习性概念能够有效描述和实现大型离散集合的识别与生成任务，且可通过迭代使近似可学习的集合变得真正可学习。

Abstract: In this study we present a formal definition of large discrete sets having, informally, three properties: their elements are easily recognized, easily generated, and the latter tasks are easily learned from examples. The formalism is specialized to sets of binary strings and a definition of "machine-learnability" based on the existence of a bounded-complexity Boolean autoencoder that fixes the elements of the set. We present experiments where the autoencoders are implemented by nets of Boolean threshold functions. Machine-learnability is demonstrated for Rorschach patterns (that may have reversed contrast in the mirrored half), and considerably "wilder" sets whose elements are only approximately fixed by admissible autoencoders. In the second case we demonstrate a simple iteration that evolves wild sets to make them properly machine-learnable.

</details>


### [43] [Modification-Considering Value Learning for Reward Hacking Mitigation in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28955)
*Evgenii Opryshko,Umangi Jain,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCVL是一种基于价值学习的奖励篡改防御方法，通过过滤可能降低预期回报的过渡数据，在压制奖励篡改与允许合法改进之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体可能利用错误指定的奖励信号获得高表观回报但实际未能完成目标（奖励篡改）。现有防御方法通过约束策略靠近安全参考，导致压制篡改与允许改进之间存在矛盾。

Method: MCVL包装离线学习器，将每个传入过渡视为候选修改，预测包含和不包含该过渡的两条训练路径，并用冻结的自举回报估计器评分，仅当包含过渡不降低评分时才接受该过渡。

Result: 在四个安全相关网格世界和三个修改后的MuJoCo连续控制任务中，MCVL在缓解奖励篡改的同时继续改进预期目标。

Conclusion: MCVL形式化了过滤的安全性和容许性条件，并基于DDQN和TD3实现了具体实例，有效缓解奖励篡改问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents can exploit misspecified reward signals to achieve high apparent returns while failing on the intended objective, a failure mode known as reward hacking. Existing practical defenses typically constrain policy updates to stay near a known safe reference, creating a tension between suppressing hacking and permitting legitimate improvement. We propose Modification-Considering Value Learning (MCVL), which operationalizes the theoretical idea of current utility optimization for standard value-based RL. MCVL wraps an off-policy learner and treats each incoming transition as a candidate modification: it forecasts two training paths, one that includes the transition and one that does not, and scores both with a frozen bootstrapped-return estimator derived from a learned reward model and value function. The transition is admitted only if inclusion does not decrease the score. We formalize conditions under which this filtering is both safe and permissive, and instantiate MCVL with DDQN and TD3. Across four safety-relevant gridworlds and three modified MuJoCo continuous-control tasks with diverse hacking mechanisms, MCVL mitigates reward hacking while continuing to improve the intended objective. Project website: ktolnos.github.io/mcvl/.

</details>


### [44] [RGLD: Randomized Global-Local Density Estimation for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28970)
*Quanling Zhao,Jiaying Yang,Ye Tian,Josh Victoria,Zhijun Wang,Pietro Mercati,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGLD是一种结合全局随机特征密度估计和局部邻居分支的无监督表格异常检测方法，通过特征袋随机化视图暴露隐藏的异常证据，在47个数据集上超越了23个基线方法，实现了精度与效率的优越平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督表格异常检测方法存在两难：传统统计检测器效率高但依赖固定数据视图和单一异常概念；深度检测器可学习灵活评分函数但速度慢且因缺乏监督信号难以调参。需要一种兼顾精度、鲁棒性和计算效率的方法。

Method: RGLD由两个分支组成：1）全局随机特征密度分支，在随机特征空间中识别处于低密度区域的样本；2）局部邻居分支，检测缺乏邻近观测支撑的样本。两个分支均采用特征袋随机化视图，从不同表示空间综合暴露异常证据。

Result: 在47个表格数据集上对比23种统计和深度基线方法，RGLD在数据集级AUROC上排名第一（胜场最多），AUPRC胜场排名第二。速度方面，比所有深度检测器快50-580倍，与统计方法运行时间相当。

Conclusion: RGLD通过巧妙的随机特征袋化设计，结合全局密度估计与局部邻居分析，在无监督表格异常检测中实现了最优的精度-效率权衡，为实际应用提供了实用且强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised tabular anomaly detection requires methods that are accurate, robust across heterogeneous datasets, and computationally efficient. Classical statistical detectors are often efficient, but they usually rely on a fixed data view and a single notion of abnormality. Deep anomaly detectors can learn more flexible scoring functions, but they are substantially slower and difficult to tune in unsupervised settings due to the lack of a reliable supervisory signal. We propose RGLD, a randomized global-local density estimator for efficient unsupervised tabular anomaly detection. RGLD combines a global random-feature density branch, which identifies samples in broadly low-density regions, with a local neighbor branch, which detects samples that are weakly supported by nearby observations. Both branches operate over feature-bagged randomized views, allowing RGLD to expose anomaly evidence that may be hidden in any single representation. We conduct experiments on 47 tabular datasets against 23 statistical and deep anomaly detection baselines under fully unsupervised setting. RGLD achieves the strongest dataset-level AUROC performance, ranking 1st in dataset wins, and ranks 2nd in AUPRC wins. RGLD is also faster than all evaluated deep detectors, achieving 50x-580x speedups, and remains competitive with statistical methods in runtime, yielding a favorable accuracy-efficiency tradeoff.

</details>


### [45] [On Surrogate Modeling of Static Response of AM Short-Fiber Thermoplastics Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28996)
*Pharindra Pathak,Vipin Kumar,Trenton M. Ricks,Suhasini Gururaja,Siddhartha Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于GNN-LSTM混合架构的数据驱动替代模型，用于预测短纤维热塑性复合材料的力学行为，能够以超两个数量级的计算加速实现高精度（R²≈0.98）的应力-应变预测，并有效识别微观结构中的力学薄弱区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短纤维热塑性复合材料在轻量化结构中应用广泛，但其力学响应受纤维取向、空间聚集和制造孔隙等介观尺度特征显著影响，且存在空间变异性。虽然介观有限元模型可以解决这种异质性，但应用于真实三维微结构时计算成本过高，亟需高效替代方法。

Method: 基于微CT数据重建微观结构，离散化为Voronoi单元；通过非线性有限元模拟（含基体损伤）对每个单元进行均质化；构建混合图神经网络-长短期记忆（GNN-LSTM）架构，编码微结构拓扑与历史依赖的力学演化信息。

Result: 替代模型对未见微结构的刚度和应力-应变预测达到R²≈0.98，计算成本降低超过两个数量级；结合实验标定的损伤法则发现纤维取向、聚集和孔隙率共同决定局部有效刚度。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条物理信息引导、数据高效的路径，可用于识别力学薄弱的微结构单元，并加速SFT构件数字孪生开发。

Abstract: Short-fiber thermoplastic (SFT) composites are increasingly employed in lightweight aerospace and automotive structures owing to their favorable strength-to-weight ratio, high production rates, and recyclability. Unlike continuous-fiber systems, the mechanical response of SFTs is governed by mesoscale interactions among fiber orientation, spatial clustering, and manufacturing-induced porosity. These features exhibit significant spatial variability in manufactured components and influence stiffness, damage initiation, and nonlinear deformation. Although mesoscale finite element (FE) models can resolve such heterogeneity, their application to realistic three-dimensional microstructures remains computationally intractable.
  A data-driven surrogate framework is proposed to predict the mechanical behavior of additively manufactured, compression-molded (AM-CM) SFTs. Microstructures reconstructed from micro-computed tomography data were discretized into Voronoi-based cells representing distinct fiber-interaction neighborhoods. Each cell was homogenized via nonlinear FE simulations incorporating matrix damage, and the resulting stress-strain responses trained a hybrid Graph Neural Network-Long Short-Term Memory (GNN-LSTM) architecture encoding microstructural topology and history-dependent mechanical evolution.
  The surrogate accurately predicts stiffness and stress-strain behavior of unseen microstructures, achieving $R^2\approx 0.98$ relative to high-fidelity FE simulations with over two orders-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost. Coupling the framework with experimentally calibrated damage laws demonstrates that fiber orientation, clustering, and porosity collectively govern local effective stiffness. The approach provides a physics-informed, data-efficient pathway to identify mechanically weak microstructural cells and accelerate digital-twin development for SFT components.

</details>


### [46] [How Far Can Sharpness and Complexity Jointly Explain Generalization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29043)
*Ziyu Cheng,Xitong Zhang,Longxiu Huang,Rongrong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Pareto分析和线性回归，系统研究了锐度（sharpness）和复杂度（complexity）两个因素对深度神经网络泛化能力的联合解释力，提出更接近函数空间的定义，发现函数导向的锐度和复杂度定义能比参数级指标更有效地解释泛化，但仍有局限性，表明两因素视角尚不能构成完整的泛化理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泛化度量研究多聚焦于单个标量指标，锐度和复杂度的联合解释力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用线性回归和Pareto分析方法，量化评估锐度与复杂度的联合解释力；并提出更接近函数空间、减少对原始参数表示依赖的锐度和复杂度新定义。

Result: 函数导向的锐度和复杂度定义比传统的参数级指标能更好地联合解释泛化，扩展了两因素视角的解释范围。

Conclusion: 锐度-复杂度视角是理解深度神经网络泛化的有效框架，但尚不能作为完整的泛化理论。

Abstract: Sharpness and complexity are two central factors in the generalization analysis of deep neural networks. Existing quantitative evaluations of generalization measures have largely focused on individual scalar measures, leaving the joint explanatory power of sharpness and complexity largely unexplored. This work studies how far sharpness and complexity can jointly explain generalization. We use linear regression and introduce a Pareto-based analysis to quantitatively evaluate the joint explanatory power of these two factors. Beyond the existing parameter-level definitions, we further propose realizations of sharpness and complexity that are closer to function space and less dependent on raw parameter representations. We find that function-oriented definitions of these two quantities expand the explanatory scope of the two-factor view beyond what is achieved by existing parameter-level metrics. Overall, our results support the sharpness-complexity perspective as an informative lens for understanding generalization across diverse settings. At the same time, the remaining failures indicate that whether this two-factor view can serve as a complete theory of generalization remains open.

</details>


### [47] [MOSAIC: Orchestrating Collaborative Knowledge Tracing with Hierarchical Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29049)
*Xinjin Li,Mengyue Wang,Yuzhen Lin,Pengbin Feng,Ziqi Sha,Yeyang Zhou,Yu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一个融合LLM语义对齐与序列建模的多粒度知识追踪框架，通过冻结LLM生成动态嵌入和分层预测提示，并引入跨粒度一致性约束，在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA结果（AUC提升3.4%，准确率提升2.5%），尤其在协作丰富和长序列场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识追踪方法存在两个关键局限：一是依赖基于ID的浅层表示，缺乏语义深度；二是局限于单一粒度掌握度估计，忽视了层次化的知识依赖关系。

Method: MOSAIC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用冻结的LLM生成动态、上下文感知的嵌入和分层预测提示；(2) 显式捕获协作信号和同伴交互； (3) 引入跨粒度一致性目标，联合正则化概念级、主题簇级和全局熟练度三个层级的掌握度估计。

Result: 在ASSISTments、EdNet和新收集的大规模MOOC数据集上，MOSAIC取得了SOTA结果，AUC提升高达3.4%，准确率提升高达2.5%。在MOOC长序列场景下AUC达到0.862。

Conclusion: MOSAIC不仅提供了高预测精度，还具备语义可解释性，尤其在协作密集型环境和长序列场景中展现出优越的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge Tracing (KT) is important for personalized education but traditionally suffers from two key limitations: a reliance on shallow ID-based representations that neglect semantic depth and a restriction to single-granularity mastery estimation that overlooks hierarchical knowledge dependencies. To address these challenges, we propose MOSAIC (Multi-granularity Online Semantic AI for Collaborative Knowledge), a novel framework that orchestrates LLM-driven semantic alignment with sequential modeling. Unlike methods that use LLMs solely as predictors, MOSAIC leverages a frozen LLM to generate dynamic, context-aware embeddings and hierarchical prediction prompts, explicitly capturing collaborative signals and peer interactions. Furthermore, we introduce a cross-granularity consistency objective that jointly regularizes mastery estimation across concept, topic-cluster, and global proficiency levels. Extensive experiments on ASSISTments, EdNet, and a newly collected large-scale MOOC dataset demonstrate that MOSAIC establishes new state-of-the-art results. Specifically, our method achieves AUC improvements of up to 3.4\% and Accuracy gains of up to 2.5 \% across all benchmarks. Notably, MOSAIC exhibits superior robustness in collaboration-rich environments and long-sequence scenarios (AUC 0.862 on MOOC), offering both high predictive precision and semantically grounded interpretability.

</details>


### [48] [A Kernel Fisher Discriminant Analysis-Based Tree Ensemble Classifier: KFDA Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29053)
*Donghwan Kim,Seung Hwan Park,Jun-Geol Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核Fisher判别分析的集成分类器(KFDA Forest)，通过Bootstrap采样和随机子空间划分促进多样性，利用KFDA的核技巧处理非线性数据，以决策树为基分类器，在UCI和KEEL数据集上验证了其性能优于现有集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成分类器通常比单一分类器更准确，现有方法在非线性数据处理方面仍有提升空间，因此提出结合KFDA的树集成方法以兼顾分类精度和非线性数据处理能力。

Method: 1) 使用Bootstrap采样生成多样本；2) 随机将变量集划分为K个子集；3) 对每个子集执行KFDA（最大化类间距离、最小化类内距离），通过核技巧将输入空间变换到核特征空间完成旋转而非降维；4) 以决策树作为基分类器构建集成。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL真实数据集上的实验结果表明，KFDA Forest相比现有集成方法具有更优的分类性能。

Conclusion: KFDA Forest作为一种新的树集成方法，结合了KFDA的非线性处理能力和集成学习的多样性优势，能够有效提升分类准确率。

Abstract: In general, an ensemble classifier is more accurate than a single classifier. In this study, we propose an ensemble classifier called the kernel Fisher discriminant analysis forest (KFDA Forest), which is a tree-based ensemble method that applies KFDA. To promote diversity, bootstrap is used, and variable sets are randomly divided into K subsets. KFDA is performed on each subset to increase classification accuracy. KFDA maximizes the distance between classes while minimizing the distance within classes. KFDA can also be applied to classification problems in a nonlinear data structure using the kernel trick because it can transform the input space into a kernel feature space, commonly named a rotation, rather than performing a dimensionality reduction. Because new feature axes and KFDA projections are parallel, decision trees are used as a base classifier. To compare the proposed method with existing ensemble methods, we apply these to real datasets from the UCI and KEEL repositories.

</details>


### [49] [When Can Conformal Risk Control Certify LLM Outputs? Bounds, Impossibility, and Adaptation for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29054)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了共形风险控制（CRC）在结构化生成任务（NER、JSON提取、QA、分类）中对LLM输出进行可靠性认证的理论极限与实践方法。主要贡献包括：证明了一个不可能性定理（当基础风险μ>α时，任何无分布方法必须放弃至少(μ-α)/(1-α)的样本）；分析了Hoeffding、经验Bernstein和基于赌博的e-CRC三种认证边界的层级关系；验证了自适应共形推理（ACI）在跨数据集漂移下的有效性。实验覆盖6个开源模型、8个数据集、4个任务和6个非一致性分数，给出了三步骤部署方案：检查可行性、选择边界和分数、缓解漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于结构化生成的LLM缺乏形式化的可靠性保证，标准的启发式弃权策略在满足用户指定的风险目标方面存在7.5-12.5%的差距。因此需要研究何时共形风险控制（CRC）能够对结构化LLM输出进行认证，以及何时无法认证。

Method: （1）证明不可能性定理：当基础风险μ>α时，任何无分布方法必须放弃至少(μ-α)/(1-α)的样本，形成闭式可行性检验。（2）分析Hoeffding、经验Bernstein和e-CRC三种边界的认证层级，发现Hoeffding到Bernstein步骤增益最大（+37%认证配置），e-CRC在数据稀缺时优势明显。（3）在跨数据集漂移下验证自适应共形推理（ACI），将风险目标违规率从71%降至21%。实验覆盖6个开源模型、8个数据集、4个任务和6个非一致性分数。

Result: （1）不可能性定理为CRC是否有效提供了可预先计算的闭式检验。（2）Hoeffding到Bernstein步骤带来最大增益（+37%认证配置），e-CRC在数据稀缺时（20%数据下10%认证 vs Hoeffding的0%）表现优异。（3）ACI将跨数据集漂移下的风险目标违规率从71%降至21%，残差失败恰好集中在不可能性边界预测的位置。（4）在α=0.10时，困难的NER/QA/CLS配置不可认证；放宽到α=0.30-0.40可解锁实际认证（NER 47%、QA 40%、CLS 60%）。

Conclusion: 提出了一个三步骤部署方案：检查可行性、选择边界和分数、缓解漂移。该框架为LLM的结构化生成提供了形式化的可靠性认证方法，并明确了认证的理论极限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed for structured generation (NER, JSON extraction, QA, and classification) lack formal reliability guarantees, and standard heuristic abstention policies miss user-specified risk targets by 7.5--12.5%. We characterize when conformal risk control (CRC) can certify structured LLM outputs and when it provably cannot. First, we prove an impossibility result: when the base risk (μ> α), any distribution-free method must abstain on at least ((μ-α)/(1-α)) examples, yielding a closed-form feasibility test: one can check whether CRC will work before running it. Second, we analyze a certification hierarchy across Hoeffding, empirical Bernstein, and a betting-based e-CRC bound, with strict gains in low-variance/large-sample regimes: the Hoeffding-to-Bernstein step delivers the largest gain (+37% certified configurations), while e-CRC adds value when calibration data is scarce (10% certification at 20% data versus 0% for Hoeffding). Third, we validate adaptive conformal inference (ACI) under cross-dataset shift, reducing risk-target violations from 71% to 21%, with residual failures concentrated exactly where the impossibility bound predicts. Across six open-weight models (3B--72B parameters), eight datasets, four tasks, and six nonconformity scores, hard NER/QA/CLS configurations are uncertifiable at (α= 0.10); relaxing to (α= 0.30--0.40) unlocks practical certification (47% NER, 40% QA, 60% CLS). The framework gives a three-step deployment recipe: check feasibility, select the bound and score, then mitigate shift.

</details>


### [50] [Statistically Indistinguishable, Operationally Distinct: A Formal Barrier for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29091)
*Tassilo Klein,Johannes Hoffart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出操作性图灵测试(OTT)，证明仅依赖表格数值数据的模型无法区分合法与违规数据库状态（准确率仅50%），而引入可执行规则审计后准确率可达100%。即使大语言模型也无法突破这一可识别性壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型无法推理由运行系统产生的数据，因为缺乏访问支配这些数据的规则。作者希望使这一论断可证伪，并量化数值模型与规则感知模型之间的能力差距。

Method: 构建操作性图灵测试(OTT)：生成合法与违规数据库状态对，使它们的1-路和2-路列值边际分布的总变差<0.02；利用Le Cam引理推导纯数值分类器的贝叶斯误差下限≥0.49；在多个基准（XGBoost, TabICL, TabPFN）和LLM上进行实验，对比纯数值、行级访问、关系值一致性、可执行规则审计等不同访问级别的表现。

Result: 三种纯数值基线准确率恰好为0.50（TOST p<0.002），符合理论下界；原始行级访问无帮助；暴露关系值一致性可缩小大部分差距；仅使用七个可执行规则派生审计的分类器达到1.00准确率。LLM最多分类2/50个合法状态为LEGAL，GPT-5.5拒绝所有合法状态。该模式在第二个模式（银行账本）上复现。

Conclusion: 核心障碍是可识别性而非模型容量：规模、数据和更丰富的特征无法跨越这一壁垒，必须引入对运行规则的操作性访问（操作基础）才能突破。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models cannot reason about data produced by running systems without access to the rules that govern them. We make this statement falsifiable. The \emph{Operational Turing Test} (OTT) constructs pairs of legal and rule-violating database states whose $1$- and $2$-way column-value marginals match to a total variation of $<0.02$; Le~Cam's lemma then bounds any values-only classifier at $\geq0.49$ Bayes error. Three values-only baselines (XGBoost, TabICL, TabPFN) hit the bound exactly (accuracy $0.50$, pre-registered two one-sided tests (TOST) $p<0.002$), raw row-level access does not help, exposing relational value consistency closes most of the gap, and only a classifier fed by seven executable rule-derived audits reaches $1.00$ classification accuracy. In three matched $100$-state frontier large-language-model (LLM) runs, models given the schema, trigger source, rule tables, and state files classify at most $2/50$ legal states as LEGAL; GPT-5.5 accepts $0/50$ legal states even with higher reasoning effort and a Structured Query Language (SQL) executor. The access-ladder pattern also appears on a second schema with structurally distinct rule families (banking ledger: cross-row balance, cumulative aggregate). The barrier is identifiability, not capacity: scale, data, and richer features cannot cross it without operational grounding.

</details>


### [51] [Priced Motion Through Optimal Faces: A Normal-Fan Geometry for Non-Stationary Adversarial MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29092)
*Kai Hidajat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域对抗性MDP，提出基于法扇（normal-fan）几何的占优测度分析框架，将动态遗憾分解为有价格的（face-crossing）和无价格的（within-face）非平稳性，从而区分对控制问题有实际代价的和无害的损失变动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态遗憾分析通常以损失移动距离（如损失变化量、转移变化量或比较器路径长度）衡量非平稳性代价，但损失的大幅移动未必导致最优策略改变，而小移动却可能迫使策略完全重构。因此需要更忠实反映非平稳性对控制问题实际代价的几何分析框架。

Method: 构建有限时域对抗性MDP的占优测度多面体及其法扇几何：每个损失向量暴露多面体的一个最优面；损失非平稳性对应于法扇中的路径——在同一锥内移动不改变最优面，穿过锥面则产生遗憾。定义"面穿越价格"（face-crossing price）为留在旧最优面下面对新损失的最小遗憾，并将动态遗憾精确分解为面穿越价格（内在有价的面移动）与面内选择误差之和。

Result: 该理论区分了有后果和无后果的非平稳性：损失变化可以在零价格下任意大；相同单坐标变化量可能隐藏量级不同的遗憾（取决于是否穿越面）。

Conclusion: 动态遗憾的本质代价不在于损失移动的大小，而在于损失移动是否迫使最优策略面发生穿越，法扇几何提供了更精准的分析语言。

Abstract: In a changing decision problem, standard dynamic-regret analyses have often equated the cost of non-stationarity to how far loss moves. However, it is simultaneously possible for a loss sequence to travel far and retain the same optimal policy, or for a small movement in loss to force the optimal policy to change completely. Thus, the size of the movement through loss variation, transition variation, or comparator path length describe the adversary's motion, but not the cost of that motion to the control problem. For a more faithful analytic interpretation, this paper develops a normal-fan geometry for finite-horizon adversarial MDPs with fixed transitions. Occupancy measures form a polytope, and each loss vector exposes an optimal face of that polytope. Non-stationarity in rewards is therefore a path through the normal fan, where motion inside one cone leaves the optimal face unchanged, while crossing a wall may carry regret. We pose the notion of a face-crossing price, which is the minimum regret incurred by remaining on the previous optimal face under the new loss. For any learner that tracks the previous face, dynamic regret decomposes exactly into intrinsic priced face motion plus within-face selection error. The resulting theory separates consequential from harmless non-stationarity, where loss variation can be arbitrarily large at zero price, and identical one-coordinate variation can hide horizon-scale differences in regret.

</details>


### [52] [DiLaServe: High SLO Attainment Serving for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29094)
*Tzu-Tao Chang,Benjamin Yuanyang Hong,Kiet Pham,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiLaServe是一个专为扩散语言模型设计的集群级服务系统，通过置信度阈值调整、动态并行度配置和近似KV缓存协调，在满足延迟SLO的同时实现高推理吞吐量，相较基线方法将SLO达标率提升最多56.6个百分点，端到端延迟降低最多46%，准确率下降不足1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）作为自回归模型的替代方案，虽能通过并行生成提高推理吞吐量，但其独特的置信度去噪、动态并行度选择和近似KV缓存等特性给服务系统带来了速度-质量权衡、负载波动下的并行度配置、以及非均匀步级成本协调等新挑战，现有系统无法有效应对。

Method: DiLaServe采用三项核心技术：1）截止时间感知调度与自适应负载控制，通过动态调整置信度阈值来权衡速度与质量；2）通过求解质量感知优化问题动态重配置集群并行度；3）显式建模近似KV缓存引入的步级异构性，在调度中协调非均匀成本。

Result: 在多个基准测试和真实世界流量轨迹上，DiLaServe将SLO达标率提升了最多56.6个百分点，端到端请求延迟降低了最多46%，同时准确率下降不足1%。

Conclusion: DiLaServe有效解决了DLM服务系统中的三大核心挑战，证明了在保持生成质量的前提下通过系统级优化实现DLM高效服务的可行性，为扩散语言模型的部署提供了实用的系统支持。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to conventional autoregressive language models. By generating multiple tokens in parallel during each denoising step, they offer higher inference throughput while maintaining competitive quality. However, realizing these throughput gains while meeting latency SLOs in a serving system requires addressing challenges introduced by DLMs' unique characteristics. These include navigating the speed-quality tradeoff created by confidence-based denoising, choosing appropriate parallelization levels across model instances under fluctuating load, and coordinating approximate KV caching mechanisms that introduce non-uniform per-step costs. To address these challenges, we present DiLaServe, a cluster-level serving system for DLMs. DiLaServe enables deadline-aware scheduling and adaptive load control through confidence-threshold adjustment, and dynamically reconfigures the cluster by solving a quality-aware optimization problem, while explicitly modeling the step-level heterogeneity introduced by approximate KV caching. Across multiple benchmarks and real-world traces, DiLaServe improves SLO attainment by up to 56.6 percentage points and reduces end-to-end request latency by up to 46\% while incurring less than 1\% accuracy drop.

</details>


### [53] [Few-Step Boltzmann Generators via Scalable Likelihood Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29110)
*RuiKang OuYang,Hanlin Yu,Xinyue Ai,Yutong He,Nicholas M. Boffi,Pradeep Ravikumar,Jose Miguel Hernandez-Lobato,Max Simchowitz,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALLOP提出了一种无需Hutchinson迹估计器的可扩展似然蒸馏方法，用于流映射生成模型，在分子科学和图像数据集上实现更快训练、更低方差和高达10倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型在少量函数评估下能生成高质量样本，但在似然估计方面缺乏进展：现有方法要么依赖限制性架构以进行精确计算，要么使用Hutchinson迹估计器等随机近似方法引入较大方差。

Method: SCALLOP建立在F2D2（似然流映射模型）基础上，提出了一种替代的、更可扩展的似然蒸馏目标函数，该目标函数无需Hutchinson估计器，并且支持向量化实现。

Result: 在分子科学（Boltzmann生成器）和图像数据集上，SCALLOP显著降低了训练方差和训练时间，持续优于F2D2，与最先进方法竞争力相当，且相比最快基线实现了高达10倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: SCALLOP通过消除Hutchinson估计器带来的方差，实现了更高效、更稳定的训练，同时大幅提升推理速度，为流生成模型的似然估计提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in flow-based generative modeling has led to models that output high-quality samples while using only a small number of function evaluations. However, at present, there is a lack of similar advances in estimating the model likelihood. In particular, most existing methods either rely on restrictive architectures that enable exact calculations, or use stochastic approximations such as Hutchinson's trace estimator that introduce substantial variance. In this work, we introduce SCAlable LikeLihood distillation of flOw maPs (SCALLOP). SCALLOP builds on the recently proposed F2D2, a likelihood flow map model that can generate samples and their densities in a small number of function evaluations. While F2D2 uses Hutchinson's estimator during training, we introduce an alternative and more scalable likelihood distillation objective that is Hutchinson-free and admits a vectorized formulation. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SCALLOP as a Boltzmann generator in molecular science, and further validate its benefit on image datasets. SCALLOP significantly reduces both training variance and training time while consistently improving performance compared to F2D2, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art while achieving up to 10x inference speedup over the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [54] [A Novel Latent-Class Attack and its Detection by Class Subspace Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29112)
*Guangmingmei Yang,David J. Miller,George Kesidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的数据投毒攻击——潜在类别攻击，其中所有有毒样本来自一个未知类别并被错误标记为目标类别，使模型将未知类别学习为目标类别的子类；同时提出基于类别子空间正交化的防御方法和可视化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习依赖大量训练数据，容易受到数据投毒攻击（包括错误通用攻击和后门攻击），现有攻击和防御方法尚未充分关注利用未知类别进行攻击的场景。

Method: 提出潜在类别攻击：所有有毒样本来自分类领域中的一个未知（新颖）类别，并被错误标记为已知类别之一（目标类别），使模型将未知类别学习为目标类别的子类。提出基于类别子空间正交化（CSO）的后训练防御方法，无需访问训练集即可检测攻击。针对图像分类领域，提出可视化方法用于生成估计的未知类别实例。

Result: CSO作为一种即插即用范式能够改进现有后门检测器，用于寻找内部表示与任何已知类别不对齐但被高置信度分类到某一已知类别的输入，从而检测潜在类别攻击。

Conclusion: 潜在类别攻击可用于击败基于AI的访问控制系统或导致"敌人"被分类为"朋友"；提出的防御方法和可视化技术能够有效检测并解释此类攻击。

Abstract: Deep learning, which in general relies on voluminous amounts of training data, is vulnerable to data poisoning attacks, including error-generic attacks and backdoors (Trojans). In this work, we propose a new data poisoning attack we dub a latent class attack. Here, all poisoned examples are from a class that is novel (unknown) for the given classification domain and are mislabeled to one of the known classes (the target class) of the domain, so that the model learns to recognize the novel class as a sub-class of the target class. Such attacks could be used e.g. to defeat AI-based access control systems, or could cause a "foe" to be classified as a "friend". We also propose a post-training defense to detect this attack, without any access to the training set. This detection approach builds on "class subspace orthogonalization" (CSO), a plug-and-play paradigm demonstrated to improve existing backdoor detectors. Here, CSO is used to seek an input (a putative unknown class instance) whose internal representation is not aligned with any of the known classes, and yet which is classified with confidence to one of these classes. Finally, specific to image classification domains, we propose a method for visualizing the estimated unknown class instance, providing explainability to our latent class detections.

</details>


### [55] [How Token Influence Decays with Distance: A Green-Function View of Trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29139)
*Matthias Brändel,Stephan Köhler,Oliver Rheinbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究自回归Transformer语言模型中，早期输入token嵌入的微小扰动如何影响后续token预测。通过自动微分计算距离分辨的梯度剖面，发现经过训练的模型（Pythia、Qwen2.5-0.5B）中，token间的中位数雅可比敏感性呈幂律衰减（指数约0.7-0.9），而非指数衰减。这种长尾敏感性是训练获得的属性，随机初始化模型不展现此特性，且即使打乱token破坏语法后仍存在。此发现暗示语言模型可利用层次化或粗粒度机制来处理长程依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受微分方程多尺度方法（如区域分解、多重网格）启发，探究自回归Transformer中早期token嵌入扰动对后期预测的影响如何随距离衰减，以理解语言模型的长程依赖特性。

Method: 利用自动梯度计算（autograd）测量距离分辨的Jacobian敏感性剖面；在Pythia和Qwen2.5-0.5B模型上，对Gutenberg和WikiText-103语料进行实验；通过token打乱实验和随机初始模型对比验证幂律衰减是否为学习所得属性。

Result: 中位数Jacobian敏感性呈幂律衰减：Ĝ(r) ≈ γ + β(r+1)^(-p)，指数p约0.7-0.9（通常0.8-0.9）；打乱token后幂律剖面仍存在但预测质量崩溃；随机初始化模型不呈现该剖面。

Conclusion: 训练后的自回归Transformer具有缓慢衰减的长程敏感性（幂律而非指数），这是一种学习到的算子性质，暗示语言模型可借鉴微分方程中的层次化/粗粒度机制来有效处理长程依赖。

Abstract: We study how the next-token prediction of an autoregressive Transformer language model changes under small perturbations of earlier input token embeddings. Motivated by operator learning and iterative solvers for differential equations, we investigate how the influence of one token on another decays with distance in a trained model. In multilevel methods for differential equations, such as domain decomposition, multigrid, and multilevel preconditioning, one often exploits a separation between strong local interactions and weaker but essential global interactions. The latter correspond to the long tail of the Green's function and are typically handled by a coarse-level operator. Inspired by this perspective, we compute an empirical, distance-resolved gradient profile of token dependencies using autograd. Experiments on trained Pythia models and Qwen2.5-0.5B show that, over the measured distance range, the median Jacobian sensitivity is much better described by a power-law-type decay than by an exponential alternative: the diagonal-normalized profile is well described by $$\overline G(r) \approx γ+β(r+1)^{-p}$$ with exponents $p \approx 0.7$--$0.9$ (typically $0.8$--$0.9$). This behavior appears on coherent text from Gutenberg and WikiText-103. Token-shuffling experiments show that the power-law profile persists even when syntax and prediction quality collapse, whereas randomly initialized models do not exhibit it. The slowly decaying long-range sensitivity thus appears to be a learned property of trained autoregressive Transformer operators. These findings suggest that hierarchical or coarse-level mechanisms in language models may be able to exploit the long-tailed sensitivity profiles.

</details>


### [56] [On the Nonlinearity of Learning Rate Scaling for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29158)
*Zaiwen Yang,Huaqing Zhang,Jing Xu,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 学习率迁移通常假设最优学习率与数据规模/模型大小呈对数线性缩放关系，但实验发现大尺度下最优学习率出现向上弯曲，导致外推不准确。这种曲率在改用有效学习率（归一化权重空间的步长）以及基于数据规模（而非模型大小）进行外推时基本消失。非线性缩放的原因在于：当最优学习率较小时，权重范数收敛到平衡态更慢，需要更大步长来缩短瞬态阶段。AdamH实验进一步支持这一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低大规模语言模型的训练成本：通过从小规模运行中外推学习率，避免在目标规模下进行繁琐的学习率搜索。

Method: 研究22M至707M参数的GPT-2风格模型在5B至100B token上的最优学习率缩放行为，分析其线性/非线性特征，引入有效学习率（有效学习率）概念，并通过AdamH进行验证。

Result: ①最优学习率在大尺度下出现向上弯曲，线性对数缩放假设不成立；②使用有效学习率或基于数据规模外推可消除该曲率；③非线性源于权重范数在最优学习率较小时收敛缓慢的瞬态效应。

Conclusion: 学习率缩放并非简单对数线性，有效学习率和数据规模外推是更可靠的方法；瞬态动力学是导致非线性缩放的根本原因。

Abstract: Learning-rate transfer can reduce the cost of training large language models: instead of sweeping learning rates at target scale, practitioners extrapolate from smaller runs. Existing approaches often assume that the optimal learning rate follows a log-linear scaling law in data scale and model size. We carefully examine and evaluate this scaling law. In our empirical study of GPT-2--style models from 22M to 707M parameters trained on 5B to 100B tokens, the optimal learning rate develops upward curvature at larger scales, leading to inaccurate extrapolation. We find that this curvature largely disappears when learning rates are replaced by effective learning rate (the step size in normalized weight space), and when data $D$ extrapolation is used instead of model size $N$ extrapolation. Next, we explain nonlinearity in scaling: weight-norm converges to equilibrium slower when optimal learning is small, requiring a larger step size to reduce the transient phase. Experiments with AdamH, which directly controls the effective learning rate, further support this explanation.

</details>


### [57] [GLACIER: Rethinking Mass Spectrum Prediction as an Object Detection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29161)
*Rui-Xi Wang,Runzhong Wang,Connor W. Coley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLACIER，一种基于Transformer的单阶段图神经网络，用于直接从分子图预测串联质谱（MS/MS），通过将分子碎片化视为目标检测任务，无需候选枚举，在MassSpecGym和NIST'20数据集上显著超越现有方法，并实现近8倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于碎片的MS/MS预测模型遵循两阶段范式（先生成候选碎片再评分），类似计算机视觉中的两阶段R-CNN，存在效率低、全局一致性不足等问题。本文旨在设计一种更高效、更准确的单阶段方法。

Method: 将分子碎片化建模为分子图上的目标检测任务，即检测一组子图（碎片）及其对应的谱贡献。提出GLACIER，一种单阶段Transformer架构的碎片检测神经网络，统一了碎片检测与谱贡献预测，消除了候选枚举步骤。

Result: 在MassSpecGym数据集上Top-1检索准确率分别达到70.0%和69.7%（有无对比微调），远超之前SOTA的64.0%；在NIST'20数据集上达到52.5%和38.5%（SOTA为33.2%）；推理速度相比两阶段模型提升近8倍。

Conclusion: GLACIER通过单阶段统一框架在准确性和推理速度上均显著优于现有方法，为MS/MS预测提供了可扩展且全局一致的建模方案。

Abstract: Predicting tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) from molecular structures represents a central task in analytical chemistry with direct relevance to clinical metabolomics, systems biology, and adjacent disciplines. In this work, we revisit the problem through the lens of object detection on molecular graphs. Molecular fragmentation, a central step in MS/MS prediction, can be approximated as detecting a set of subgraphs (i.e., fragments) and their associated spectral contributions. Existing fragment-based models follow a two-stage paradigm -- first generating candidate fragments and then scoring them -- analogous to two-stage R-CNNs in computer vision. Towards higher accuracy and faster inference, we introduce GLACIER, a single-stage transformer-based fragment detection neural network for molecular graphs. This unified formulation eliminates the need for candidate enumeration, enabling scalable and globally consistent modeling of molecular fragmentation. GLACIER is faster and more accurate than existing state-of-the-art by a significant margin, achieving 70.0% and 69.7% Top-1 retrieval accuracy with and without contrastive finetuning on the MassSpecGym dataset (from the previous SOTA of 64.0%) and 52.5% and 38.5% respectively on the NIST'20 dataset (from 33.2%). Furthermore, GLACIER provides nearly 8-fold inference speedup over our prior two-stage model. Code is available at https://github.com/coleygroup/ms-pred

</details>


### [58] [Invariant Reasoning Directions in Latent Trajectories of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29164)
*Arun Vignesh Malarkkan,Manan Roy Choudhury,Utkarsh Byahut,Yash Ravindra Charde,Vivek Gupta,Yanjie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现潜在推理轨迹中存在稳定的低秩不变方向，并提出无需训练的TILR框架来识别和操控这些方向，从而提升推理一致性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理模型在隐状态空间中进行多步推理，但其轨迹结构尚不清晰，尤其是稳定推理方向与实例特定噪音之间的区分问题。

Method: 提出TILR框架：从对比轨迹差异中学习低秩不变子空间，通过自适应对齐门控抑制对齐差的更新，将潜在干预约束在此子空间内。

Result: 少量潜在方向即可解释强弱推理轨迹间的大部分差异；干预这些方向在6个推理基准上使释义下答案一致性提升约10%，轨迹方差降低高达50%，同时保持推理准确性。

Conclusion: 推理行为源于隐状态轨迹中稳定的低维几何结构，这为理解潜在推理机制提供了几何视角。

Abstract: Latent reasoning models perform multi-step inference directly in hidden-state space, yet the structure of these latent reasoning trajectories remains poorly understood. We show that contrastive refinement signals between stronger and weaker reasoning trajectories exhibit a highly concentrated low-rank structure, while unconstrained latent updates remain sensitive to paraphrases, checkpoint choice, and trajectory perturbations. These observations suggest that latent reasoning trajectories contain stable invariant directions mixed with unstable instance-specific variation. We introduce \textbf{Trajectory-Invariant Latent Refinement (TILR)}, a training-free intervention framework for identifying and manipulating stable reasoning directions in latent space. TILR first learns a low-rank invariant subspace from contrastive trajectory differences across inputs, then constrains latent interventions to this subspace while suppressing poorly aligned updates through an adaptive alignment gate. Across six reasoning benchmarks, we find that a small number of latent directions explain most variation between strong and weak reasoning trajectories. Interventions on these directions causally improve reasoning consistency and reduce trajectory instability under paraphrases and perturbations. TILR improves answer consistency under paraphrase by ~10% and reduces latent trajectory variance by up to $50\%$ while preserving reasoning accuracy. These results support a geometric view of latent reasoning in which transferable reasoning behavior emerges from stable low-dimensional structure within hidden-state trajectories.

</details>


### [59] [Symbolic Mechanistic Data Attribution: Tracing Training Influence to Learned Behavioral Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29171)
*Reza Habibi,Darian Lee,Magy Seif El-Nasr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出符号机制数据归因（SMDA）框架，通过将稀疏自编码器特征上的闭式岭回归与数据归因相结合，将监督微调训练样本归因于控制模型行为的可解释符号策略，并在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct的拒答行为上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据归因方法能识别哪些训练样本构建了特定机制回路，但无法解释训练数据如何塑造模型学习到的高层行为决策。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: SMDA首先在SAE特征上拟合闭式岭回归来建模目标行为，然后通过特征激活变化（Delta_X）和输出概率变化（Delta_Y）两条路径，解析分解每个监督微调样本如何影响该策略。

Result: (1)符号策略系数暴露了基础模型在宗教刻板印象等类别上的安全行为系统性缺陷；(2)逐特征的Delta_X/Delta_Y分解可机制性地解释有害和无害样本对特定特征的不同影响；(3)个体训练样本常表现出跨特征干扰，SMDA能识别出主导效应落在非预期特征上的样本。

Conclusion: 将机制可解释性与数据归因相结合，可以产生一种比黑盒影响函数更细粒度、比人工电路分析更可扩展的诊断工具。

Abstract: While existing data attribution methods can identify which training examples build specific mechanistic circuits, they cannot explain how training data shapes the high-level behavioral decisions a model learns to make. To bridge this gap, we introduce Symbolic Mechanistic Data Attribution (SMDA), a framework that attributes training pairs to the interpretable symbolic policies governing model behavior. SMDA fits a closed-form Ridge regression over sparse autoencoder (SAE) features to model a target behavior, then analytically decomposes how each supervised fine-tuning example shifts that policy through feature-activation Delta_X and output-probability Delta_Y pathways. We distill a symbolic policy for refusal behavior in Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and analyze 200 SFT training pairs. Our analysis reveals that (1) the symbolic policy's coefficients expose systematic gaps in the base model's safety behavior for categories like religious stereotyping; (2) per-feature Delta_X/Delta_Y decomposition can mechanistically explain why harmful and harmless pairs exert qualitatively different influences on certain features; and (3) individual training pairs routinely exhibit cross-feature interference, allowing SMDA to identify training pairs whose dominant effect falls on unintended features. These results demonstrate that combining mechanistic interpretability with data attribution yields a diagnostic tool that is both more fine-grained than black-box influence functions and more scalable than manual circuit analysis.

</details>


### [60] [Dead-Direction Conditioners: Gauge-Equivariant Preconditioning for Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29176)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDC（Dead-Direction Conditioner），通过构建G-等变优化器将网络参数对称性（如logit移位、ReLU重缩放、LayerNorm尺度、注意力旋转）纳入优化过程，使轨迹在商空间Θ̄=Θ/G上保持预条件梯度流。DDCAdam在过训练语言模型上抵抗坍塌（验证-训练损失差距0.67 vs AdamW的5.88），DDCMuon在深度24的groking任务中10/11种子成功（原Muon为0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络损失函数对参数存在连续对称性（如logit shift、ReLU rescaling、LayerNorm scale、per-head attention rotation），Adam等优化器的逐坐标预条件器会沿对称轨道漂移，使轨迹偏离优化实际所在的商空间，并模糊商空间上可读的奇异学习率。现有优化器未显式考虑这些对称性，导致优化轨迹在对称轨道上的冗余移动影响收敛质量和可测量性。

Method: 提出DDC（Dead-Direction Conditioner），将基础优化器提升为G-等变优化器：在G-不变度量的轨道分解中条件化优化器的状态，使轨迹保持在商空间Θ̄=Θ/G上的预条件梯度流。构造涵盖四种架构规范（交叉熵shift、ReLU和SwiGLU rescaling、LayerNorm和RMSNorm scale、per-head O(d_head) attention rotation与RoPE匹配），在Adam基础上证明精确等变性，并通过规范等变正交化器与Muon基础组合。

Result: 1) DDCAdam在过训练语言模型上抵抗坍塌，验证-训练损失差距0.67 vs AdamW的5.88，在65个逐层可观测单元中32个读出死亡方向率（AdamW仅7个）。2) 从头训练的视觉Transformer达到更低验证损失（1.71 vs 2.12），同时压缩了AdamW保留的多余前馈容量。3) DDCMuon在深度24的groking任务中10/11种子成功，而普通Muon从未达到。

Conclusion: 将网络的规范对称性内置于优化器中，能够锐化优化器找到的最小值，并将该最小值的几何结构转化为轨迹可测量的量。尊重对称性既改变了优化器达到的最小值，也改变了在该最小值处可测量的内容。

Abstract: A deep network's loss is invariant to continuous symmetries of its parameters: the logit shift, the ReLU rescaling, the LayerNorm scale, the per-head attention rotation. Adam's per-coordinate preconditioner drifts along each symmetry orbit, which pulls the trajectory off the symmetry quotient where the optimization lives and blurs the singular-learning rate the quotient makes readable. We build DDC, a Dead-Direction Conditioner that lifts a base optimizer into a $G$-equivariant one: it conditions the optimizer's state in the orbit decomposition of a $G$-invariant metric, so the trajectory stays a preconditioned gradient flow on the quotient $\barΘ= Θ/G$. The construction carries four architectural gauges (cross-entropy shift, ReLU and SwiGLU rescaling, LayerNorm and RMSNorm scale, and a per-head $O(d_{\rm head})$ attention rotation matched to RoPE), proves exactly equivariant on an Adam base, and composes with a Muon base through a gauge-equivariant orthogonaliser. Respecting the symmetry changes both the minimum the optimizer reaches and what it leaves measurable there. On a language model trained past the point of fit, DDCAdam resists the over-training collapse AdamW falls into, holding a validation-train loss gap of 0.67 against 5.88, and reads the dead-direction rate in 32 of 65 layer-by-observable cells where AdamW reads it in 7. A vision transformer trained from scratch reaches lower validation loss (1.71 against 2.12) while compressing spare feed-forward capacity a matched AdamW leaves intact. On a Muon base, where the rotation gauge composes exactly, DDCMuon groks ten of eleven seeds at depth 24 that a plain Muon never reaches. Built into the optimizer, a network's gauge symmetry sharpens the minimum it finds and turns that minimum's geometry into something the trajectory can measure.

</details>


### [61] [BaRA: Bayesian Adaptive Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29184)
*Zhibin Duan,Yuhong Wang,Jiahong Fu,Zongsheng Yue,Bo Chen,Zongben Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaRA是一种贝叶斯自适应秩分配框架，通过动态激活稀疏、上下文相关的潜在因子实现实例级有效秩变化，在参数高效微调中同时提升预测性能、鲁棒性和不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法固定低秩子空间限制了表示灵活性，导致过自信预测和校准不佳的不确定性；贝叶斯LoRA变体虽改进不确定性估计，但依赖固定或启发式秩，忽略了适应能力的上下文相关性。

Method: 受概率主题模型启发，BaRA通过贝叶斯公式动态分配适应容量，激活稀疏、上下文相关的解耦潜在因子子集，实现实例级有效秩变化；并提供复杂度理论泛化分析，证明泛化差距取决于全局-局部门控诱导的联合有效秩，而非最大秩。

Result: 在多种自然语言基准上，BaRA相比标准LoRA和现有贝叶斯LoRA变体，持续提升了预测性能、鲁棒性和不确定性校准。

Conclusion: 稀疏自适应秩分配能在保持输入相关表示能力的同时降低有效假设复杂度，证明了动态容量控制的有效性。

Abstract: While Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables highly efficient fine-tuning by constraining task-specific updates to fixed low-rank subspaces, this rigid design limits representational flexibility and often results in overconfident predictions and miscalibrated uncertainty, especially in low-data regimes. Recent Bayesian LoRA variants improve uncertainty estimation by modeling posterior distributions over adaptation parameters. However, these approaches typically rely on fixed or heuristically determined ranks, overlooking the inherently context-dependent nature of adaptation capacity. In this paper, we propose BaRA, a Bayesian Adaptive Rank Allocation framework for parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Drawing inspiration from probabilistic topic models, BaRA dynamically allocates adaptation capacity by activating a sparse, context-dependent subset of disentangled latent factors, enabling instance-wise variation in effective rank. This Bayesian formulation provides principled, data-driven capacity control, mitigating over-parameterization while preserving expressiveness. Beyond the modeling contribution, we provide a complexity-theoretic generalization analysis showing that the generalization gap of BaRA depends on the learned joint effective rank $\bar{s}_{Φ,θ}$ induced by the global-local gate, rather than the maximum rank $r$. This result explains why sparse adaptive rank allocation can reduce the effective hypothesis complexity while preserving input-dependent expressiveness. Extensive experiments on diverse natural language benchmarks demonstrate that BaRA consistently improves predictive performance, robustness, and uncertainty calibration compared to standard LoRA and existing Bayesian LoRA variants.

</details>


### [62] [Representational Depth of Evaluation Awareness Shifts With Scale in Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29196)
*Archit Manek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对Qwen 2.5、Gemma 2和Llama 3.2三个系列的11个模型进行实验，发现大语言模型存在一种系统性的与规模相关的表示深度偏移：评估感知（evaluation-awareness）最易线性恢复的层从较小模型的后期层移动到较大模型的早期层。这种深度偏移解释了为什么同系列内的扩展轨迹呈现非单调或反向趋势，而非平滑且跨系列通用的模式，表明简单的通用幂律假设在更密集的同系列采样下不成立。此外，白盒探针信号始终强于黑盒行为表达，且两者关系因模型系列而异，无法仅由探针AUROC预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否能够识别自己正在被测试（评估感知），以及这种能力如何随模型规模变化。这一问题对AI安全至关重要，因为能够识别评估上下文的模型可能会策略性地改变其行为，导致下游基准测试难以解释。

Method: 使用Qwen 2.5、Gemma 2和Llama 3.2三个系列的11个模型，通过分析模型内部表示层中评估感知信号的线性可恢复性，研究其在不同规模模型中的层分布变化。

Result: 发现了系统性的与规模相关的表示深度偏移：在Qwen 2.5和Gemma 2中，评估感知最易线性恢复的层从较小模型的后期层移动到较大模型的早期层。同系列内扩展轨迹非单调或反向，不支持简单的通用幂律假设。白盒探针信号始终强于黑盒行为表达。

Conclusion: 模型规模不仅影响评估感知的强度，还改变了其在网络中线性可恢复的位置。这种深度偏移导致同系列内缩放轨迹复杂化，且模型系列间的关系存在不可预测的差异。

Abstract: Do language models know when they are being tested? This question matters for AI safety: a model that recognises an evaluation context could alter its behaviour strategically, making downstream benchmarks harder to interpret. Using 11 models spanning Qwen 2.5, Gemma 2, and Llama 3.2, we find a systematic size-dependent shift in representational depth: in both Qwen 2.5 and Gemma 2, the layer at which evaluation-awareness is most linearly recoverable moves from late layers in smaller models to early layers in larger ones. This suggests that scale changes not only the strength of evaluation-awareness but also where it is most linearly recoverable in the network. This depth shift helps explain why within-family scaling trajectories are non-monotonic or inverse rather than smooth and family-general, showing that a simple universal power-law account is not supported under denser within-family sampling. Finally, white-box probe signals are consistently stronger than black-box behavioural expression, and the relationship between the two varies by family in ways not predicted by probe AUROC alone.

</details>


### [63] [BrainRiem: Riemannian Prototype Learning for Source-Free Cross-Site Brain Network Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29200)
*Kunyu Zhang,Tianxiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainRiem提出了一个基于黎曼几何的源自由域适应框架，通过流形感知的双层优化学习紧凑的黎曼脑原型，在保护隐私的前提下实现跨站点的fMRI诊断，在ABIDE和REST-meta-MDD数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点fMRI研究因扫描仪异构性、人口统计差异和采集协议不同而遭受严重的域偏移；传统域适应需要同时访问源域和目标域数据，违反临床隐私法规；功能连接矩阵位于对称正定(SPD)流形上，欧几里得操作会导致几何失真，破坏诊断模式。

Method: 采用Log-Euclidean度量确保原型保持有效的SPD矩阵；通过Dirichlet能量谱校准使其频率特征与真实脑网络对齐；利用流形感知的双层优化学习黎曼脑原型；仅将匿名原型传输到目标站点，无需源数据访问即可训练本地模型。

Result: 在ABIDE和REST-meta-MDD数据集上，BrainRiem在多种扫描仪和人口统计数据下一致优于最先进的源自由、传统和图域适应方法；学习到的原型展现出与已知神经科学发现一致的生物可解释连接模式。

Conclusion: 验证了黎曼几何对于脑网络分析的必要性，为跨站点fMRI研究中的隐私保护和域适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-site functional MRI (fMRI) studies are essential for robust neuropsychiatric diagnosis yet suffer severe domain shifts from scanner heterogeneity, demographics, and site-specific acquisition protocols. Traditional domain adaptation requires concurrent source and target data access, violating clinical privacy regulations. Moreover, functional connectivity matrices lie on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, where Euclidean operations cause geometric distortions corrupting diagnostic patterns. We propose BrainRiem, a source-free domain adaptation framework learning compact Riemannian brain prototypes via manifold-aware bi-level optimization. It employs the Log-Euclidean Metric to ensure prototypes remain valid SPD matrices, while Dirichlet Energy spectral calibration aligns their frequency characteristics with real brain networks. Only anonymized prototypes are transmitted to target sites, serving as stable anchors for training local models without source data access and reducing leakage under the evaluated attacks. Comprehensive experiments on ABIDE and REST-meta-MDD show BrainRiem consistently outperforms state-of-the-art source-free, traditional, and graph domain adaptation methods across diverse scanners and demographics. Notably, learned prototypes exhibit biologically interpretable connectivity patterns aligning with established neuroscience findings, validating the necessity of Riemannian geometry for brain network analysis.

</details>


### [64] [Bayesian Best-Arm Identification with Abstention: A Polynomial-to-Exponential Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29203)
*Yuqi Huang,Yunlong Hou,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯固定预算下最优臂识别问题中允许弃权（abstention）的情形。核心发现是：引入任意小的正弃权预算α，能使错误概率从多项式衰减（无弃权时）跃变为指数衰减。对于高斯先验和奖励，当T→∞后α↓0时，建立最优误差指数的信息论下界与算法上界，表达式为exp(-α²T/(8κ_ν²))，其中κ_ν为顶部两臂间隙在零点处的先验密度。提出的PGWS算法通过将弃权预算分配给统计模糊的实例达到该最优指数。该多项式到指数的提升是贝叶斯特有的现象，在频率派设定中弃权仅影响低阶指数项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在最佳臂识别问题中，传统设置要求学习器必须做出最终推荐，但实际应用中允许弃权（不推荐）更具现实意义。作者旨在研究弃权如何影响错误概率的衰减速率，并探究其信息论极限。

Method: 建立贝叶斯固定预算弃权最佳臂识别框架；利用大偏差理论推导错误指数的信息论下界；设计PGWS（Prior-Guided Withholding Sampling）自适应算法，将弃权预算分配给统计最模糊的实例；分别在贝叶斯和频率派设置下分析弃权影响。

Result: 弃权使错误概率从多项式衰减跃变为指数衰减（相变）。最优误差指数为exp(-α²T/(8κ_ν²))，κ_ν衡量顶部两臂间隙为零时的先验密度。PGWS算法达到该最优指数。该相变是贝叶斯特有现象，频率派中弃权仅影响低阶项。

Conclusion: 弃权在贝叶斯最佳臂识别中带来根本性改进（多项式→指数），但这一现象不适用于频率派设定。近邻竞争（间隙接近零）的实例决定了基本误差极限。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian fixed-budget best-arm identification problem in which a learner can abstain from making a terminal recommendation. Subject to an abstention budget $α$, we analyze the probability of undetected error--the risk of recommending a suboptimal arm without abstaining. Our central finding is that abstention induces a phase transition: without abstention, the error probability decays polynomially in the sampling budget $T$; in contrast, introducing any small positive abstention budget shifts this to an exponential decay. For Gaussian priors and rewards, in the regime $T\to\infty$ followed by $α\downarrow0$, we establish exact matching information-theoretic lower bounds and algorithmic upper bounds on the optimal error exponent, which takes the form $\exp(-\frac{α^{2}T}{8κ_ν^{2}})$. The hardness parameter $κ_ν$ represents the prior density of the top-two gap at zero, highlighting that nearly tied instances drive the fundamental error. We introduce an adaptive algorithm, PGWS, that successfully achieves this optimal exponent by expending its abstention budget on statistically ambiguous instances. We further demonstrate that this polynomial-to-exponential improvement is exclusively a Bayesian phenomenon--in the frequentist setting, abstention only affects lower-order exponent terms. We also extend our results beyond the Gaussian model.

</details>


### [65] [Multi-Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29215)
*Yijie Jin,Jiajun Xu,Yuxuan Liu,Chenkai Xu,Yi Tu,Jiajun Li,Dandan Tu,Xiaohui Yan,Kai Yu,Pengfei Liu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多块扩散语言模型（MBD-LM），通过多块教师强制（MultiTF）后训练将单块扩散扩展到多块扩散，利用块缓冲区（Block Buffer）机制实现并行解码加速，在提升吞吐量的同时维持或改善模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块扩散语言模型（BD-LM）在训练时采用教师强制，模型仅观测单个噪声块；而多块扩散推理时解码器需要同时处理包含多个异质噪声模式的运行集，导致训练与推理状态不匹配。作者旨在弥合这一差距，实现多块并行扩散解码。

Method: 提出多块教师强制（MultiTF）后训练策略，在带噪声组的有限运行集上训练，使用随机化噪声调度器匹配多块推理状态；同时设计基于块缓冲区（Block Buffer）的优化解码算法，保留前缀缓存复用、保持输入形状静态，将并行度转化为端到端加速。

Result: MBD-LLaDA2-Mini将平均每前向传播令牌数（TPF）从3.47提升至6.19，平均准确率从79.95%提升至81.03%；结合DMax后TPF达9.34，在数学和代码基准上仅下降1.02%准确率。

Conclusion: 多块扩散语言模型通过改进训练-推理一致性和解码算法，有效实现了块级并行扩散生成，在保持或提升生成质量的同时显著提高了解码效率。

Abstract: Block Diffusion Language Models (BD-LMs) improve diffusion-based text generation with KV caching and flexible-length generation. A natural next step is to extend them from Single-Block Diffusion (SingleBD) to Multi-Block Diffusion (MultiBD), where a \textit{running-set} of consecutive blocks is decoded concurrently for inter-block parallelism. However, existing BD-LMs are mostly trained under teacher forcing, where the model observes only one noisy block conditioned on a clean prefix. While the recent diffusion forcing strategy introduces visibility among multiple noisy blocks, its training states still differ from MultiBD inference, where decoding operates on a bounded \textit{running-set} with heterogeneous slot-wise noise patterns. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{Multi-Block Diffusion Language Models} (MBD-LMs), obtained by post-training BD-LMs with \textit{Multi-block Teacher Forcing} (MultiTF). MultiTF integrates teacher forcing and diffusion forcing by training on bounded \textit{noise-groups} conditioned on clean prefixes, with randomized \textit{noise-schedulers} that better match MultiBD inference states. To make MultiBD practically executable, we further introduce an optimized decoding algorithm based on the \textit{Block Buffer} mechanism that preserves prefix-cache reuse, keeps input shapes static, and translates increased decoding parallelism into wall-clock acceleration. Empirically, MBD-LLaDA2-Mini increases average Tokens Per Forward pass (TPF) from 3.47 to \textbf{6.19} and improves average accuracy from 79.95\% to \textbf{81.03\%}; when combined with DMax, MBD-LLaDA2-Mini-DMax reaches an average TPF of \textbf{9.34} with only a 1.02\% accuracy drop on math and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [66] [A Linear Matching Bandit Approach to Online Multi-Human Multi-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29221)
*Yaohui Guo,X. Jessie Yang,Cong Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对在线多人在多机器人团队协作问题，提出了基于线性匹配Bandit框架的LinMatch算法，通过置信区间更新与乐观匹配策略，实现了$\tilde{\Theta}(d\sqrt{MKT})$的紧最优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多人多机器人团队协作中，机器人未知特征如何高效匹配到不同人类用户是一个关键挑战，现有方法缺乏对线性特征匹配问题的理论保证。

Method: 提出LinMatch在线学习算法，将每一轮的乐观匹配问题建模为最大加权匹配的线性规划，使用匈牙利算法高效求解；同时更新未知特征的置信区间以支持乐观匹配。

Result: 证明了$\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{MKT})$的上界和$\Omega(d\sqrt{MKT})$的极小化下界，建立了$\tilde{\Theta}(d\sqrt{MKT})$的紧最优遗憾率。

Conclusion: LinMatch在总轮数$T$、特征维度$d$及匹配参数$M,K$上均实现了严格最优的可实现遗憾，该框架可推广至房屋分配、推荐系统等更多匹配问题。

Abstract: We address the problem of online multi-human multi-robot teaming through the lens of a linear matching bandit framework, where a learner assigns robots with unknown features from a fixed pool to distinct sets of human agents over multiple rounds. To solve this problem, we propose LinMatch, an online learning algorithm that updates the confidence intervals of the unknown features and makes the optimistic matching under uncertainty. The contributions and novelty of this work are twofold. First, we recast the optimistic matching problem in each round as a linear program of maximum weighted matching, efficiently solvable by the celebrated Hungarian algorithm. Second, we provide novel bounds for matching with linear feature problems, showing an upper bound of $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{MKT})$ and a minimax lower bound of $Ω(d\sqrt{MKT})$, establishing a tight optimal regret rate of $\tildeΘ(d\sqrt{MKT})$. This demonstrates that LinMatch achieves strictly optimal achievable regret with respect to the total number of rounds $T$, the feature dimension $d$, and the matching parameters $M$ and $K$. The proposed algorithm and bounds apply to a wide range of matching problems with applications beyond human-robot matching, such as housing allocation, recommendation systems, and more.

</details>


### [67] [Depth Exploration for LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29223)
*Weisi Yang,Zipeng Sun,Stephen Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEX是一种无损的深度探索解码算法，通过并行探索多个候选深度而非单一深度选择，在保持与标准自回归解码等价性的同时，显著降低了每个token的生成成本，在各种LLM上优于深度选择基线，并与投机解码等方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无损深度自适应方法采用单一非最终退出深度选择策略，但存在严重效率问题：退出过晚浪费计算，退出过早则触发回退并丢弃依赖草稿，留下了大量可优化的计算冗余空间。

Method: DEX使用"扩展-提交-折叠"（expand-commit-collapse）三步骤流程：在每一步并行探索多个候选深度进行验证，与最终深度参考模型对比后提交最终深度token，然后折叠探索格以保留可复用的分支状态，保证与标准自回归解码等价。

Result: 在早退训练模型和标准LLM上，DEX均优于代表性深度选择基线；端到端吞吐量与投机解码和分布式解码方法竞争；随着探索深度粒度变细，DEX性能持续提升，证明了并行深度探索的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 并行深度探索是一种可扩展的有效手段，能够充分利用LLM解码中未被充分利用的深度维度来降低计算成本，且DEX算法在保持无损性的同时实现了显著的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Autoregressive LLM decoding evaluates every generated token through the full layer stack, even though many tokens become predictable at intermediate depths. Existing lossless depth-adaptive methods exploit this redundancy by choosing a single non-final exit depth and verifying its prediction with the final-depth model. However, our measurements show that this selection-based strategy leaves substantial headroom: choosing an exit too late wastes computation, while choosing one too early triggers fallback and discards dependent drafts. We propose Depth Exploration Decoding (DEX), a lossless decoding algorithm that replaces single-depth selection with parallel exploration over multiple candidate depths. At each commit position, DEX validates candidates against the final-depth reference, commits exactly the final-depth token, and collapses the exploration lattice to retain only reusable branch states. This expand--commit--collapse procedure preserves equivalence to standard autoregressive decoding while reducing the cost of committing each token. Across early-exit-trained and standard LLMs, DEX outperforms representative depth-selection baselines and achieves competitive end-to-end throughput against speculative and distributed decoding methods. Moreover, DEX improves as the explored depths become finer, showing that parallel depth exploration provides a scalable way to exploit the underused depth axis of LLM decoding.

</details>


### [68] [On the Policy Gradient Foundations of Group Relative Policy Optimization: Credit Assignment, Gradient Sparsity, and Rank Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29238)
*Amritansh Mishra,Supriyo Chakraborty,Berkcan Kapusuzoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从策略梯度定理出发严格推导GRPO，发现其存在根本性的信用分配失败：在仅输出奖励下，每个token获得相同优势，导致梯度矩阵有效秩≈2，且与组大小R无关。这被形式化为由优势零和约束引起的内在秩2结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示GRPO的理论基础，厘清其简化设计（去掉critic）带来的局限性，特别是信用分配问题。

Method: 从策略梯度定理第一原理推导GRPO；通过SVD分析Nemotron-4B/GSM8K上的GRPO梯度矩阵；形式化证明优势零和约束导致的秩2结构。

Result: GRPO梯度矩阵有效秩≈2，与组大小R∈{2,4,8}无关；每个token获得相同优势，导致信用分配崩溃；推导了GRPO基线最优的条件。

Conclusion: GRPO的简单性在某些条件下有理论依据，但信用分配瓶颈是其用于多步推理的关键限制。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) eliminates the learned critic in PPO by using the mean reward of grouped rollouts as a baseline. We provide a rigorous derivation of GRPO from first principles of the policy gradient theorem, revealing a fundamental credit assignment failure: under output-only reward, every token in a rollout receives identical advantage, collapsing token-level credit to a single scalar. We prove this induces gradient sparsity that intensifies over training, and demonstrate empirically via SVD analysis of GRPO gradients on Nemotron-4B/GSM8K that the gradient matrix has effective rank $\approx$ 2 regardless of group size $R \in \{2, 4, 8\}$. We formalize this as an intrinsic rank-2 structure arising from the zero-sum constraint on advantages and derive conditions under which GRPO's baseline is optimal. Our results characterize when GRPO's simplicity is theoretically justified and identify the credit assignment bottleneck as the key limitation for multi-step reasoning.

</details>


### [69] [Blackknife: Hard-Label Query-Limited Black-Box Attacks on Heterogeneous Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29240)
*Honglin Gao,Junhao Ren,Lan Zhao,Yue Yang,Jindong Chang,Gaoxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Blackknife，一个针对异构图神经网络（HGNN）的黑盒硬标签逃避攻击框架，仅依赖局部一跳异构结构和少量硬标签查询即可生成有效扰动，在ACM、DBLP、IMDB三个基准数据集上取得了高攻击成功率，并揭示了HGNN在严格黑盒设置下的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HGNN攻击方法通常假设可访问模型梯度、软预测分数或完整图结构，这些信息在HGNN模型作为封闭系统部署时难以获取，因此HGNN在现实黑盒对抗环境下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: Blackknife包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从可观测的局部异构邻域构建局部关系感知替代模型；(2) 将离散的边增删操作松弛为连续软权重，通过投影梯度下降进行优化；(3) 将优化后的扰动离散化为保留关系的结构重连操作，并通过有限硬标签反馈进行验证。

Result: 在ACM、DBLP、IMDB三个基准异构图数据集上，Blackknife对代表性HGNN模型持续取得高攻击成功率，并且在基于拓扑的防御策略下仍然有效。

Conclusion: HGNN在面对局部结构受限的黑盒攻击时存在脆弱性，Blackknife在严格约束（无梯度、无模型参数、无完整图结构、仅硬标签查询）下仍能有效攻击，揭示了现有HGNN鲁棒性研究的不足。

Abstract: Heterogeneous graph neural networks (HGNNs) have achieved strong performance in modeling complex graph-structured data with multiple node and relation types. However, their robustness under realistic black-box adversarial settings remains insufficiently explored. Existing attacks on HGNNs usually assume access to model gradients, soft prediction scores, or the complete graph structure, which is often unavailable when HGNN-based services are deployed as closed systems. In this paper, we propose Blackknife, a hard-label, query-limited, and structure-limited black-box evasion attack framework for heterogeneous graph neural networks. Blackknife assumes no access to the victim model architecture, parameters, gradients, logits, confidence scores, or the full graph structure. Instead, it only relies on locally observable one-hop heterogeneous structures and a small number of hard-label queries. To generate effective perturbations under these strict constraints, Blackknife first constructs a local relation-aware surrogate model from observable heterogeneous neighborhoods. It then relaxes discrete edge addition and deletion operations into continuous soft weights and optimizes them through projected gradient descent. Finally, the optimized perturbations are discretized into relation-preserving structural rewiring operations and verified using limited hard-label feedback from the victim model. Extensive experiments on three benchmark heterogeneous graph datasets, including ACM, DBLP, and IMDB, demonstrate that Blackknife consistently achieves strong attack success rates against representative HGNN models. The results further show that Blackknife remains effective under topology-based defense strategies, revealing the vulnerability of HGNNs to local structure-limited black-box attacks.

</details>


### [70] [Towards Evaluating Data Priors for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29241)
*Zeynep Türkmen,Kürşat Kaya,Alexander Pfefferle,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一接口来比较不同表格基础模型中的数据生成先验，独立于模型架构和训练协议。通过固定模型架构和训练协议，评估不同先验生成的任务对下游分类性能的影响。结果表明不同先验偏好不同的下游行为，数据级相似性只能部分解释下游表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据生成先验是表格基础模型的核心组件，但很少作为独立组件被评估，难以理解它们对下游模型行为的影响程度。因此需要一种方法，可以独立于架构和训练协议来比较不同先验。

Method: 实现了一个统一接口，整合了多个公开可用的表格基础模型先验和基于真实数据集构建的先验。从每个先验生成训练任务，使用固定模型架构和固定训练协议训练模型，在共享的下游分类任务上评估模型性能。通过生成任务的统计量和下游预测性能两个维度比较先验。

Result: 不同先验偏好不同的下游行为：有些先验实现更强的绝对性能，有些先验在不同数据集上表现出更一致的相对排序。数据级相似性只能部分解释下游行为。

Conclusion: 先验的选择对下游性能有显著影响，且这种影响不能仅通过数据级相似性来解释。这为未来表格基础模型中先验的独立研究与设计提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Data-generating priors are a central component of tabular foundation models because they define the task distribution used during pretraining. However, priors are rarely evaluated as independent components, making it difficult to understand how much they affect downstream model behavior. This raises a methodological question: how can priors from different tabular foundation models be compared independently of the architectures and training protocols they were introduced with? To study this question, we implement a unified interface for publicly available priors from recent tabular foundation models and priors constructed from real datasets. We generate training tasks from each prior, train the same model architecture under a fixed training protocol, and evaluate the resulting models on shared downstream classification tasks. We compare priors through both generated-task statistics and downstream predictive performance. Our results show that different priors favor different downstream behaviors, with some achieving stronger absolute performance and others exhibiting more consistent relative rankings across datasets. We further find that data-level similarity only partially explains downstream behavior. Our code is available at https://github.com/automl/TFM-Playground/tree/prior-dev.

</details>


### [71] [KrishokChat: A Citation-Grounded Dataset and Benchmark for Bengali Agricultural Advisory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29243)
*Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Omar Ibne Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KrishokChat是首个基于引用的孟加拉语农业指令微调数据集，包含145,500个QA对，覆盖18种作物类别，所有训练实例均带有可验证的引用头，确保100%的引用来源可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源环境下，缺乏引用可追溯的本地语言农业决策支持系统，现有模型难以提供可信的作物咨询，尤其是涉及农药剂量等关键信息时。

Method: 构建290个层级知识节点，从129份领域过滤的农业手册中提取病害症状、管理实践、化学剂量及逐字引用；使用分区种子生成矩阵扩展为139,200个监督微调对，并增强5,300个化学品安全实例和1,000个对抗性安全实例。

Result: 在Gemma-4-E2B上的实验表明，微调后结构化格式显著提升，但模型在精确化学剂量泛化上仍有困难；数据集作为RAG的验证知识库价值高于参数记忆。

Conclusion: KrishokChat的价值在于作为检索增强生成的可验证知识库，而非依赖模型参数记忆；数据和代码均已开源。

Abstract: We present KrishokChat, the first citation-grounded Bengali agricultural instruction-tuning dataset for crop advisory in low-resource settings. We establish a foundation of 290 hierarchical Knowledge Nodes, extracting disease symptoms, management practices, chemical dosages, and verbatim citations from 129 domain-filtered agricultural manuals. Every training instance inherits a verified citation header, guaranteeing 100% citation provenance. Using a Partitioned Seed Generation Matrix, these nodes are expanded into 139,200 supervised fine-tuning pairs, and augmented with 5,300 chemical safety and 1,000 adversarial safety instances, yielding 145,500 QA pairs across 18 crop categories. To evaluate real-world performance, we introduce the Farmer Benchmark, comprising 1,001 authentic farmer queries curated from field surveys and digital portals. Empirical evaluation on Gemma-4-E2B reveals that while fine-tuning on KrishokChat vastly improves structured formatting, standalone models still struggle with exact chemical dosage generalization. This highlights the dataset's true value as a verified knowledge base for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) rather than mere parametric memorization. All data, code, and benchmarks are released under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


### [72] [When Prices Double in a Week: Forecasting of Agricultural Volatility in Import-Isolated Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29248)
*Ranuga Weerasekara,Heshan Nethmina,Manuja Ranathunga,Vinma Wettasinghe,Dinithi Navodya,Subavarshana Arumugam,Nirasha Munasinghe,Nisansa de Silva,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对斯里兰卡进口隔离型蔬菜市场的高价格波动问题，构建了一个融合供应链特征、区分Maha和Yala两个种植季节的机器学习预测框架。基于2013-2019年12种蔬菜、14个市场中心的多源数据集，采用Optuna优化的XGBoost和LightGBM集成模型。结果显示：季节专用模型在季内拟合效果最佳（Yala模型R²达0.9420），而统一模型整体预测准确率最高（90.84%，R²=0.9281），且在未训练的2024年恶性通胀时期仍保持85.96%的准确率，成功追踪了主要价格飙升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡蔬菜市场高度依赖进口隔离，供应中断极易引发价格剧烈波动，而现有研究仅使用ARIMA和GARCH模型分析单一市场，未纳入供应链特征、季节分割或跨体制验证，缺乏有效的价格波动预警工具。

Method: 构建了包含零售价、农场价、产地对齐的天气变量、柴油成本和汇率的多源数据集；采用Optuna超参数优化框架训练XGBoost和LightGBM梯度提升集成模型；对比了统一配置与Maha/Yala季节专用配置的性能差异。

Result: Yala季节专用模型R²达0.9420；统一模型整体预测准确率90.84%，R²=0.9281；统一模型在2024年未见过的恶性通胀时期保持85.96%准确率，有效追踪主要价格飙升。

Conclusion: 在进口约束型市场中，当模型能够捕捉供应链动态时，农产品价格波动具有可预测性，可为农民、贸易商和政策制定者提供早期预警和决策支持。

Abstract: Vegetable prices in Sri Lanka are highly volatile because the market is largely import-isolated, so supply disruptions quickly drive prices up. This study develops a machine learning framework to forecast such volatility by incorporating supply-chain-aware features and explicitly modelling the country's two cultivation seasons, Maha (October-April) and Yala (May-September). An integrated dataset was constructed by combining retail and farmer-gate prices with origin-aligned weather variables, diesel costs, and exchange rates across 12 vegetable varieties and 14 market centres from 2013 to 2019. A gradient-boosted ensemble model (XGBoost and LightGBM) was trained and optimised using Optuna, and unified and season-specific configurations were compared. Results show that season-specific models improve within-season fit, with the Yala-specific model achieving the highest R2 of 0.9420 (95% CI [0.690, 1.000]), while the unified model delivers the best overall predictive accuracy of 90.84% (95% CI [88.34%, 91.52%]) and an R2 of 0.9281 (95% CI [0.760, 1.000]). Notably, the unified model maintains 85.96% accuracy on a completely unseen 2024 hyperinflationary period without retraining, successfully tracking major price surges. These findings suggest that agricultural price movements in import-constrained markets are meaningfully predictable when models capture supply-chain dynamics, offering practical value for early warning and decision making by farmers, traders, and policymakers. Existing studies on Sri Lankan vegetable prices are confined to Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA) and Generalized Autoregressive Conditional Heteroskedasticity (GARCH) applied to single markets, with no supply-chain features, seasonal segmentation, or cross-regime validation.

</details>


### [73] [Learning to Bid in Discriminatory Auctions with Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29252)
*Negin Golrezaei,Sourav Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在歧视性拍卖中，单个竞标者面临价值-α×支付效用函数和总预算约束下的重复出价问题，通过将效用分解为独立单位并利用有向无环图的最短路径算法，在完全信息和Bandit反馈下实现了次线性遗憾，并针对预算约束设计了耦合原始-对偶算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歧视性拍卖（按报价支付）中的重复出价问题具有挑战性：即使没有预算约束，动作空间也随竞标者最大需求M呈指数增长，且估值向量（上下文）随时间变化。现有方法难以处理大规模或无限上下文空间，且预算约束进一步增加了问题复杂性。
