Method: 1. 将跨单位的效用分解为独立子问题，利用有向无环图（DAG）最短路径算法设计多项式时间学习算法；2. 在完全信息和Bandit反馈下均实现次线性遗憾，其中Bandit设置利用完全交叉学习（观察到选定动作在实现上下文下的效用即可揭示该动作在所有反事实上下文下的效用）；3. 针对预算约束（ρ=B/(MT)<1），提出耦合原始-对偶算法：DAG过程使用对偶调整边权重进行原始更新，在线梯度下降更新对偶变量；4. 实现每轮时间和空间复杂度与上下文数量无关。

Result: 在完全信息和Bandit反馈下均获得次线性遗憾；Bandit设置下遗憾与上下文数量无关；预算约束下达到ρ-近似次线性遗憾；算法每轮时间与空间复杂度不依赖于上下文数量，可扩展至大规模甚至无限上下文空间。

Conclusion: 通过效用分解和DAG最短路径方法有效解决了歧视性拍卖中动作空间指数级的问题，完全交叉学习消除了Bandit反馈下对上下文数量的依赖，耦合原始-对偶框架为预算约束问题提供了近似最优解，且算法具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: We study repeated bidding in multi-unit discriminatory (pay-as-bid) auctions for a single bidder with per-round utility equal to value minus $α$ times payment, where $α\in[0,1]$ is a cost-of-capital parameter. The bidder aims to maximize cumulative utility over $T$ rounds subject to a total budget $B$. The problem is challenging even without budgets: the action space is exponential in $M$, the maximum demand of the bidder and the valuation vector (context) varies over time. Exploiting a decomposition of utility across units, we develop polynomial-time learning algorithms based on shortest paths in a directed acyclic graph, obtaining sublinear regret under both full-information and bandit feedback. In the bandit setting, the regret is independent of the number of contexts due to complete cross-learning: observing the utility of the chosen action under the realized context reveals the utility for the same action under all counterfactual contexts. With budget constraints, when the average normalized per-round budget $ρ=\frac{B}{MT}<1$, we design a coupled primal-dual algorithm in which the DAG-based procedure uses dual-adjusted edge weights for primal updates, while online gradient descent updates the dual variable, yielding $ρ$-approximate sublinear regret. Finally, we give implementations whose per-round time and space are independent of the number of contexts, enabling scalability to large or even infinite context spaces.

</details>


### [74] [Nonlinear mixture model motivated subspace clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29261)
*Ivica Kopriva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将非线性混合模型（NMM）的K阶泰勒展开与线性子空间并集（UoS）模型等价，建立了盲源分离与子空间聚类之间的理论联系，揭示了光滑阶K对应子空间维度d等关键关系，并在六个基准数据集上验证了这些理论结果对子空间维度估计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立盲源分离中非线性混合模型与子空间聚类中线性子空间并集模型之间的理论联系，为子空间维度的估计提供理论基础。

Method: 对非线性混合模型进行K阶泰勒展开，将其近似为线性子空间并集模型，通过数学推导建立两种模型参数之间的对应关系。

Result: 发现光滑阶K对应子空间维度d，KC等于锚点数，表示向量的稀疏度等于K（即d），并在六个基准数据集上使用五种子空间聚类算法验证了这些关系对子空间维度边界估计的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立的理论结果对于子空间聚类算法估计的自表示矩阵的后处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: We derive the linear union-of-subspaces (UoS) model for subspace clustering (SC) from the nonlinear mixture model (NMM) used in blind source separation (BSS) to represent a D-dimensional observation vector as an unknown multivariate nonlinear mapping of C latent variables. Assuming the mapping is differentiable up to an unknown order K, we approximate NMM by a K-th order Taylor expansion, yielding a model equivalent to the linear UoS framework underlying SC. This establishes that: (i) the smoothness order K corresponds to the unknown subspace dimension d; (ii) KC equals the number of anchors; and (iii) the sparsity of the representation vector equals K (i.e., d). These relationships enable estimation of bounds on subspace dimension, and that is validated on six benchmark datasets using five established SC algorithms. Established theoretical results are important for post-processing of self-representation matrices estimated by SC algorithms.

</details>


### [75] [PCGD: Physics-Guided Conditional Graph Diffusion for TCAD Device Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29272)
*Yihan Zhang,Zhiteng Zhang,Kun Chen,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCGD是一种物理引导的条件图扩散框架，在非结构化TCAD网格上预测耦合的静电势和载流子密度场，通过混合目标函数在扩散轨迹中逐步施加物理约束，实现了亚百分比的场预测误差（0.835%），并将最大PDE残差降低了近三个数量级，同时通过LoRA自适应高效迁移到未见拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TCAD半导体器件模拟因迭代求解漂移-扩散方程而计算成本极高。现有ML代理要么将内部物理降维为宏观标量回归，要么依赖缺乏迭代精炼的单步映射，无法解决刚性的耦合场问题。

Method: PCGD采用条件感知的MeshGraphNet去噪器，通过全局交叉注意力注入边界条件和器件结构上下文。结合数据驱动去噪与物理引导的混合目标函数，集成无指数准费米梯度匹配和噪声感知的PDE残差，在迭代扩散轨迹中逐步强制执行物理约束。

Result: 在混合PN/MOS基准上，PCGD达到0.835%的平均相对场误差，优于确定性单步回归（1.207%）和局部扩散（1.585%）基线。最大PDE残差降低近三个数量级。在未见SOI拓扑上通过LoRA自适应达到0.815%误差，仅需全微调5.30倍的数据和14.34倍的参数。

Conclusion: PCGD弥合了生成式代理的计算效率与传统TCAD的严格物理保真度之间的差距，实现了高度可扩展、面向场级分析的鲁棒器件工程。

Abstract: Technology computer-aided design (TCAD) semiconductor device simulation is fundamentally constrained by the high computational cost of iteratively solving coupled drift-diffusion equations. Existing ML surrogates either reduce internal physics to macroscopic scalar regressions, or rely on single-step mappings that lack the iterative refinement required to resolve stiff, coupled fields. To address this, we introduce PCGD, a Physics-Guided Conditional Graph Diffusion framework operating natively on unstructured TCAD meshes to predict coupled electrostatic and carrier density fields. PCGD employs a Condition-Aware MeshGraphNet denoiser that explicitly injects boundary conditions and device structure context via global cross-attention. By augmenting data-driven denoising with a physics-guided hybrid objective that integrates exponent-free quasi-Fermi gradient matching with noise-aware PDE residuals, PCGD progressively enforce physical constraints in the iterative diffusion trajectory. This strategy successfully bypasses the numerical instabilities typical of stiff drift-diffusion equations. Evaluated on a challenging mixed PN/MOS benchmark, PCGD significantly outperforms deterministic one-step regression (1.207% error) and local diffusion (1.585% error) baselines by achieving a sub-percent mean relative field error of 0.835%, while concurrently reducing maximum PDE residual errors by nearly three orders of magnitude compared to pure diffusion. It also transfers robustly to unseen SOI topologies (0.815% error) via LoRA adaptation, using 5.30$\times$ less data and 14.34$\times$ fewer parameters than full fine-tuning. Ultimately, PCGD bridges the computational efficiency of generative surrogates with the rigorous physical fidelity of traditional TCAD, unlocking highly scalable, field-level analysis for robust device engineering.

</details>


### [76] [Adaptive Block Diffusion: Resolving Training-Inference Mismatch in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29275)
*Gagan Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型在固定上下文结构下训练与任意配置推理不匹配的问题，提出自适应块扩散（ABD），通过优化前缀窗口配置分布上的去噪风险，实现训练与推理的结构对齐，避免离格退化，并在多种解码策略下保持结构不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通常在固定上下文结构下训练，限制去噪过程到预定的token子集，导致训练与推理之间存在不匹配——推理时需要处理任意配置，从而引发训练网格外的性能退化。

Method: 将配置视为随机变量，在全配置空间上优化前缀窗口配置分布上的去噪风险，无需修改模型架构即可训练单一模型覆盖所有配置。

Result: ABD展现出跨解码尺度的结构不变性，避免离格崩溃，恢复块大小与困惑度之间的单调关系，在目标尺度上匹配或超越固定块专用模型。

Conclusion: 跨解码策略的泛化能力由训练分布的支撑集决定；ABD保证了任何推理策略（其配置在训练中被覆盖）的去噪最优性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) are typically trained under fixed context structures, restricting denoising to predetermined token subsets. This creates a mismatch between training and inference, where models must operate over arbitrary configurations, leading to degradation off the training grid. We propose Adaptive Block Diffusion (ABD), which resolves this mismatch by optimizing denoising risk over a distribution of prefix-window configurations. By treating the configuration as a stochastic variable, ABD trains a single model over the full configuration space without architectural changes. We show that generalization across decoding strategies is governed by the support of the training distribution, and that ABD guarantees denoising optimality for any inference policy whose configurations are covered during training. Empirically, ABD exhibits structural invariance across decoding scales, avoiding off-grid collapse and recovering a monotonic relationship between block size and perplexity, while matching or outperforming fixed-block specialists at their target scales.

</details>


### [77] [Deterministic Decisions for High-Stakes AI. A Zero-Egress Pipeline with the Deployability of RAG and the Accuracy of Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29280)
*Craig Atkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了零样本LLM教育咨询智能体存在“干预偏差”——即在无需干预的情况下过度推荐干预措施。在OULAD数据集上，零样本GPT-4o的假阳性率达43个百分点，而监督策略学习（决策Transformer和XGBoost）可消除此偏差。研究发现LLM-as-judge评估方法对此偏差“失明”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并量化零样本大语言模型在教育咨询场景中一个此前未被记录的失效模式——干预偏差（intervention bias），即模型在事后最优策略要求不干预时仍推荐行动。

Method: 在Open University Learning Analytics Dataset上设计六臂消融实验（N=800学生，四个时间截点）。零样本臂使用GPT-4o、RAG和SQL增强检索；监督臂使用轨迹条件化的ONNX决策变换器（Decision Transformer）和快照XGBoost分类器，基于相同的oracle标注轨迹，采用严格前缀特征训练。

Result: 零样本GPT-4o在第56天（oracle判定70.1%学生无需干预）仍对73%学生推荐干预，假阳性率43个百分点。监督策略学习消除偏差：DT达到macro-F1 0.79，macro-recall 0.85，0%行动翻转率，亚5ms CPU决策延迟。LLM-as-judge评估（DeepEval G-Eval）无法检测干预偏差。

Conclusion: 干预偏差是零样本LLM教育智能体的关键失效模式；监督策略学习可有效消除此偏差。研究发现揭示了LLM-as-judge评估方法存在“评估鸿沟”（Evaluation Gap），无法识别过度干预问题。研究范围限于Stage-2决策验证，高保真度反映特征-标注对齐而非通用高利害AI能力。

Abstract: We identify intervention bias as a previously unquantified failure mode of zero-shot large-language-model (LLM) educational advisory agents: without task-specific training, they recommend action when a hindsight-optimal oracle policy mandates inaction. In a six-arm ablation on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (N=800 students, four temporal cutoffs), at day 56 -- when the oracle designates 70.1% of students as needing no intervention -- zero-shot GPT-4o recommends action for 73%, a 43 percentage-point false-positive rate. Commercial RAG and SQL-augmented retrieval are comparably miscalibrated; at 10,000 students this implies about 4,300 unnecessary advisor contacts per cycle.
  Supervised policy learning eliminates this bias: a trajectory-conditioned ONNX Decision Transformer (DT) and a snapshot XGBoost classifier, trained on the same oracle-labelled trajectories under strict prefix-only features, both achieve near-zero calibration error. The DT reaches macro-F1 0.79 (macro-recall 0.85) across all five action classes, predicting even the rare load-reduction action without collapsing, at a 0% action flip rate and sub-5 ms CPU decision latency. The two supervised arms are on par; the DT's edge over XGBoost at the final cutoff is indicative only (unpaired across cohorts).
  Scope: we validate Stage-2 decision-making (EAV state vector to supervised policy) under controlled oracle input from structured OULAD data; high fidelity reflects feature-oracle alignment, not general high-stakes-AI capability. The most robust finding is the intervention-bias contrast, not the absolute accuracies. We also show an Evaluation Gap: LLM-as-judge scoring (DeepEval G-Eval) is blind to intervention bias, rewarding fluent over-prescription rather than decision quality.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Trajectory Matching: Reflow with Marginal Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29287)
*Chen Wang,Peiran Yun,Pan Xie,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出基于重流（reflow）的蒸馏方法存在轨迹匹配不能唯一确定学生模型诱导分布的理论局限，并提出边际对齐正则化器来惩罚学生与教师模型在蒸馏区间端点的边际分布差异，从而提升少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和连续流生成模型的高质量生成依赖确定性采样的ODE离散化，但精确离散化通常需要大量步数，少步生成效率是关键挑战。现有reflow-based蒸馏方法通过简化教师ODE轨迹让学生模型用更少步数逼近教师传输，但存在理论局限：轨迹匹配损失相同的学生模型可能诱导不同的端点边际分布，导致生成质量差异。因此需要一种能确保学生模型边际分布与教师边际分布一致的正则化方法。

Method: 提出边际对齐正则化器，通过沿学生模型诱导的ODE追踪对数密度变化并利用冻结的教师模型评估分数，来惩罚学生诱导边际与教师边际在蒸馏区间端点的差异。该方法无需额外的可训练网络或对抗优化，统一适用于vanilla reflow和piecewise reflow等重流家族方法。进一步证明了局部边际对齐可控制最终时刻学生与教师分布之间的总变差距离的 telescoping 上界。

Result: 在基准骨干网络上的实验验证了所提方法在少步生成中的有效性。

Conclusion: 证明了边际对齐正则化能有效弥补reflow蒸馏中轨迹匹配的理论缺陷，通过惩罚边际分布差异提升学生模型的生成质量，且具有统一适用性和理论保证。

Abstract: Diffusion and continuous-flow generative models achieve high-quality generation, and their deterministic sampling can be formulated as solving learned ODE dynamics. However, accurate ODE discretization often requires many steps, making efficient few-step generation a key challenge. Among acceleration strategies, reflow-based distillation simplifies teacher ODE trajectories so that a student model can approximate the teacher transport with fewer steps. We identify a theoretical limitation of this paradigm, namely that trajectory matching can under-determine the distribution induced by the student model. In particular, two student models can attain the same trajectory-matching loss while inducing different endpoint marginal distributions, which may lead to different generation quality. To address this limitation, we introduce a marginal-alignment regularizer that penalizes the discrepancy between the student-induced marginal and the corresponding teacher marginal at the endpoint of each distillation interval. The regularizer is computed by tracking log-density changes along the ODE induced by the student model and evaluating scores from the frozen teacher model, without requiring auxiliary trainable networks or adversarial optimization. The resulting framework applies uniformly to the reflow family, including vanilla reflow and piecewise reflow. We further prove a telescoping total-variation bound showing that local marginal alignment controls the final-time discrepancy between the student-induced and teacher-induced distributions. Experiments on benchmark backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method for few-step generation.

</details>


### [79] [SP-CACW: Convergence-Aware Client Weighting for Selfish Personalized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29322)
*Yaron Kiselman,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SP-CACW，一种面向自私个性化联邦学习的收敛感知客户端权重框架，通过最小化目标客户端收敛误差的上界来分配聚合权重，在多个数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习优化全局平均目标，对于数据分布与总体差异显著的客户端表现不佳。协作学习只有让每个参与者都受益才能持续，因此需要研究如何让目标客户端利用同伴梯度最小化自身风险，同时避免负迁移。

Method: 提出SP-CACW（Selfish Personalization with Convergence-Aware Client Weighting），通过最小化目标客户端收敛误差的上界来选择聚合权重，显式地在同伴偏差与随机方差之间进行权衡，并可以对有害同伴分配零权重。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-100和LEAF Shakespeare数据集上，该方法与强个性化基线和聚类基线相比具有竞争力或更优表现。

Conclusion: SP-CACW通过收敛感知的客户端权重分配，有效解决了联邦学习中的自私个性化问题，在理论上提供了光滑和有界方差假设下的收敛保证，实验上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative learning is sustainable only when it benefits each participant. Standard federated learning optimizes a global average objective, which can under perform for clients whose data distributions differ substantially from the population. We study selfish personalization: how a designated target client can use peer gradients to minimize its own risk while avoiding negative transfer. We propose SP-CACW, a convergence-aware client-weighting framework that selects aggregation weights by minimizing an upper bound on the target client's convergence error. The resulting rule explicitly trades off peer bias against stochastic variance and can assign zero weight to harmful peers. We provide convergence guarantees under smoothness and bounded-variance assumptions and evaluate the method on MNIST, CIFAR-100, and LEAF Shakespeare, where it is competitive with or improves over strong personalized and clustering baselines.

</details>


### [80] [Deciphering Region-Level Signatures from Latency Measurements in LEO Satellite Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29324)
*Xiang Shi,Yifei Zhang,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Starlink的RTT测量数据，提出分层分析框架提取多尺度统计特征，用于区域级延迟特征表征。研究发现延迟差异与基础设施可用性和Dish-to-PoP距离强相关，最小RTT是最具判别力的特征，模型在短期数据上达到83%准确率，但长期泛化能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量测量工作，但区域级性能特征背后的原理仍未被充分理解，限制了在动态网络条件下识别特定区域的延迟特征的能力。

Method: 基于LENS公开数据集的Starlink RTT测量，提出分层分析框架，将原始RTT序列转化为多尺度统计特征进行跨区域比较。使用互信息分析和XGBoost进行特征重要性评估。

Result: 延迟差异与基础设施可用性和Starlink Dish-to-PoP距离强关联；最小RTT是最具判别力的特征；模型在短期数据上达到83%准确率，但长期性能下降。

Conclusion: 当前模型在长期时间跨度上泛化能力有限，未来需要自适应模型和特征表示来支持长期性能分析。

Abstract: Low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite Internet has become an indispensable infrastructure that provide growing coverage for global users. Despite extensive measurement efforts, the principles underlying region-level performance characteristics remain insufficiently understood, limiting the ability to identify region-specific latency signatures under dynamic network conditions. In this paper, we formulate the problem of region-level latency characterization using Starlink round-trip time (RTT) measurements from the public LENS dataset. We then propose a hierarchical analytical framework that transforms raw RTT sequences into multi-scale statistical features for cross-region comparison. Using data from five geographically representative regions, we demonstrate that latency differences are strongly associated with deployment factors, particularly infrastructure availability and Starlink dish-to-Point-of-Presence distance. Mutual information analysis identifies minimum RTT as the most discriminative feature, which is further supported by XGBoost-based feature importance. The proposed model well achieves 83% accuracy on short-term data. However, its performance degrades over longer periods, indicating limited temporal generalization and motivating the need for adaptive models and feature representations for long-term performance in the future.

</details>


### [81] [Sample Complexity of Scientific Discovery: PAC Learnability of Compositional Function Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29331)
*Şuayp Talha Kocabay,Talha Rüzgar Akkuş,Kerem Yalçın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从PAC学习理论角度证明了符号回归中组合函数树的Rademacher复杂度并非随结构数量指数爆炸，而是由深度d和算子Lipschitz常数控制，风险界为O(L^d/√n)，并通过实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归中的科学发现通常被认为在统计和计算上难以处理，因为表达式的假设空间随深度组合增长。本文旨在重新审视统计学方面，证明在光滑算子词汇表构成的组合函数树中，泛化误差并非必然指数增长。

Method: 通过PAC学习理论分析组合函数树的Rademacher复杂度，利用有限词汇表K的并界和Maurer型向量收缩，推导出复杂度上界为(Kb√2L)^{d-1}ℜ_n(ℋ_comp^1)，并给出高概率风险界O(L^d/√n)。同时构建可微算子树代码库进行实验验证。

Result: Rademacher复杂度受深度d和算子Lipschitz常数L控制，而非结构数量指数增长；风险界为O(L^d/√n)；实验显示经验泛化差距与预测复杂度项(L̂^d)/√n正相关。

Conclusion: 组合函数树的泛化能力在温和Lipschitz条件下是可控制的，为符号回归的可学习性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Scientific discovery via symbolic regression is often viewed as statistically and computationally intractable because the hypothesis space of expressions grows combinatorially with depth. This paper revisits the statistical side through the lens of PAC learning, focusing on compositional function trees built from a finite vocabulary of smooth operators (e.g., $\{+,\times,\sin,\exp\}$ and affine maps). We prove that the relevant generalization quantity, Rademacher complexity, hence the excess risk, does not necessarily blow up exponentially with the number of distinct symbolic structures, but is controlled by (i) the depth $d$ and (ii) the Lipschitz constants of the base operators along the composed computation graph. Concretely, under mild Lipschitz conditions on operators and bounded affine leaves, a finite-union bound over a vocabulary of size $K=|\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{base}}|$ together with Maurer-type vector contraction yields $\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{d}) \leq (Kb\sqrt{2}L)^{d-1}\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{1})$ with arity bound $b$; corresponding high-probability risk bounds scale as $\mathcal{O}(L^{d}/\sqrt{n})$ when $K,b=O(1)$ and $\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{1})=O(n^{-1/2})$. We complement the theory with a modular codebase that trains differentiable operator trees (not MLPs) on synthetic "physics-like" targets of controlled depth and shows that the empirical generalization gap correlates positively with the predicted complexity term $(\widehat{L}^{d})/\sqrt{n}$.

</details>


### [82] [AMR: Adaptive Modality Routing for Multimodal Polyglot Speaker Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29335)
*Chuxiao Zuo,Yao Zhu,Minqiang Xu,Manhong Wang,Yunke Zhang,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自适应模态路由(AMR)的多模态多语种说话人识别系统，通过动态评估模态质量、融合W2V-BERT 2.0音频编码器和IResNet-18人脸编码器，在POLY-SIM 2026评测集上四协议平均准确率达99.07%，超越FOP基线32.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中多模态说话人识别面临两大挑战：模态缺失以及训练与测试条件间的语言不匹配；此外背景多说话人对话、环境噪声和重叠语音进一步降低了识别准确率。

Method: 系统基于自适应模态路由(AMR)模块：两个模态适配器处理来自W2V-BERT 2.0（音频）和IResNet-18（人脸）的嵌入，产生模态适应嵌入；可训练路由器据此估计动态模态权重，聚合模态特定logits进行最终预测；采用模态感知训练策略，构建四类样本对模拟不同输入条件，以KL散度显式监督权重分配。

Result: POLY-SIM 2026评测集上：英语多模态(P3) 99.93%、乌尔都语多模态(P5) 100.00%、英语纯音频(P4) 97.50%、乌尔都语纯音频(P6) 98.83%，四协议平均准确率99.07%，超越FOP基线32.73%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于AMR的多模态多语种说话人识别系统在多种模态条件和语言设定下均表现出高鲁棒性和准确性，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Multimodal speaker identification systems face two key challenges in real-world deployment: missing modalities and language mismatch between training and testing conditions. In practical scenarios, background multi-speaker conversations, ambient noise, and overlapping speech further degrade identification accuracy. To address these challenges, we propose a multimodal polyglot speaker identification system for the POLY-SIM 2026 Grand Challenge. The system is fundamentally built upon Adaptive Modality Routing(AMR), a modality fusion module that dynamically assesses per-sample input quality and integrates modality information. Specifically, AMR employs two modality adapters to process the embeddings extracted from a linguistically robust audio encoder(W2V-BERT 2.0) and a large-scale pretrained face encoder(IResNet-18), producing modality-adapted embeddings. Based on these adapted embeddings, a trainable router estimates dynamic modality weights, which are subsequently applied to aggregate the modality-specific logits for the final prediction. To optimize this routing mechanism, we adopt a modality-aware training strategy that constructs four types of sample pairs to simulate diverse input conditions, with KL divergence serving as explicit supervision for weight assignment. Experimental results on the POLY-SIM 2026 evaluation set show that the proposed system achieves identification accuracy of 99.93%(English multimodal, P3), 100.00%(Urdu multimodal, P5), 97.50%(English audio-only, P4), and 98.83%(Urdu audio-only, P6). The average accuracy across all four protocols is 99.07%, surpassing the Fusion and Orthogonal Projection(FOP) baseline by 32.73%.

</details>


### [83] [Reliability, Faithfulness, and the Limits of Post-hoc Explanations of Opaque Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29346)
*Nick Oh,Helen Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证后验解释方法无法独立支撑关于现象结构的科学主张，因为可靠性（模型预测匹配现象结果）和忠实性（解释匹配模型）都不确保模型以现象的方式运作，需要外部验证来支持候选假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示机器学习后验解释方法在科学应用中存在的根本性局限：即使模型可靠、解释忠实，也未必能真正揭示现象的内在结构。

Method: 逻辑论证分析，通过区分可靠性（模型-现象匹配）、忠实性（解释-模型匹配）和结构性匹配（模型-现象运作方式一致）三个层次，剖析后验解释链条的推理缺陷。

Result: 可靠性和忠实性都不确保模型与现象在运作方式上一致；解释链条仅能在外部验证支持下提供候选假设，无法独立支撑关于现象结构的论断。

Conclusion: 后验解释方法对科学发现的支持是有限度的，不能仅凭可靠的模型和忠实的解释就得出关于现象结构的知识，需要额外的外部证据进行三角验证。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanation methods are routinely used to interpret scientific machine learning models, with the deliverable understood to be insight into the phenomenon the model has been trained on. The transition may be taken to be secured once the model is reliable enough and the explanation faithful enough. We argue it is not. Reliability checks that the model's predictions match the phenomenon's outcomes, and faithfulness checks that the explanation matches the model, but neither checks whether the model works as the phenomenon works, which is what a claim about structure requires. The chain can support candidate hypotheses under external corroboration, but it cannot, on its own, support claims about how the phenomenon is in fact structured.

</details>


### [84] [Adaptive Financial Transformer with Regime-Gated Attention for Stock Return Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29347)
*Dishan Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融市场非平稳特性，提出AFT模型（自适应金融Transformer），通过市场状态编码器、自适应门控网络和自适应金融上下文模块，将95个金融特征分组为11个语义类别，动态调整自注意力机制。同时修正了序列对齐和回测漏洞，引入联合优化预测误差、方向准确率和非重叠夏普比率的复合目标函数。实验显示在降低15.2%模型复杂度下达到竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构对所有输入特征统一处理，无法适应金融市场的非平稳特性和不同市场状态下特征重要性的动态变化，且现有方法存在序列对齐和回测漏洞导致性能虚高的问题。

Method: 1) 将95个金融特征按语义分组为11个类别；2) Market Regime Encoder捕捉潜在市场状态；3) Adaptive Gate Network和Adaptive Financial Context模块根据市场状态和特征语义关系动态调整注意力权重；4) 修正序列对齐和回测问题；5) 联合优化MSE、方向准确率和非重叠夏普比率的复合损失函数。

Result: 在时序评估、5个随机种子、消融实验、超参数优化、可解释性分析和多股票验证下，AFT达到竞争性预测性能，同时降低15.2%模型复杂度，通过特征选择提升参数效率。

Conclusion: AFT提供了一种可解释的Transformer架构用于金融时间序列预测，通过语义分组和动态注意力有效应对市场非平稳性，且修正了先前工作中的评估偏差。

Abstract: Adaptive Financial Transformer (AFT) is proposed for stock return prediction under non-stationary financial markets. The model incorporates a Market Regime Encoder, an Adaptive Gate Network, and an Adaptive Financial Context module to dynamically bias self-attention based on semantic relationships between financial indicators. Unlike conventional Transformer architectures that treat all input features uniformly, the proposed approach groups 95 engineered financial features into 11 semantic categories and adapts attention according to latent market regimes. The study also identifies and corrects sequence alignment and backtesting issues that can inflate reported trading performance, and introduces a financially-aware composite objective that jointly optimizes prediction error, directional accuracy, and non-overlapping Sharpe ratio. Extensive experiments compare the proposed architecture against classical machine learning models, recurrent neural networks, and Transformer baselines using chronological evaluation, five random seeds, ablation studies, hyperparameter optimization, explainability analysis, and multi-stock validation. Results demonstrate competitive predictive performance while reducing model complexity by 15.2% and improving parameter efficiency through feature selection, providing an interpretable Transformer architecture for financial time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [85] [Interventional Flow Matching: Prospective Dose-Response Forecasting with Velocity-Field Jacobian Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29386)
*Amirreza Dolatpour Fathkouhi,Justin Lee,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Interventional Flow Matching (IFM)，一种基于连续时间生成框架的干预性预测方法，用于在血糖管理中预测患者对计划治疗序列的生理轨迹。IFM通过正则化速度场的雅可比矩阵来施加生理约束，无需显式ODE或 rollout 模拟，在UVA/Padova 1型糖尿病模拟队列上实现了观测精度与干预响应正确性的最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测仅关注观测准确性，无法保证对计划性干预（如胰岛素和碳水化合物摄入）的正确响应。生理轨迹预测本质上是一个前瞻性干预问题，需要模型能够正确反映治疗策略变化带来的因果效应。

Method: IFM是一种连续时间生成框架，在有界潜在血糖空间中，以患者历史和计划未来治疗驱动因素为条件，学习流匹配速度场。通过正则化速度场关于平滑处理驱动因素的雅可比矩阵，直接对学习到的动态施加符号约束和剂量有界局部敏感性：胰岛素降低血糖，碳水化合物升高血糖，且响应幅度在生理合理范围内。

Result: 在UVA/Padova 1型糖尿病模拟队列上，IFM在观测驱动因素RMSE和干预响应指标之间取得了最佳平衡。实验一致证明其对胰岛素和碳水化合物驱动因素均产生生理正确的响应，并保持了较高的方向和排序一致性。

Conclusion: IFM为生理约束下的前瞻性干预预测提供了一种无需显式ODE建模且无需 rollout 模拟的有效方案，在血糖管理这一关键医学应用中验证了其有效性，有望推广至更一般的治疗策略规划问题。

Abstract: Predicting a patient's physiological trajectory under a planned treatment sequence is a prospective interventional problem, not standard time-series extrapolation. We study this problem in glucose management, where insulin and carbohydrate records are policy-dependent: future drivers are coupled to patient state, behavior, and clinical decision rules, so observational forecasting accuracy alone does not guarantee correct responses to planned interventions.
  We introduce Interventional Flow Matching (IFM), a continuous-time generative framework for physiologically constrained prospective forecasting. IFM conditions a flow-matching velocity field on patient history and planned future drivers in a bounded latent glucose space. Rather than embedding strict mechanistic glucose--insulin ODE equations or enforcing causality through rollout-based simulations, IFM uses a solver-free regularization: it penalizes the Jacobian of the instantaneous velocity field with respect to smoothed treatment drivers. This imposes signed, dose-bounded local sensitivities directly on the learned dynamics: insulin lowers glucose, carbohydrates raise it, and both responses remain within plausible ranges.
  On a simulated UVA/Padova type 1 diabetes cohort, IFM achieves the strongest balance between observed-driver RMSE and interventional response metrics. Across experiments, it consistently produces physiologically correct responses to both insulin and carbohydrate drivers while maintaining high directional, and ranking consistency.

</details>


### [86] [Temporal Posed and Spontaneous Gesture Recognition from Electromyography in the Rock-Paper-Scissors Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29423)
*Xin Wei,Huakun Liu,Felix Dollack,Monica Perusquia-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过摇号猜拳（RPS）游戏实验，探索了表面肌电信号（EMG）在实时手势识别中的时间预测优势。研究发现EMG在手势可见开始前至少800ms即可检测到肌肉活动，峰值提前约342ms。在模仿手势和自发手势条件下，自手势识别准确率分别为63.4%和53.6%。此外，通过对手EMG信号检测观察者手势，峰值准确率达65%（滞后约2082ms），表明交互动态中包含对手反应信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时手势识别系统在多用户场景中需要快速识别，而EMG信号具有肌肉活动先于可见运动的特性，可提供时间预测优势。现有研究缺乏对EMG时序特征在真实交互（如RPS游戏）中的量化评估，因此本文探索EMG在手势发生前的可检测时间窗口及其识别准确性。

Method: 24名参与者以两人一组进行RPS游戏，采用双通道EMG记录前臂肌肉活动。分析EMG起始点（至少提前800ms）和峰值（提前约342ms）与手势可见起始的时间关系。在模仿和自发两种手势条件下评估自手势识别准确率，并检验通过对手EMG信号推断其人观察到的手势的可能性。

Result: EMG在手势可见前至少800ms可检测到起始活动，峰值提前约342ms。模仿手势识别准确率63.4%，自发手势识别准确率53.6%（个体差异显著）。对手EMG检测观察手势峰值准确率65%，出现在手势可见后约2082ms。

Conclusion: EMG信号的手势前时间预测优势为人机交互和辅助技术等需要快速意图识别的应用提供了潜在收益。未来工作需优化起始点检测算法，减少自发运动变异性对识别性能的影响，以适应动态真实环境。

Abstract: The importance of gesture recognition has been acknowledged in many domains requiring real-time recognition systems. Two requirements for these are fast recognition in multiuser contexts. Therefore, we explored the temporal characteristics of electromyography (EMG) and its accuracy in recognizing gestures in a Rock-Paper-Scissors (RPS) game. Twenty-four participants played RPS in dyads, while a two-channel EMG was recorded from the forearm. We found out that EMG onsets could be detected at least 800 ms before the gesture's visible onset, and that the EMG peaks around 342 ms before the visible onset of the gesture. Furthermore, we evaluated self-gesture recognition in both posed and spontaneous gesture conditions. The mean accuracy for posed gestures reached 63.4%. The model trained on posed gestures achieved 53.6% for spontaneous gestures, with considerable variation across individuals. We also checked whether detecting a player's gesture from the opponent's EMG was possible. The peak mean accuracy was 65%, peaking at 2082 ms after the visual onset of the gesture. This suggests that the opponent's reaction to an observed gesture contains information about the observed gesture due to the dynamics of the interactions while playing. The temporal predictive advantage of EMG signals, where muscle activation precedes observable movement, offers potential benefits for applications requiring rapid intent recognition, such as human-computer interaction and assistive technologies. Future work should focus on refining onset detection and reducing the impact of spontaneous movement variability across conditions to improve recognition performance in dynamic and real-world environments.

</details>


### [87] [Randomized neural operator for parametric PDEs with fast training and conformal uncertainty quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29440)
*Zirui Deng,Jingbo Sun,Deyu Meng,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCA—RaNN是一种结合PCA降维与固定随机特征、闭式最小二乘读出的潜在神经算子，将潜在算子学习转化为固定特征线性回归，训练速度比传统神经算子快1-3个数量级，同时保持竞争性精度，并支持不确定性量化与在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子求解参数化PDE需要昂贵的非凸训练，难以高效支撑不确定性量化、设计优化和反问题等需要反复求解的应用场景。

Method: 结合PCA降维与固定随机特征，将潜在算子学习转化为固定特征线性回归，使用闭式最小二乘读出；提出能量匹配缩放规则和轻量双参数BFGS修正优化特征尺度；通过集成平均降低预测方差。

Result: 在Burgers、Darcy、Navier-Stokes和反向热方程基准上，训练时间减少1-3个数量级，精度与现有算子学习基线相当；集成支持分割共形预测区间，线性读出支持递归最小二乘在线自适应。

Conclusion: PCA—RaNN提供了一种高效、支持不确定性量化的代理模型，适用于多查询科学工作流。

Abstract: Repeatedly solving parametric PDEs is essential for uncertainty quantification, design optimization and inverse problems, but conventional neural operators require expensive non-convex training. We introduce PCA--RaNN, a randomized latent neural operator that combines PCA-based dimensionality reduction with fixed random features and a closed-form least-squares readout. It recasts latent operator learning as fixed-feature linear regression, reducing training time by one to three orders of magnitude across benchmarks while maintaining competitive accuracy. We introduce an energy-matched scaling rule and a lightweight two-parameter BFGS refinement to correct suboptimal feature scales. Ensemble averaging reduces predictive variance. On Burgers, Darcy, Navier--Stokes and backward heat equation benchmarks, PCA--RaNN provides a favorable speed--accuracy trade-off against operator-learning baselines. The ensemble supports split-conformal prediction intervals, and the linear readout enables rapid online adaptation via recursive least squares without retraining hidden features. This provides an efficient, uncertainty-aware surrogate for many-query scientific workflows.

</details>


### [88] [Interpretable Inverse Design of Metal-Organic Frameworks with Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29459)
*Kyungmin Nam,Seunghee Han,Jihan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM4MOF是一个闭环框架，利用语言模型代理进行金属-有机框架的逆向设计，通过可解释的化学推理和模拟验证，在400次评估内就能在六个任务上聚焦高性能结构，并能从头生成新MOF，性能优于随机搜索和遗传算法，成本约每轮1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOF逆向设计面临组合空间巨大、属性标签昂贵、现有机器学习模型缺乏可解释性等挑战，亟需一种能高效搜索且能提供设计见解的方法。

Method: 采用双代理闭环框架：一个代理提出可解释的设计假设（涉及金属节点、连接子、孔几何和功能化学），另一个代理将其转化为约束条件筛选候选MOF；通过四条诊断束（应用不同子集约束）对比评估，区分几何、化学或金属选择对性能的影响；无需针对每个目标单独训练模型。

Result: 在六种吸附、分离和电子结构任务中，LLM4MOF仅400次评估就能聚焦高性能结构；可从头生成新MOF并在实时模拟中验证；性能优于随机搜索和遗传算法，每轮成本约1美元。

Conclusion: 语言模型代理能够执行可解释、基于模拟的逆向设计，无需为每个目标单独训练模型，为MOF等材料的智能设计提供了一种高效、低成本且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Inverse design of metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) requires searching a combinatorially vast space where property labels are expensive and most machine-learning models reveal little about why a structure succeeds. We introduce LLM4MOF, a closed-loop framework in which language-model agents reason about chemistry, build candidate MOFs, and test them in simulation, refining hypotheses over ten autonomous iterations. One agent proposes interpretable design hypotheses over metal nodes, linkers, pore geometry, and functional chemistry, and a second translates them into constraints that select candidate MOFs, each made of a metal node, organic linker, and matching topology. Each hypothesis is tested through four diagnostic beams that apply different subsets of its constraints, so comparing them shows whether geometry, chemistry, or metal choice drives performance. Even when blind to the global property landscape of databases, LLM4MOF concentrates its search on top-performing structures across six adsorption, separation, and electronic-structure tasks within 400 property evaluations. The same loop also generates new MOFs de novo and validates them in live simulation, where it adapts the geometry to each requested condition, outperforming random search and a genetic algorithm at roughly $1 per campaign. LLM4MOF shows that language-model agents can run interpretable, simulation-grounded inverse design without training a model per objective.

</details>


### [89] [Prototype Latent World Model Replay for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29465)
*Weizhi Nie,Hui Wang,Weijie Wang,Yuting Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出原型潜在世界模型回放（Prototype Latent World Model Replay），一种无内存的类增量学习框架。该方法使用冻结的ImageNet预训练编码器将图像映射到潜在状态空间，每个类通过多个以原型为中心的分布进行总结。新类到来时，从原型世界模型中采样旧类潜在状态，结合新类真实特征训练轻量适配器和分类器，并加入监督对比项促进类内紧凑性和新旧类分离。在Split CIFAR-100上，Inc5/Inc10/Inc20设置下LastAcc分别从4.55%/9.06%/16.96%提升至31.64%/37.06%/43.10%，AvgAcc达45.86%/52.19%/56.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决类增量学习中无法保存原始旧样本时，如何保留旧类决策区域的问题，提出无需存储原始图像的无记忆回放框架。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的ImageNet预训练编码器将图像映射到稳定潜在状态空间；2) 每个类通过多个以原型为中心的分布（含类特定方差）进行总结；3) 新类到来时从原型世界模型采样旧类潜在状态；4) 联合旧类采样状态和新类真实特征训练轻量适配器和分类器；5) 在适配器空间加入监督对比项促进类内紧凑性和新旧类分离。

Result: 在Split CIFAR-100上，Inc5/Inc10/Inc20设置下LastAcc分别从4.55%/9.06%/16.96%提升至31.64%/37.06%/43.10%，AvgAcc达45.86%/52.19%/56.18%。消融实验表明稳定潜在状态回放是主要收益来源，对比分离进一步优化新旧类几何结构。

Conclusion: 原型潜在记忆能够保留可复用的类状态分布，而不仅仅是拟合当前分类器，为类增量学习提供了一种有效且无需存储原始样本的解决方案。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning requires a model to learn new classes while preserving decision regions for old ones. This is difficult when raw old samples are no longer available. We propose Prototype Latent World Model Replay, a memory-free framework that stores old classes as distributions over stable hidden states rather than as images. A frozen ImageNet-pretrained encoder maps each image into a latent state space. In this space, each class is summarized by several prototype-centered distributions with class-specific variances. When new classes arrive, the model samples old latent states from this prototype world model. It then trains a lightweight adapter and classifier using both sampled old states and real new-class features. We also add a supervised contrastive term in the adapter space to promote intra-class compactness and old-new class separation. On Split CIFAR-100, our method improves over fine-tuning under Inc5, Inc10, and Inc20 without storing raw exemplars. The full Ours-LWM+Con model raises LastAcc from 4.55% to 31.64%, from 9.06% to 37.06%, and from 16.96% to 43.10% in Inc5, Inc10, and Inc20, respectively. It also achieves AvgAcc of 45.86%, 52.19%, and 56.18%. Ablation and retention analyses show that stable latent-state replay is the main source of the gain. Contrastive separation further refines the old-new geometry. These results suggest that prototype latent memory preserves reusable class-state distributions, rather than only fitting the current classifier.

</details>


### [90] [Self-Supervised Calibration of Scientific Instruments Using Physical Consistency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29466)
*M. Rejmund,A. Lemasson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息驱动的自监督学习框架，无需预校准信号或外部标签，即可从原始测量数据中联合学习潜在校准参数和任务特定预测，并在VAMOS++磁谱仪中实现了离子电荷态的高精度确定与探测器状态的自动监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准是机器学习在科学仪器中部署的主要障碍之一，因为传统校准依赖专家干预、专用程序和人工标注数据，效率低下且难以自动化。

Method: 利用已知物理约束迭代生成伪标签，将校准转化为自监督优化问题；通过迭代分数伪标签法，利用原子质量的离散性逐步精化预测；采用物理信息驱动的自监督框架，同时学习潜在校准参数和任务预测。

Result: 在VAMOS++磁谱仪中实现了精确的离子电荷态重构；推断出的校准系数提供了探测器状态的紧凑表征，支持对增益漂移、压力变化和探测器老化的自动监测；生成的标签可迁移至专用模型，用于量化探测器缺陷并追踪其时空演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学仪器的自校准与自监控建立了一般性范式，推动了能够自主进行校准、分析和性能优化的智能实验系统的发展。

Abstract: Calibration remains one of the principal obstacles to the deployment of machine learning in scientific instrumentation because it typically relies on expert intervention, dedicated procedures, and manually labelled data. We introduce a physics-informed self-supervised framework that jointly learns latent detector calibration parameters and task-specific predictions directly from raw measurements without requiring pre-calibrated signals or external labels. The method exploits known physical constraints to generate pseudo-labels iteratively, transforming calibration into a self-supervised optimization problem. The approach is demonstrated for ionic charge-state determination in the VAMOS++ magnetic spectrometer, where the calibration of a segmented ionization chamber and the inference of ionic charge states are learned simultaneously. Starting from a weak prior on the mean ionic charge state, the model progressively refines its predictions through iterative fractional pseudo-labelling driven by the discrete nature of atomic masses. Beyond accurate ionic charge-state reconstruction, the inferred calibration coefficients provide a compact representation of the detector state that enables automated monitoring of gain drifts, pressure variations, and detector aging. The resulting labels can subsequently be transferred to specialized models that quantify detector imperfections and track their spatial and temporal evolution. These results establish a general paradigm for self-calibrating and self-monitoring scientific instruments and represent a step toward intelligent experimental systems capable of autonomous calibration, analysis, and performance optimization.

</details>


### [91] [Structured Proper Loss Geometries for Multiclass Classification: Theory and Controlled Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29471)
*Soumyadip Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种多分类目标函数（CAPM、HPG、APMS），从理论上推导了它们的条件遗憾、曲率、范围和logit梯度边界，并通过消融实验表明这些方法在干净数据上接近交叉熵，在某些噪声标签场景下有描述性提升，但总体不支持其具有普遍优越性的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格真确的评分规则在总体水平上能识别真实条件类别分布，但其曲率会改变优化行为和有限样本表现，因此需要研究不同多分类目标的曲率特性及其对实际效果的影响。

Method: 研究三种多分类目标：类感知二次Bregman评分（CAPM）、强凸生成器带约束log-cosh脊（HPG）、以及带退火概率-边界惩罚的HPG目标（APMS）。推导了CAPM和HPG的条件遗憾、曲率、范围和logit梯度边界，以及APMS的精确惩罚范围和条件目标位移边界。在Digits、威斯康星乳腺癌、合成混淆和长尾问题上进行5次种子实验，涵盖干净标签、对称和成对翻转噪声、类别不平衡、校准评估、输入腐蚀和一阶对抗扰动。

Result: 候选方法在干净数据上接近交叉熵，在某些噪声标签场景下有描述性提升，但5次种子实验仅作描述性解释而非显著性证据。在Digits 40%对称标签噪声下，选定的噪声标签基线表现更好；在30:1合成长尾实验中，显式先验调整方法表现更好。消融实验未显示候选特定的图、脊或边界组件的持续优势。

Conclusion: 数学分析建立了所述性质，实验界定了经验证据范围，两者共同不支持这些方法具有普遍优越性的主张。

Abstract: Strictly proper scoring rules identify the true conditional class distribution at population level, but their curvature can alter optimization and finite-sample behavior. We study three multiclass objectives: a class-aware quadratic Bregman score (CAPM), a strongly convex generator with constrained log-cosh ridges (HPG), and an HPG objective with an annealed probability-margin penalty (APMS). CAPM is treated as a structured instance of established quadratic scoring-rule theory. We derive conditional-regret, curvature, range, and logit-gradient bounds for CAPM and HPG, and prove exact penalty-range and conditional-target displacement bounds for APMS. Controlled five-seed experiments use Digits, Wisconsin breast cancer, and synthetic confusion and long-tail problems under clean labels, symmetric and pair-flip corruption, class imbalance, calibration evaluation, input corruption, and first-order adversarial perturbations. The candidates are close to cross-entropy on clean data and show descriptive gains in some noisy-label cells, but the five-seed comparisons are interpreted descriptively rather than as significance evidence. The selected noisy-label baselines perform better on Digits with 40% symmetric label noise, and explicit prior-adjustment methods perform better in the 30:1 synthetic long-tail experiment. Ablations do not show a consistent benefit from the candidate-specific graph, ridge, or margin components. The mathematical analysis establishes the stated properties, and the experiments delimit the empirical evidence; together they do not support a claim of general superiority.

</details>


### [92] [CRAFT: Counterfactual Credit Assignment from Free Sibling Rollouts for Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29476)
*Zibin Meng,Kani Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT 通过三项改进（反事实令牌重要性、非对称控制、极化KL惩罚）解决了自蒸馏强化学习中教师-学生对数概率差值的回顾性与符号盲点问题，在几乎零额外计算下实现带符号的逐令牌信用分配，并通过可独立关闭的开关确保算法改进与实现漂移的可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏强化学习使用教师-学生对数概率差值作为蒸馏损失的门控信号，该信号存在双重局限：①回顾性（仅评分已实现的轨迹，无法评估反事实轨迹）；②符号盲点（无法指示教师偏好动作是否会损害轨迹整体表现）。

Method: CRAFT 包含三个支柱：①反事实令牌重要性（Pillar 1），复用GRPO的G-1兄弟轨迹，通过对数概率差值进行重要性加权，形成分组级反事实优势变化的自归一化估计，实现带符号的逐令牌信用分配；②非对称控制器（Pillar 2），根据门控活动的指数移动平均动态调整蒸馏权重与参考KL权重；③极化KL惩罚（Pillar 3），根据信用信号的符号在逐令牌级别在模式寻找与模式覆盖更新之间切换。

Result: 提供了估计量的一致性和方差界证明，给出结构级和比特精确的可重复性保证，在三种智能体环境、四种模型规模和五种端到端方法（外加两个表格化的先前工作基线）上评估CRAFT。其中包含Adaptive-CRINGE比较器（与CRAFT共享Pillar 2），隔离了反事实贡献的影响。

Conclusion: 每个支柱具有独立开关，禁用时损失和梯度在IEEE-754算术上与基线字节一致，确保任何测量增益可归因于算法变化而非实现漂移。

Abstract: Self-distilled agentic reinforcement learning augments trajectory-level reward with a token-level distillation loss, using as its teacher the same policy conditioned on privileged context. The prevailing recipe gates this loss by a single scalar, the teacher-student log-probability gap. This signal is doubly limited: it is retrospective, scoring only the realised rollout and never the counterfactual ones, and it is sign-blind, never signalling when a teacher-preferred action would have harmed the trajectory. We introduce CRAFT, a three-pillar credit-assignment scheme that addresses both limitations. Pillar 1, Counterfactual Token Importance, reuses the G-1 sibling rollouts that GRPO already samples and importance-weights them by the log-probability gap to form a self-normalised estimate of the group-level counterfactual change in advantage from up-weighting teacher-preferred actions at each step; this yields a signed per-token credit at near-zero extra compute. Pillar 2 is an asymmetric controller that raises the distillation weight as it lowers the reference-KL weight along an exponential moving average of gate activity, and conversely. Pillar 3 polarises the KL penalty token by token, switching between a mode-seeking and a mode-covering update according to the sign of the credit. Each pillar has an independent switch that, when disabled, renders the loss and gradient byte-identical to the baseline in IEEE-754 arithmetic, so any measured gain is attributable to algorithmic change rather than implementation drift. We prove the estimator's consistency and a variance bound, give structural and bit-exact reproducibility guarantees, and evaluate CRAFT across three agentic environments, four model scales, and five end-to-end methods, plus two tabulated prior-work baselines. Among these is Adaptive-CRINGE, a comparator sharing Pillar 2 with CRAFT, isolating the counterfactual contribution.

</details>


### [93] [Reported Confidence in LLMs Tracks Commitment More Than Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29490)
*Dharshan Kumaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段弃权范式，发现大语言模型的言语置信度反映的是内部"提交就绪"状态而非答案正确性，与对数概率置信度存在本质分离：对数概率追踪答案证据和正确性，而言语置信度则是面向行为的承诺信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大语言模型中言语置信度是否应被理解为对答案正确性的概率估计，因为当前广泛使用言语报告作为不确定性度量，但其基础属性尚不明确。

Method: 采用认知神经科学中的两阶段弃权范式：模型先回答并报告置信度，再决定是否提交给用户或弃权。在4个非推理模型、多种提示框架和置信度格式下，比较言语置信度与校准后的token对数概率对弃权决策和正确性的预测能力，并进行机制分析（Gemma 3/4的激活空间表征和因果操控）。

Result: 言语置信度预测弃权决策的能力显著优于预测正确性；对数概率则相反。移除与对数概率共享的方差后，言语置信度与弃权决策的关联保持，与正确性的关联降至接近随机。激活空间分析显示，支持言语置信度生成的post-answer状态主要按弃权决策而非正确性组织，两者方向近似正交。因果操控言语置信度特定方向可改变弃权行为。

Conclusion: 言语置信度与对数概率置信度不可互换：对数概率追踪答案证据和正确性，而言语置信度是面向行为的内部提交就绪状态读出。这挑战了将言语报告作为可靠性代理的实践。

Abstract: Confidence is an estimate of the probability that a chosen answer is correct. Verbal confidence reports are widely used as uncertainty measures in large language models, but whether they are best understood as estimates of correctness is unclear. We test this with a two-stage abstention paradigm from the neuroscience of perceptual decision making: a model first answers and reports its confidence, then decides whether to commit it to a user or abstain. Across four non-reasoning models, prompt framings, and confidence formats, verbal confidence predicted the commit/abstain decision substantially better than whether the answer was correct. Calibrated token log-probabilities showed the opposite profile, with abstention-prediction coupled to correctness discrimination, the signature of an answer-evidence signal. After removing the variance verbal confidence shared with log-probabilities, the residual stayed aligned with commitment while its link to correctness fell to near chance. The dissociation generalised to four reasoning models across four benchmarks of varying difficulty, from hard multiple-choice to frontier-level freeform questions. Mechanistic analyses in Gemma 3 and 4 were convergent: a post-answer state known to causally support verbal-confidence generation already encoded the future abstention decision before the abstention prompt, organised mainly by that decision rather than by correctness, the two lying in approximately orthogonal directions in activation space. Steering along a verbal-confidence-specific direction causally shifted abstention. Verbal and log-probability confidence are thus not interchangeable: log-probabilities track answer evidence and correctness, whereas verbal confidence is better understood as a behaviour-facing readout of an internal commit-readiness state, challenging the practice of treating verbal reports as proxies for reliability.

</details>


### [94] [Reinforcement Learning in Super Mario Bros: Curriculum, Pedagogy, and Optimal Level Design in World 1-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29511)
*Jesse Ponnock,Lucas Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用强化学习算法（Q-Learning、SARSA、Monte Carlo、DQN）首次实证验证了《超级马里奥兄弟》World 1-1的经典设计具有可测量的教学结构，其中Monte Carlo表现最优（94.9%胜率），且其规范关卡排序在学习效率、收敛速度和零灾难性失败方面均优于随机排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管World 1-1被广泛认为是游戏设计的典范，其渐进式结构被认为能教授玩家核心机制，但这一教学效果是否可以被经验性地量化测量尚未得到验证。作者希望用量化方法（强化学习）来实证检验这一设计中的教学结构是否真实存在。方法中突出Mote Carlo表现最优，并分析了它与DQN等方法的差异。结论是World 1-1的规范设计编码了可测量的教学结构，且这种结构不能通过随机排列复现。

Method: 将World 1-1实现为完全离散环境，比较四种强化学习算法（Q-Learning、SARSA、Monte Carlo、DQN）在三个渐进复杂版本上的表现；随后使用最优的Monte Carlo算法进行课程学习实验，将关卡划分为六个规范片段进行12种排列实验。

Result: Monte Carlo胜率94.9%±1.5%，优于DQN（76.4%±3.4%）；Monte Carlo通过最大化获胜路径上的中间奖励而非选择最直接路径来学习。在课程实验中，规范排序收敛最快、学习效率最高，且是唯一零灾难性失败的条件，无随机排列能同时满足这三个指标。

Conclusion: 首次为World 1-1的经典设计提供了经验性验证，证明其编码了真实的教学结构：能够可测量地加速学习，且无法通过随机排列复制。

Abstract: World 1-1 of Super Mario Bros is widely celebrated as a masterclass in game design: its progressive structure is credited with teaching players core mechanics through the level itself. We ask whether that structure is empirically measurable using reinforcement learning. We implement World 1-1 from scratch as a fully discrete environment and compare four algorithms -- Q-Learning, SARSA, Monte Carlo, and Deep Q-Network (DQN) -- across three progressively complex versions of the same level. Monte Carlo emerges as the strongest agent (94.9% $\pm$ 1.5% win rate), outperforming DQN (76.4% $\pm$ 3.4%) by learning to maximize intermediate rewards along winning paths rather than taking the most direct route. We then use Monte Carlo in a curriculum experiment permuting World 1-1's six canonical segments across twelve conditions. Canonical ordering converges fastest, achieves the highest learning efficiency, and is the only condition with zero catastrophic failures; no random permutation matches all three criteria simultaneously. These results provide, to the best of our knowledge, the first empirical validation that World 1-1's canonical design encodes genuine pedagogical structure: one that measurably accelerates learning and cannot be replicated by chance.

</details>


### [95] [A Mathematical Optimization Approach for Expert-Informed Bayesian Best Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29516)
*Nolan Alexander,Henning Mortveit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EBBS方法，通过将领域专家的特征相关性概率估计（基于多受访者聚合）作为对数几率惩罚项融入MIO最佳子集选择的目标函数，实现专家知识驱动的稀疏回归特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的最佳子集选择仅依赖观测数据，无法利用领域专家对候选预测因子相关性排名的先验知识，缺乏将专家概率评估直接整合到目标函数中的框架。

Method: 将多受访者的专家意见聚合为每个特征的先验概率（使用泊松二项分布估计边际概率、成对胜率或归一化平均秩），以对数几率惩罚项形式嵌入MIO最佳子集问题的最大后验（MAP）目标函数中。

Result: 从理论上推导了MAP公式并刻画了其性质；当所有专家无意见时，EBBS退化为经典最佳子集选择；合成与真实数据集的实证结果尚未给出（forthcoming）。

Conclusion: EBBS为在最佳子集选择中融入专家先验知识提供了一种理论严谨、计算可行的框架，填补了现有方法在专家知识整合方面的空白。

Abstract: A central challenge in statistical modeling is identifying the subset of features that belong in the true regression model. The classical best subset selection problem, recently made tractable via mixed-integer optimization (MIO), finds the globally optimal sparse solution. It does not, however, make use of any information beyond the observed data. In many applied settings, domain experts can meaningfully rank or score the relevance of candidate predictors, yet no existing framework integrates such probabilistic expert assessments directly into the best-subsets objective. This paper presents Expert-Implied Bayesian Best Subsets (EBBS), a method that incorporates domain-expert probability estimates of feature relevance into the MIO best-subsets problem through a maximum a posteriori (MAP) framework. Expert views from multiple respondents are aggregated into a single prior probability per feature using the Poisson binomial distribution for marginal probability estimates, the pairwise win rate for pairwise comparisons, or the normalized mean rank for ordinal rankings. This probability enters the objective function as a log-odds penalty term that smoothly encourages or discourages the selection of each feature consistent with the expert consensus. This paper provides analytic derivations of the MAP formulation and characterizes its theoretical properties. The proposed model reduces to Best Subsets when experts all have no views. Empirical results on synthetic and real datasets are forthcoming.

</details>


### [96] [Anti-Collapse Dynamics and the Emergence of Multi-Time-Scale Learning in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29519)
*Lorenzo Livi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环神经网络中长程学习的困难，发现网络的时间尺度分布由状态动力学与参数动力学的耦合决定，存在两种遗忘模式：指数遗忘的"塌缩"模式和幂律遗忘的"反塌缩"模式。反塌缩模式依赖于训练过程中重尾波动对长时间尺度的推动，且需要一个关键参数——谱指数β来统一控制时间尺度的分布与遗忘速率。研究证明了只有在架构和优化器具备生成宽谱能力时，反塌缩模式才能成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络在长程依赖学习上面临根本性困难，因为梯度随时间滞后呈指数衰减导致学习所需数据量指数增长。现有研究多关注架构改进，但缺乏对遗忘率渐近衰减类别的理论理解，不清楚是何种机制决定网络是快速指数遗忘还是缓慢幂律遗忘。

Method: 通过粗粒化随机过程对状态动力学与参数动力学的耦合进行建模，从理论上分析了这两种动力学之间的竞争关系：训练驱动有效时间尺度趋于短值，而重尾波动推动少数时间尺度趋向长值。通过严格的数学推导证明了扩展反塌缩模式存在的条件，并引入谱指数β统一描述时间尺度分布与遗忘速率。

Result: 证明了遗忘的渐近衰减类别不是由架构固定决定的，而是由耦合动力学涌现产生。扩展反塌缩模式（幂律遗忘）仅当重尾推动强到足以平衡训练驱动的拉力时才存在。此外，即使存在强重尾推动，如果架构生成宽谱的能力受到严重限制，网络仍会塌缩为指数遗忘模式。重尾波动不是需要抑制的噪声，而是维持长程学习的关键机制。

Conclusion: 长程学习的本质机制是重尾波动与训练驱动之间的动态平衡，这为设计具备长程学习能力的网络提供了理论基础：需要同时考虑架构的谱生成能力和优化器引入的波动特性，而非仅关注单一因素。

Abstract: Long-range learning is hard for recurrent networks trained with stochastic gradient descent, because the influence of a past input fades with the lag $\ell$, and if it fades too fast the dependence cannot be learned from finite data. This fade is captured by an envelope $f(\ell)$. An exponential fade makes the data needed to learn a lag-$\ell$ dependence grow exponentially, putting long horizons out of reach; a power-law fade keeps the cost polynomial. We show that the asymptotic decay class of $f(\ell)$ is not fixed by the architecture. Instead, it emerges from the coupling between the state dynamics and parameter dynamics, settling into either a collapsed regime (fast, exponential forgetting) or an extended, anti-collapsed regime (slow, power-law forgetting). The intuition is a competition within these coupled dynamics. Training drives the network's effective time scales toward short ones, while rare, heavy-tailed fluctuations of the learning dynamics push a few of them to very long values. The extended regime survives only when these heavy-tailed pushes are strong enough to balance the pull. We make this mathematically precise with a coarse-grained stochastic process and prove exactly when the extended regime exists. A single exponent, the spectral exponent~$β$, then governs both the spread of time scales and how slowly the network forgets. Realizing the regime in practice needs one more ingredient: the joint action of the architecture and the optimizer must be able to hold such a broad spread. A network whose capacity to generate broad time-scale spectra is severely constrained still collapses, even when supplied with strong heavy-tailed forcing. Heavy-tailed fluctuations thus act not as noise to be suppressed, but as the mechanism that sustains long-range learning.

</details>


### [97] [Not All Objectives Are Born Equal: Priority-Constrained Descent for Hierarchical Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29521)
*Dara Varam,Mohamed I. Alhajri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD)，一种利用目标层次结构的梯度优化框架，通过单个参数τ控制主目标方向保持与次目标约束之间的权衡，在压缩、稀疏性等任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习中多目标优化方法忽视了目标之间的层次结构（如主目标与次目标的重要性差异），存在对称性问题，无法利用目标空间固有的优先级关系。

Method: 提出Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD)框架：保持主目标下降方向的同时，施加最小必要的方向扭曲以保证次目标的进展，扭曲强度由单一参数τ∈[0,1]控制。该公式对目标缩放具有不变性，且在二/三目标问题上有精确闭式解。

Result: 在结构化网络压缩、非结构化稀疏性和低秩性任务中，PCD实现了Pareto优势，每个目标上的性能更优，且提供了次目标进展保证。τ参数提供了可解释的权衡。

Conclusion: PCD有效地利用目标层次结构的先验知识，在保持主目标性能的同时保证次目标收敛，优于现有对称性多目标方法，且τ提供了直观的调控方式。

Abstract: Deep learning problems rarely involve objectives that are equal in importance. A primary objective defines the goal, whilst secondary objectives, such as sparsity, compression, or robustness constrain the solution. While existing multi-objective methods have proven effective in practice, they have a clear symmetry problem and neglect the inherent objective hierarchy built into these objective spaces. We introduce Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD), a gradient-based optimization framework designed to explicitly exploit hierarchical objective structures. PCD preserves the direction of primary descent whilst allowing for the minimal distortion necessary to guarantee progress on secondary objectives, controlled by a single $τ\in [0, 1]$ that dictates the strength of the distortion. The resulting formulation is invariant to objective scaling and admits exact closed-form solutions for problems with two and three objectives. We evaluate PCD within structured network compression settings, unstructured sparsity and low-rankness, and across a variety of synthetic experiments, showing Pareto dominance and better per-objective performance with secondary progress guarantees over existing methods, further exhibiting the interpretable trade-off that $τ$ provides.

</details>


### [98] [Do Models Read What They Write? Causal Registers in Scratchpad Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29522)
*Benjamin Shih,John Winnicki,Eric Darve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过状态追踪任务验证了过程监督的关键条件：模型必须将中间状态写入scratchpad并基于这些状态进行后续计算。实验表明，经过状态写入训练的模型在编辑内部状态后，有80%-91%的概率基于编辑后的状态预测正确结果，而仅训练最终答案的模型则不能。这为scratchpad监督提出了更精确的目标：不仅要让中间推理可读，更要训练模型实际使用这些写入的状态进行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程监督的核心假设是模型可以暴露对后续行为重要的中间变量，但这一假设成立的前提是scratchpad必须与实际计算绑定——即模型写入状态后，后续步骤应基于该状态进行计算。现有研究缺乏对这一绑定关系的严格验证。

Method: 使用受控的状态追踪任务（已知更新规则），比较三种模型：（1）仅训练报告最终状态的模型；（2）训练写入中间状态后再给出最终答案的模型；（3）预训练基线。在评估时，编辑模型内部表示中的一个写入状态，同时保持可见的scratchpad文本不变，观察模型是否基于编辑后的内部状态预测正确的下游结果。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B上，状态写入模型在两种任务变体中分别有80%和91%的测试样本预测了编辑后状态隐含的正确下一阶段比特位。而预训练基线和仅最终答案的对照模型保持在基线水平。额外对照实验排除了通用下一token引导和复制其他续写的可能性，表明预测依赖于编辑后的状态和当前移动两者。该因果使用模式在多个模型家族中复制。

Conclusion: 研究结果指向scratchpad监督的一个更精确目标：不仅要让中间推理过程可读（legible），更要训练模型使写入的状态被模型实际用作计算的一部分。这为改进过程监督方法提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: A central hope behind process supervision is that models can expose intermediate variables that matter for their later behavior. For this to help with alignment, a scratchpad must be tied to the computation: when the model writes a state, later steps should compute from that state. To test this requirement, we use a controlled state-tracking task with a known update rule, comparing models trained to report only the final state with models trained to write intermediate states before giving the final answer. At evaluation, we edit the internal representation of one written state while leaving the visible scratchpad text fixed. Because the transition rule is known, the edit has a single correct downstream consequence. In Qwen2.5-Coder-7B, the state-writing model predicts the next phase bit implied by the edited state on 80% and 91% of held-out examples across the two task variants, while pretrained and final-answer-only controls remain near baseline. Additional controls rule out generic next-token steering and copying another continuation: the prediction depends on both the edited state and the current move. The same causal-use pattern replicates across model families. Together, these results suggest a sharper goal for scratchpad oversight: not just to make intermediate reasoning legible, but to train written states that the model uses as part of its computation.

</details>


### [99] [The Mirage of Optimizing Training Policies: Monotonic Inference Policies as the Real Objective for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29526)
*Jing Liang,Hongyao Tang,Yi Ma,Yancheng He,Weixun Wang,Xiaoyang Li,Ju Huang,Wenbo Su,Jinyi Liu,Yan Zheng,Jianye Hao,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM强化学习后训练中因推理引擎与训练引擎概率不一致导致的训练-推理不匹配问题，指出现有方法忽视了目标对齐缺陷，提出单调推理策略改进（MIPI）目标及相应框架MIPU，通过构建采样器参考候选更新并利用推理侧间隙代理选择性接受同步候选，在高不匹配条件下提升了推理性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM强化学习训练中存在由训练引擎与推理引擎分离导致的概率不一致问题（训练-推理不匹配），这种天然的off-policy现象会毒化训练过程。现有方法虽尝试解决off-policyness来稳定训练策略，但忽略了目标对齐缺陷——训练引擎中对策略的有效更新并不能保证推理策略（即部署使用的策略）的改进。

Method: 提出单调推理策略改进（MIPI）目标，并据此设计MIPU框架：一个两步式LLM强化学习方法，首先构建采样器参考的候选更新，然后利用推理侧间隙代理（inference-side gap proxy）选择性接受同步后的候选更新，从而确保推理策略的单调改进。

Result: 在两种模型规模、高不匹配条件下进行的实验表明，MIPU框架提升了平均推理性能和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示并解决了LLM强化学习后训练中因训练-推理引擎不匹配导致的目标对齐问题，所提出的MIPI目标和MIPU框架能有效保证推理策略的单调改进，在高不匹配场景下兼具性能提升与训练稳定性优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has gained growing attention in large language model (LLM) post-training, yet RL training remains fragile and can suffer from instability or collapse. One vital cause is training-inference mismatch: LLM adopts separate inference and training engines for generation efficiency and training precision, which in practice exhibits inconsistent probabilities for the same trajectories on training and inference sides, even with synchronized model parameters. This naturally induces a special type of off-policyness ever existing and poisoning the training. Prior works have made various efforts in addressing the off-policyness to stabilize the training policies under the mismatch. In this paper, we point out the objective misalignment neglected by existing works that an effective update to the policy in the training engine not necessarily ensures the improvement of the inference policy, i.e., the one used in deployment. To this end, we propose a new policy optimization objective for LLM RL, named Monotonic Inference Policy Improvement (MIPI). Following this principle, we introduce Monotonic Inference Policy Update (MIPU), a two-step LLM RL framework that constructs sampler-referenced candidate updates and selectively accepts synchronized candidates using an inference-side gap proxy. Experiments conducted on two model scales under high mismatch show that MIPU improves average reasoning performance and training stability.

</details>


### [100] [VISTA-DZ: Visual Semantic Trajectory Adaptation for Personalized Dilemma Zone Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29548)
*Chuheng Wei,Ziye Qin,Ziran Wang,Guoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA-DZ框架，通过将历史轨迹转化为视觉表示并用视觉语言模型生成行为语义描述，实现个性化的停车-通行决策及决策时间预测。在SDZ和FDZ数据集上，该模型在域内仿真准确率达93.26%，跨20名保留驾驶员平均准确率90.22%，并展示了仿真到现实的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉口 dilemma zone 中驾驶员决策安全关键，但现有个性化模型依赖手工标量描述符，对个体行为（如风险偏好、制动习惯、决策阈值）的建模能力有限，无法充分捕捉驾驶员的个性化差异。

Method: 提出 VISTA-DZ 框架：将历史轨迹转换为视觉表示，由视觉语言模型生成行为描述（语义profile）；通过语义嵌入条件化双输出预测网络；结合双向GRU编码器、驾驶员条件多头交叉注意力及特征级线性调制（FiLM）进行时序证据选择与特征自适应。

Result: 在SDZ和FDZ数据集上，VISTA-DZ域内仿真准确率93.26%，20名保留驾驶员平均准确率90.22%；跨域实验展示了可行的零样本仿真到真实迁移，以及仿真数据结合有限现场数据时的更好泛化效果。

Conclusion: 语义profile条件化方法能有效捕获驾驶员个性化决策行为，在停车-通行预测和决策时间预测任务上优于轨迹仅与手工个性化基线，且具备跨域迁移潜力。

Abstract: Driver decision making in the dilemma zone at signalized intersections is safety critical, as vehicles approaching a yellow signal must decide whether to stop or proceed within limited time and distance margins. Accurate prediction of both stop-go decisions and decision timing is important for adaptive signal control, advanced driver assistance systems, and human-centered intelligent transportation applications. However, dilemma zone behavior is strongly driver dependent. Similar approach trajectories may lead to different decisions across drivers because of differences in risk preference, braking habit, and decision threshold. Existing personalized models often rely on handcrafted scalar descriptors, which provide useful but limited summaries of individual behavior. This paper proposes VISTA-DZ, a semantic-profile-conditioned framework for personalized stop-go and decision-time prediction. Historical trajectories are converted into visual representations, interpreted by a vision-language model to generate behavioral profiles, and encoded as semantic embeddings to condition a dual-output prediction network. The final model combines a bidirectional GRU encoder, driver-conditioned multi-head cross-attention, and Feature-wise Linear Modulation for temporal evidence selection and feature adaptation. Experiments on the SDZ dataset and a newly collected FDZ dataset show that VISTA-DZ outperforms trajectory-only and handcrafted personalization baselines, achieving 93.26% in-domain simulation accuracy and 90.22% mean accuracy across 20 held-out simulation drivers. Cross-domain results further show feasible zero-shot simulation-to-real transfer and better real-world generalization when simulation data are combined with limited field data.

</details>


### [101] [Optimizer Memory Makes Shuffle Order a First-Order Source of Fine-Tuning Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29554)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在微调过程中，数据洗牌顺序产生的噪声比无记忆优化器预测的更大。固定时钟优化器（如AdamW）的动量缓冲和计数器随步索引而非学习率缩放时间推进，导致相同梯度集因位置不同产生O(η)量级对比，而非理论预期的O(η²)。作者通过升维状态展开隔离机制，导出无需拟合的噪声规模公式，并给出顺序噪声误差条、位置归因权重和种子预算判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论认为无记忆优化器下重排等多元集只产生二阶效应，但实践中微调对数据顺序高度敏感，甚至单个种子就能翻转A/B对比结果。本文旨在揭示固定时钟优化器（如AdamW）中存在的这一被低估的噪声源，并量化其影响。

Method: 通过升维状态展开分析固定时钟优化器（AdamW、固定β动量、SGD）的局部等多元集对比；利用比特确定性重放从预热优化器状态隔离机制；推导闭式渐近阶方差下限（AdamW冻结预处理器时无需拟合系数）。

Result: 固定时钟优化器的等多元集对比为O(η)而非O(η²)；阶方差斜率：AdamW为1.83，固定β动量为2.00，SGD为4.00；时钟匹配可恢复常规指数；导出无需拟合的渐近阶方差下限公式。

Conclusion: 数据顺序噪声在固定时钟优化器中显著大于理论预期，必须纳入微调比较的误差预算；本文提供的位置归因权重和种子预算判据可用于设计更可靠的实验对比。

Abstract: Shuffle order can be a larger source of fine-tuning noise than a memoryless analysis predicts: fixed-clock optimizer memory makes local equal-multiset contrasts first order in the learning rate rather than second order, and the resulting order channel can be large enough for a single seed to flip a close A/B comparison. We isolate this mechanism and derive a fit-free way to size the noise it produces. For a memoryless optimizer, reordering an equal multiset has no first-order endpoint term; the leading local contrast is the $O(η^2)$ gradient bracket. Fixed-clock optimizers such as AdamW are different. Their moment buffers, preconditioner state, and de-biasing counters advance with the step index rather than with the learning-rate-scaled time $τ=ηk$, so the same gradient can receive a position-dependent endpoint weight. For any fixed finite measurement window, a lifted-state expansion gives an $O(η)$ equal-multiset contrast whenever the first-order replay coefficient is nonzero, while regular and clock-matched controls remain $O(η^2)$; a bare fixed-$β$ momentum buffer is already enough. A bitwise-deterministic replay from one warmed optimizer state isolates the mechanism, giving order-variance slopes 1.83 for AdamW, 2.00 for fixed-$β$ momentum, and 4.00 for SGD; matching the memory clock to $τ$ restores the regular exponent. For AdamW with a frozen preconditioner, the same impulse-weight kernel gives a closed-form asymptotic order-variance floor after the local potentials are measured, with no fitted coefficients. The result is local to the measurement window (independent reshuffling can average the channel across windows), but it yields order-noise error bars, positional attribution weights, and a seed-budget criterion for fine-tuning comparisons.

</details>


### [102] [Persona-Trained Monte Carlo: Estimating Market-Outcome Distributions via Swarms of Persona-Conditioned Neural Policy Bots in a Limit Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29556)
*Salavat Ishbulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人格训练蒙特卡洛（PTMC）方法，通过多次模拟人格条件化神经策略交易机器人群体在限价订单簿中的交互，来估计市场结果统计量的分布，并给出了形式化框架、跨学科设计依据和验证路线图，但尚未实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（经典蒙特卡洛、手工编码的基于主体模型、单智能体强化学习、LLM生成式智能体）在捕捉交易者异质性和市场微观结构动态方面存在局限，PTMC旨在通过人格参数化的神经策略机器人群体模拟来更真实地估计市场统计量分布。

Method: PTMC方法的核心是：(1) 训练一个策略网络，多个机器人共享该网络但通过从学习的交易者异质性分布中采样的个体人格参数进行条件化；(2) 在连续双向拍卖中交互生成价格路径作为单次蒙特卡洛样本；(3) 重复独立的人格群体采样得到集成样本用于估计目标市场统计量。随机性来源包括人格采样、动作采样和可选的外生冲击。

Result: 本文尚未实现PTMC，主要贡献是提出了形式化估计量、跨学科设计论证（涵盖计算经济学、市场微观结构、行为金融、深度强化学习、LLM智能体、新闻驱动交易、系统性风险、经济物理学和博弈论）以及四层验证方法论（风格化事实匹配、微观结构层验证、智能体层验证、历史压力测试对比零智能基线）。

Conclusion: PTMC框架通过人格参数化的神经策略机器人群体模拟，为市场统计量估计提供了一种新范式，结合了基于主体的计算经济学和深度强化学习，但尚未实现，未来需解决策略网络训练、人格分布学习、计算效率等开放性问题。

Abstract: We propose Persona-Trained Monte Carlo (PTMC), a method for estimating distributions of market-outcome statistics by repeatedly simulating limit-order-book interaction among swarms of persona-conditioned neural-policy trading bots. Each run instantiates many bots sharing one trained policy network but conditioned on heterogeneous, individually sampled persona parameters drawn from a learned trader-heterogeneity distribution; the bots interact in a continuous double auction, and the resulting price path is one Monte Carlo sample. Repeating this over independent persona-population draws yields an ensemble from which a target market statistic is estimated. Randomness enters through persona draws, within-run action sampling, and optional exogenous shocks, not solely through price as in classical Monte Carlo. We distinguish PTMC from adjacent paradigms, including classical Monte Carlo, hand-coded agent-based models, single-agent reinforcement learning, and large-language-model-based generative agents. To justify the design, we survey cross-disciplinary foundations -- agent-based computational economics, market microstructure, behavioral finance, deep reinforcement learning, generative/LLM-based agents, news-driven trading, systemic risk, econophysics, and game theory -- connecting each literature to a specific design choice in the policy network, training data, or validation protocol. We formalize the PTMC estimator and its convergence properties, specify a candidate bot architecture and training objective, and propose a four-level validation methodology: stylized-fact matching, microstructure- and agent-level checks, and historical stress-test comparison against a zero-intelligence baseline. The framework is proposed but not implemented: we contribute a formal estimator, a cross-disciplinary design justification, and a validation roadmap, and conclude with open research questions.

</details>


### [103] [Speculative Pre-Positioning: Decoding Stateful Sessions to the Next Decision Point Off the Critical Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29565)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种推测性预定位（Speculative Pre-positioning）方法，让有状态推理会话在请求间隙继续解码，利用空闲时间预计算后续请求的起始状态，从而将首Token生成延迟从约39毫秒（前缀缓存方案）降低到约1.0毫秒，且无需草稿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无状态推理服务器（如vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM）在请求之间处于空闲状态，加速器等待新请求到来。这些空闲时间未被利用，导致每次新请求都必须从头执行前缀匹配和初始解码，造成首Token延迟瓶颈。

Method: 在有状态会话中，利用请求间隙继续用目标模型自身的前向传播进行解码，沿会话路径向前推进到下一个决策点（预定位）。当新请求到达时，从预计算的状态恢复（增量方式），或当置信度门被触发时，直接从缓存分布中通过近似常数时间的词汇扫描返回答案，无需解码。该方法不需要草稿模型，仅增加能耗和有限的有界误接受率。

Result: 对于能力强的模型，置信度门可以达到接近全覆盖的覆盖率和约87%的精确率，首Token返回时间约为1.0毫秒；而前缀缓存方案仍需39毫秒的解码延迟。较小的模型则无法触发置信度门。

Conclusion: 推测性预定位通过利用请求间空闲时间进行预计算，能够显著降低首Token延迟，其收益依赖于模型能力：只有足够强大的模型才能有效触发置信度门并实现接近零延迟的响应。

Abstract: A stateless inference server (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM) idles between requests while the accelerator waits; a stateful session reclaims that idle time. Speculative pre-positioning decodes the session forward to its next decision point with the target model's own forward pass and no draft model, moving the cross-request prefill and entry-decode off the critical path: the next request resumes from a pre-paid entry on its delta, or, when a confidence gate fires, is answered from a cached distribution in one near-constant vocabulary scan with no decode, at a cost only of energy and a rare, bounded false accept. The payoff is conditional on capability: a capable model fires the gate at near-full coverage and about 87% precision (a smaller one never clears it), returning the first token in about 1.0 ms versus the 39 ms decode a prefix cache still pays.

</details>


### [104] [The Joint Effect of Quantization and Sampling Temperature on LLM Safety Alignment: A Factorial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29581)
*Hari Prasad,Ritam Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了LLM部署中量化精度（FP16/INT8/INT4）与采样温度（T=0~1.0）对安全性的影响，发现标准量化通常安全中性，高温显著增加决策不稳定，但两者未系统性叠加放大风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估通常将模型压缩（量化）和温度采样视为固定实现细节，忽略了它们在实际部署中可能相互影响、共同影响模型安全性的不确定性。

Method: 对6个模型家族、9个指令微调模型进行因子化评估，涵盖3种精度（FP16、GPTQ INT8、AWQ INT4）和6种温度（0~1.0），共161种配置，约32.2万条响应，由6模型安全集成进行评判。

Result: 标准量化通常安全中性，INT4对9个模型中7个保持或降低攻击成功率，退化集中在最弱模型SmolLM3-3B（18.5%→36.0%）；高温显著增加决策不稳定性，DFR在T=1.0时达53.0%；量化与温度的交互呈次加性（复合退化指数-0.195~+0.045），未系统性叠加。

Conclusion: 标准INT4/INT8量化对强对齐模型合理，但高温下的安全声明应报告多采样稳定性而非仅平均攻击成功率。

Abstract: Modern LLM deployments routinely compress models and raise sampling temperature to reduce cost, latency, or repetition, yet safety evaluations usually treat these choices as fixed implementation details. This leaves a practical uncertainty: does a model that is safe at FP16 and greedy decoding remain safe after it is quantized and sampled stochastically, or do the two deployment knobs amplify one another? We study this question with a factorial evaluation of 9 instruction-tuned models from six families, 3 precisions (FP16, GPTQ INT8, AWQ INT4), and 6 temperatures ($T{=}0$ to $1.0$), yielding 161 configurations and $\approx$322k responses judged by a six-model safety ensemble. Contrary to the concern that low-bit deployment broadly erodes alignment, standard non-adversarial quantization is usually safety-neutral: INT4 keeps or lowers attack success for 7 of 9 models, with clear degradation concentrated in the weakest baseline model, SmolLM3-3B ($18.5\%{\to}36.0\%$). The larger risk comes from sampling: higher temperature sharply increases decision instability for vulnerable models, with DFR reaching 53.0\% at $T{=}1.0$, even when average ASR changes modestly. Finally, the interaction is not a ``double penalty'': our Compound Degradation Index remains largely sub-additive ($-0.195$ to $+0.045$), indicating that quantization and temperature do not systematically compound. These results suggest a deployment rule of thumb: standard INT4/INT8 quantization can be reasonable for strongly aligned models, but safety claims at elevated temperature should report multi-sample stability, not only average attack success.

</details>


### [105] [Bilevel Optimization for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29582)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过双层优化视角系统综述了神经架构搜索（NAS）方法，将其分为基于采样的方法和基于双层理论的方法，并提出了一种辅助数学规划框架，该框架通过同时更新架构和模型参数的最优下降方向，实现了更原理性且理论上一致的结果。研究表明，基于双层理论的方法在准确性和效率上普遍优于基于采样的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）本质上是双层优化问题（外层优化架构参数，内层优化网络权重），但现有NAS方法缺乏统一的优化理论视角，亟需从双层优化角度进行系统梳理和理论提升。

Method: 将现有NAS方法分为两类：(1) 基于采样的方法：使用不同架构采样器搜索最优架构；(2) 基于双层理论的方法：运用双层优化原理解架构搜索问题。进一步提出辅助数学规划框架，通过引入二阶信息（模型训练损失函数的曲率信息），同时沿各自最优下降方向更新架构参数和模型参数，确保参数最优性。

Result: 辅助数学规划框架能够系统性地整合训练损失函数的二阶信息，确保修改架构参数时模型参数的最优性，并可同时用于超参数和模型微调。对比分析表明，基于双层理论的方法在准确性和效率上均优于基于采样的方法。

Conclusion: 双层优化为NAS提供了统一的理论视角；辅助数学规划框架可实现架构与模型参数的协同最优更新，是更原理性的NAS解决方案；未来可进一步拓展该框架至更广泛的超参数优化场景。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization has become an influential and widely adopted framework for addressing hierarchical optimization problems in machine learning, providing an effective approach to modeling the interaction between two levels of optimization, with applications such as hyperparameter tuning, meta-learning, adversarial training, and data poisoning. Neural Architecture Search (NAS), a subfield of hyperparameter optimization, is a prime example of a bilevel optimization problem, with architecture parameters optimized at the outer-level and network weights optimized at the inner level. This paper presents a structured overview of NAS through the lens of bilevel optimization. We categorize existing NAS approaches into two main classes: sampling-based methods, which search optimal architectures using different architecture samplers, and bilevel theory-based methods, which solve the architecture search problem using bilevel optimization principles. We further highlight our current research direction, wherein the bilevel NAS formulation is addressed through an auxiliary mathematical programming framework. This framework enables the systematic integration of second-order information from the model's training loss function and ensures the optimality of the model parameters while modifying architecture parameters. By simultaneously updating the architecture and model parameters along their respective optimal descent directions derived from the auxiliary mathematical program, these methods achieve more principled and theoretically consistent results. The same auxiliary program can also be used for simultaneous hyperparameter and model fine-tuning. A comparative analysis shows that bilevel theory-based approaches generally outperform sampling-based methods, both in accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [106] [STEMGym: Benchmarking Sequential Decision-Making under Dose Budgets in Autonomous Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29592)
*Can Polat,Erchin Serpedin,Mustafa Kurban,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过STEMGym基准测试发现，在扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）中，决定剂量效率的关键因素是分析（感知）管道，而非导航策略。使用CNN分析器搭配简单光栅扫描可提升DEC-AUC 5.5倍，而贝叶斯或自适应导航相比光栅扫描无显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"更智能的导航是样本高效采集的主要杠杆"这一自主科学成像的主流假设，系统性地评估感知与导航在STEM剂量效率中的相对贡献。

Method: 构建STEMGym基准测试平台，包含15个物理模拟STEM环境（5种材料、3种难度、4种表征任务），使用DEC-AUC指标量化剂量效率，测试33种智能体配置并在现实剂量预算下比较不同感知管道（CNN、视觉语言模型等）与导航策略（光栅扫描、贝叶斯、自适应有限状态机）的组合效果。

Result: CNN分析器+光栅扫描的DEC-AUC（0.287）相比无CNN的光栅基线（0.052）提升5.5倍；而用贝叶斯或自适应导航替代光栅扫描无统计显著增益；生产级视觉语言模型性能比任务特定CNN差~13倍。

Conclusion: 自主电子显微镜中ML投入应集中在感知管道而非导航策略上，STEMGym提供了统一剂量预算下解耦感知、导航与规划的测试基础设施。

Abstract: A central premise of autonomous scientific imaging is that smarter navigation, whether Bayesian, RL-based, or otherwise adaptive, is the principal lever for sample-efficient acquisition. We present evidence to the contrary in scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM), an atomic-resolution imaging modality whose every measurement deposits damaging electron dose. We introduce STEMGym, an open-source Gymnasium benchmark of 15 physics-simulated STEM worlds spanning five materials, three difficulty levels, and four characterisation tasks, scored by the Dose-Efficiency Curve area (DEC-AUC), a single scalar capturing the information-vs-dose Pareto frontier. Across 33 agent configurations under realistic dose budgets, the dominant determinant of dose efficiency is the analyst (perception) pipeline, not the navigator: pairing a trained CNN analyst with naïve raster scanning raises DEC-AUC by 5.5x over a CNN-free raster baseline (0.287 vs.\ 0.052), while substituting Bayesian or adaptive finite-state-machine navigation for raster yields no statistically significant further gain. Production-tier vision-language models further underperform task-specific CNNs by {\sim}13x on crystallographic defect analysis. By decoupling perception, navigation, and planning under a unified dose budget, STEMGym reframes where ML effort should be invested in autonomous electron microscopy and provides the measurement infrastructure to test it.

</details>


### [107] [How AI settled the complexity of the oldest SGD algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29593)
*Michał Dereziński,Xiaoyu Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文讲述了Kaczmarz算法（随机梯度下降最早形式）的最坏情况复杂度是如何通过现代AI模型（如ChatGPT和Gemini）合作发现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Kaczmarz算法的最坏情况复杂度，利用现代AI模型的能力来解决经典算法问题。

Method: AI模型（ChatGPT和Gemini）协同合作，共同分析Kaczmarz算法的理论性质。

Result: 成功发现了Kaczmarz算法的最坏情况复杂度。

Conclusion: 现代AI模型不仅受惠于Kaczmarz算法（作为SGD的雏形），还能反过来帮助解决该算法的理论难题，展示了AI辅助数学研究的潜力。

Abstract: In 1937, Stefan Kaczmarz proposed a simple algorithm for solving systems of linear equations. This algorithm turned out to be the earliest known example of stochastic gradient descent, a ubiquitous computing paradigm that drives the training of modern AI models such as ChatGPT and Gemini. Now, those AI models have joined forces to discover the worst-case complexity of the Kaczmarz algorithm. This paper tells the story of how it happened.
