Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($β\in \{β_{\mathrm{lo}}, β_{\mathrm{mid}}, β_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$β$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $β$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(β, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $β^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


### [182] [When Does Online Imitation Learning Help in LLM Post-Training? The Role of (Non-)Realizability Beyond Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30445)
*Huaqing Zhang,Jingchu Gai,Juno Kim,Bingbin Liu,Andrej Risteski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"误差累积"是在线模仿学习优势的主要来源这一观点，转而证明在线交互的收益取决于设定是否可实现（即学生策略类能否表示专家策略）。在可实现设定下，离线IL已能匹配专家性能；在不可实现（错误设定）设定下，离线IL存在信息论瓶颈，而在线IL即使在分布严重不匹配时仍能实现高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线模仿学习（特别是on-policy蒸馏）在LLM后训练中表现优异，但对其优势来源缺乏原则性理解。本文旨在澄清在线交互何时以及为何比离线方法更有效，挑战"误差累积"这一主流解释。

Method: 理论分析与实证验证相结合。通过区分可实现（realizable）与不可实现（misspecified）设定，在线下IL与在线IL之间进行对比分析。在可实现设定下进行实证验证；在不可实现设定下给出信息论瓶颈的理论证明，并提出基于奖励的结构化误设定刻画。

Result: 1) 在可实现设定下，离线IL已能匹配专家性能，在线IL无额外优势；2) 在不可实现设定下，即使horizon H=1，离线IL也存在信息论瓶颈；3) 提出了基于奖励的结构化误设定刻画，在此条件下在线IL能在分布严重失配时仍取得高性能。

Conclusion: 在线模仿学习的优势并非源于误差累积的缓解，而是取决于设定是否可实现。在不可实现设定下，在线交互的信息优势是突破离线方法瓶颈的关键。

Abstract: Online imitation learning (IL), particularly on-policy distillation, has emerged as a strong LLM post-training approach, often outperforming offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Yet a principled understanding of when and why online interaction helps remains unclear. In this work, we challenge the view that error accumulation is the main source of online IL's advantage, and instead show that the benefits of online interaction depend critically on whether the setting is realizable, i.e., whether the student policy class can represent the expert policy. Under realizability, we empirically find that offline IL already matches expert performance. In contrast, in non-realizable (misspecified) settings, we prove that offline IL encounters an information-theoretic bottleneck even when horizon $H=1$, and propose a structural characterization of misspecification relative to the reward, under which online IL provably achieves high performance despite a large distributional mismatch between the expert and student policies.

</details>


### [183] [Internal-State Probes Read the Situation, Not the Action: Three Negative Results for Pre-Action Misalignment Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30449)
*Max Fomin,Elad David,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究模型内部探针能否在有害文本或工具动作生成前进行有效监测（预动作监测）。通过对三个模型系列（Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Gemma-3-27B-IT）的三种方法进行测试，发现构造有效性、语义可读性和引导效应均无法成为稳健的预动作监测器——它们都被泛化性检验或特异性检验所否定。作者贡献了一套将内部读出声索转化为预动作测试的方法论，并报告了有限范围内的负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控智能体系统的内部状态，以在有害行为实际发生前进行预警——即实现"预动作监测"的更强主张。现有探针往往仅描述当前输入或轨迹，而未能真正预测未来行为。

Method: 测试三种方法：Qwen模型的微调/基座方向向量、Llama模型在不安全前缀最后一个token上的探针、Gemma模型的情感概念向量用于投影和引导。构建预动作测试框架，包括泛化性检验（跨场景/动作迁移）和特异性检验（区分目标概念与无关概念）。

Result: Qwen方向区分微调与基座AUC达1.000，但在0/143个预助理轮次和0/342个不安全延续行中触发阈值；Llama特征解码提示域AUC达0.999，但最优未来行为探针仅AUC 0.801，跨域迁移在5/6有序对中非正；Gemma情感投影语义有意义，但共享前缀最小对在首个差异输入前无法区分，引导特异性被无关概念（猫、天气、体育、地理）削弱。

Conclusion: 内部读出的预动作监测主张必须通过场景/动作泛化和概念特异性双重控制检验；目前三种方法均未通过检验，报告有限范围内负面结果；提供了一套将内部读出声索转化为可检验预动作测试的方法论。

Abstract: Probes on model internals could help monitor agentic systems if they identify harmful text or tool actions before those actions are generated. We ask when an internal readout supports this stronger pre-action claim, rather than merely describing the prompt, construction contrast, or current trajectory. We test three methods across three model families: a Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct fine-tune/base direction, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct probes at the last token of unsafe prefills, and Gemma-3-27B-IT emotion-concept vectors used for projection and steering in a blackmail tool-action scenario. Across these cases, construction validity, semantic legibility, and steering effects do not become robust pre-action monitors: each is undercut by a generalization or specificity check. The Qwen direction separates fine-tune from base at AUC 1.000, yet crosses its threshold on 0/143 audited pre-assistant turn contexts and on 0/342 Qwen prefill rows where the model continues the unsafe trajectory. The Llama features decode prompt domain almost perfectly (AUC 0.999), while the best future-behavior probe reaches AUC 0.801 and only +5.1 pp accuracy lift over majority; single-source cross-domain transfer is non-positive on five of six ordered pairs. Gemma emotion projections are semantically meaningful, but a shared-prefix minimal pair has indistinguishable states before the first differing input, and steering specificity weakens against unrelated learned directions such as cats}, weather, sports, and geography. We contribute a methodology for converting internal-readout claims into pre-action tests, and report scoped negative results: monitor claims must survive both scenario/action generalization and concept-specificity controls. Code is released at https://github.com/maxf-zn/misalignment_monitoring

</details>


### [184] [Exploring Differences Between Tabular Enterprise Data and Public Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30452)
*Myung Jun Kim,Maximilian Schambach,Frank Essenberger,Andre Sres,Johannes Höhne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了企业表格数据与常见基准测试数据的差异，发现现有表格模型在典型基准测试上的表现无法推广到真实企业数据，表明需要专门针对企业级数据的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据在数据科学中占据主导地位但缺乏专门研究，现有表格模型基准测试未能反映企业数据的真实特性。

Method: 通过对企业数据统计特征的分析，并在TabPFN、TabICL、ConTextTab等表格模型上进行性能测量与对比。

Result: 企业数据与典型表格基准存在显著差异；在典型基准上表现良好的模型在企业数据上可能表现不佳，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 现有表格模型缺乏跨数据域的泛化能力，亟需构建具有企业级特性的新基准。

Abstract: Tabular data dominate the landscape of data science, increasingly attracting innovative machine learning models and tailored benchmarks. Yet, little is known for enterprise data, where tables constitute the backbone of business operations. To broaden the benchmarking landscape for business applications, this work aims to actualize the characteristics of enterprise data by providing an analysis of data statistics and performance measurements of tabular models such as TabPFN, TabICL and ConTextTab. Through our analysis, we find enterprise data markedly differ from tabular benchmarks and we demonstrate that a tabular model that performs well on typical tabular benchmarks may perform poorly on real world enterprise data -- and vice versa. This lack of generalization underlines the need for additional benchmarks with enterprise-grade characteristics.

</details>


### [185] [Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity: A Noise Measure for Geometry-Adaptive SGD Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30455)
*Muhammad Hamza,Ayush Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率加权梯度多样性（CWGD），一种几何感知的优化噪声度量，通过Hessian逆平方根加权各样本梯度多样性。在强凸二次目标上证明CWGD调制的余弦学习率调度可将渐近优化误差上限降低最多两倍，并在多种条件下实现约20%的最终优化误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准小批量SGD收敛分析用单一方差项建模梯度噪声，平等对待所有参数方向，忽略了高曲率方向噪声影响较小（因为学习率已受约束）的事实。需要一种几何感知的噪声度量来更紧地刻画有效优化噪声。

Method: 提出CWGD，通过Hessian逆平方根加权梯度多样性。对强凸二次对角Hessian目标，理论证明CWGD调制的余弦调度可降低误差上限。实现CWGD-Cosine算法，使用Hutchinson方法估计对角Hessian（对二次目标精确）。

Result: CWGD-Cosine在多种条件数、批次大小和噪声结构下，相比标准余弦退火持续实现约20%更低的最终优化误差，渐近误差上限最多降低两倍，且二次设置中开销可忽略。

Conclusion: CWGD是一种原理性的几何感知优化噪声度量，在二次目标上效果显著。讨论了退化曲率估计器的修正、鲁棒性分析及非凸优化中Hessian过时等局限性，为推广到更一般的学习问题奠定基础。

Abstract: The standard convergence analysis of mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD) models gradient noise using a single variance term that treats all parameter directions equally, ignoring the fact that noise in high-curvature directions has less impact because learning rates are already constrained there. We introduce Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity (CWGD), a geometry-aware measure that weights per-sample gradient diversity by the inverse square root of the Hessian, providing a tighter proxy for the effective optimization noise. For strongly convex quadratic objectives with diagonal Hessians and isotropic noise, we prove that a CWGD-modulated cosine learning-rate schedule can reduce the asymptotic optimization error floor by up to a factor of two compared with standard cosine annealing. We implement this idea as CWGD-Cosine using a Hutchinson-based diagonal Hessian estimator that is exact for quadratic objectives. Across a range of condition numbers, batch sizes, and noise structures, CWGD-Cosine consistently achieves approximately 20% lower final optimization error than standard cosine annealing while incurring negligible overhead in the quadratic setting. We also identify and correct a degenerate curvature estimator, analyze the robustness of the proposed estimator, and explicitly discuss the limitations of the method, including Hessian staleness in non-convex optimization. These results establish CWGD as a principled geometry-aware measure of optimization noise and motivate future extensions to more general learning problems.

</details>


### [186] [MuonSSM: Orthogonalizing State Space Models for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30461)
*Thai-Khanh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich-Uyen Vo,Thieu N. Vo,Tan M. Nguyen,Cuong Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonSSM通过动量路径和低秩输入注入的Newton-Schulz变换来显式条件化记忆更新的几何结构，从而稳定SSM训练，改善梯度传播、减轻频谱放大并丰富长程记忆表示，在语言、视觉和时间序列基准测试中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型（SSM）在处理长序列时，由于一阶更新条件数差和更新几何不平衡，存在训练不稳定和记忆退化问题。

Method: 提出MuonSSM框架：不直接约束循环转移矩阵，而是通过动量增强路径和低秩输入注入上的轻量级Newton-Schulz变换来显式条件化记忆更新的几何结构，保持有界且谱条件良好的更新，同时保留并行扫描复杂度。

Result: 在语言、视觉和时间序列基准上，集成到多种SSM主干中均获得准确性、鲁棒性和长上下文性能的一致提升。

Conclusion: 更新几何的条件化是实现稳定、可扩展序列建模的原则性路径。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) have emerged as efficient linear-time alternatives to attention for long-sequence modeling. However, existing SSMs often suffer from instability and memory degradation over extended horizons due to poorly conditioned first-order updates and unbalanced update geometry. We introduce MuonSSM, a general framework that stabilizes SSM training by explicitly conditioning the geometry of memory updates rather than the recurrent transition matrix. MuonSSM augments SSMs with a momentum-based pathway and a lightweight Newton Schulz transformation on low-rank input injections, yielding bounded and spectrally conditioned updates while preserving parallel scan complexity. Theory shows that MuonSSM improves gradient propagation, mitigates spectral amplification, and enriches memory representations over long horizons. Extensive experiments across language, vision, and time-series benchmarks show consistent gains in accuracy, robustness, and long-context performance when integrated into diverse SSM backbones. These results establish geometric conditioning of updates as a principled pathway to stable, scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [187] [Discovering Collaboration from Novelty: Random Network Distillation for Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30499)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Ivana Dusparic,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机网络蒸馏（RND）的轻量级聚类联邦学习方法，通过客户端本地RND预测器的预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计客户端之间的相似性，在联邦训练开始前就发现有意义的客户端分组，实现聚类与主训练的完全解耦，适用于非独立同分布数据下的大规模分布式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非独立同分布数据下表现不佳，单一全局模型难以代表客户端分布的多样性。现有聚类联邦学习方法通常将聚类分配与主训练循环耦合，增加了计算和通信开销。

Method: 每个客户端在本地数据上训练一个紧凑的随机网络蒸馏（RND）预测器，利用其预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计与其他客户端的相似性，从而在联邦训练开始前发现有意义的客户端分组，整个过程无需共享原始数据或重复评估主模型。

Result: 聚类结果在运行时从本地新颖性估计中自然涌现，无需预先指定聚类数量或协作结构，且聚类与学习过程完全解耦，提供了任务无关且高效的机制。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将聚类与主训练解耦，为大规模分布式系统在非独立同分布数据下实现自主协作提供了一种轻量级、任务无关且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning often suffers under non-independently and identically distributed data, where a single global model may fail to represent the diversity of client distributions. Clustered Federated Learning mitigates this issue by training specialized models for groups of similar clients, but existing approaches often couple cluster assignment with the main training loop, increasing computational and communication costs. We propose a lightweight clustering approach based on Random Network Distillation. Each client trains a compact Random Network Distillation predictor on its local data and uses its prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with other clients. This enables the discovery of meaningful client groups before federated training, without sharing raw data or repeatedly evaluating the main model. Crucially, the resulting federations emerge from local novelty estimates at runtime, making the method suitable for autonomous large-scale distributed systems where neither the number of clusters nor the collaboration structure can be specified a priori. Overall, by decoupling clustering from learning, the method provides a task-agnostic and efficient mechanism for autonomous collaboration under non-independently and identically distributed data.

</details>


### [188] [Muon learns balanced solutions in matrix factorization without slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30509)
*Mark Rhee,Jamie Simon,Dhruva Karkada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Muon优化器在矩阵分解问题中与梯度下降的三大动力学差异：避免慢速鞍点动力学、超越临界学习率阈值、以及守恒量的变化，并利用Muon的结构特性设计了能在两步内实现近乎完美对齐的学习率调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Muon优化器在矩阵分解这一最小学习问题中的动力学行为，并与梯度下降进行对比，揭示两者在参数轨迹、收敛速度和守恒量等方面的根本差异。

Method: 理论分析Muon和梯度下降在矩阵分解问题中的参数动力学，包括：1) 分析小初始化下的鞍点-鞍点动力学与模式学习顺序；2) 研究学习率超过局部损失锐度临界阈值时的稳定性；3) 推导两种优化器的守恒量（Muon保持√(P^T P)-√(Q^T Q)，梯度下降保持P^T P-Q^T Q）；4) 分析小随机初始化下的自发对齐速率并预测经验对齐速率。

Result: 1) Muon避免慢速鞍点动力学，以相同速率学习所有顶模，较小模式先收敛；2) Muon在学习率超过临界阈值时仍保持稳定，解除了学习率与条件数的耦合，通过指数学习率退火实现快速收敛；3) Muon流守恒√(P^T P)-√(Q^T Q)，梯度流守恒P^T P-Q^T Q，但两者均能从零初始化找到平衡解；4) 小随机初始化下权重自发对齐，理论导出的对齐速率能预测一般情况下的经验对齐速率；5) 利用Muon结构性质设计了只需两步优化就能达到近乎完美对齐的学习率调度。

Conclusion: Muon优化器在矩阵分解中展现出与梯度下降截然不同的动力学特性，特别是在稳定性、收敛速度和守恒量方面具有优势，其结构性质可被利用来设计高效的学习率调度策略。

Abstract: Matrix factorization (i.e., problems of the form $\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$) is a minimal learning problem that exhibits both nonlinear parameter dynamics and representation learning. In this setting, we study how parameter trajectories under the Muon optimizer differ from those of gradient descent. We identify three main dynamical differences: 1) Muon avoids the slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics from small initialization. Muon instead learns all the top modes of $\mathbf{M}^\star$ at the same rate, with the smaller modes converging first. 2) Muon remains stable even when the learning rate exceeds the critical threshold set by the local loss sharpness. This frees the learning rate from the condition number of the problem, enabling rapid convergence via exponential learning rate annealing. 3) Once the weights are aligned with each other and the target, Muon flow conserves the matrix quantity $\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$, while gradient flow is known to conserve the matrix $\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$. Despite having distinct conserved quantities, both optimizers find the so-called \textit{balanced} solution from vanishing initialization. When training from small random initialization, the weights spontaneously align early in training. We derive the alignment rates in simple settings and show that they predict the empirical alignment rates in general. Finally, we exploit structural properties of Muon to construct a learning rate schedule that achieves near-perfect alignment in only two optimization steps.

</details>


### [189] [ITSPACE: Monotone Gaussian Optimal Transport Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30523)
*Woojoo Na,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITSPACE方法，通过近端极大极小化直接优化精确Bures-Wasserstein目标，在协方差对齐任务中比现有方法更快达到低BW-gap解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵是域适应和高斯嵌入等机器学习流程中的紧凑特征描述符，在中心高斯近似下，未正则化的Wasserstein-2最优传输散度在SPD锥上具有闭式解（Bures-Wasserstein目标），但现有优化方法效率有限。

Method: ITSPACE是一种近端majorization-minimization方法，通过平方根分解中的闭式更新直接优化精确BW目标；每次迭代满足BW目标的充分下降不等式；在非精确极坐标计算下提供显式证书间隙界；天然支持秩限制因子并保持PSD结构。

Result: 在真实协方差对齐基准上，ITSPACE比BW梯度下降、基于其他协方差几何的方法以及熵正则化样本OT基线更快达到低BW-gap解。

Conclusion: ITSPACE是一种轻量级内环原语，适用于在严格步数和计算预算下从无标签目标批次进行适配的场景。

Abstract: Covariance matrices serve as compact descriptors of feature distributions in many machine-learning pipelines, including domain adaptation and Gaussian embeddings. Under a centered Gaussian approximation, the unregularized Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport (OT) discrepancy admits a closed form on covariances given by the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) objective on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) cone. We propose ITSPACE (Iterative Transport for Stable Proximal Alignment of Covariance Embeddings), a proximal majorization-minimization method that directly optimizes this exact BW objective through closed-form updates in a square-root factorization. In exact arithmetic, each iteration satisfies a sufficient-decrease inequality for the BW objective; under inexact polar computations, we provide an explicit certificate-gap bound controlling deviations from exact descent. The resulting iterations preserve PSD structure by construction and naturally support rank-restricted factors, making ITSPACE well-suited as a lightweight inner-loop primitive in settings where adaptation must be performed from unlabeled target batches under strict step and compute budgets. Across real-world covariance-alignment benchmarks, ITSPACE reaches low-BW-gap solutions substantially faster than BW-gradient descent, methods based on other covariance geometries, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines.

</details>


### [190] [Convergence of Continual Learning in Homogeneous Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30559)
*Matan Schliserman,Gon Buzaglo,Itay Evron,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同构模型中弱正则化持续分类问题，将其表征为任务边界集上的连续投影。该结果统一了之前仅限于静态深度模型或持续线性模型的分析。研究表明全局收敛通常失败，但利用非凸投影理论，识别了同构深度网络的规则性属性，保证在随机和循环任务序列下具有局部线性收敛。最后扩展到持续回归，统一了同构模型框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作要么局限于静态（单任务）深度模型，要么局限于持续线性模型，缺乏对更一般的同构模型中持续学习行为的统一理论分析。

Method: 将弱正则化持续分类问题形式化为同构模型下任务边界集上的连续投影；利用非凸投影理论分析深度网络的规则性属性。

Result: 全局收敛通常不成立，但在随机和循环任务序列下可保证局部线性收敛。

Conclusion: 统一了同构模型下持续分类与回归的分析框架。

Abstract: We characterize weakly regularized continual classification in homogeneous models as sequential projections onto task margin sets. This result generalizes prior analyses restricted to either stationary (single-task) deep models or continual linear models. We show that global convergence generally fails, even for simple models linear in data but nonlinear in parameters. Nevertheless, by leveraging results from nonconvex projection theory, we identify regularity properties of homogeneous deep networks that guarantee local linear convergence under random and cyclic task sequences. Finally, we extend our analysis to continual regression, unifying the framework for homogeneous models.

</details>


### [191] [Attractor States Emerge in Multi-Turn LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30571)
*Ting-Wen Ko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究LLM在多智能体开放对话中是否存在吸引子行为（话题无关的稳定行为集），发现自玩对话呈现模型特异性吸引子，混合对话中某些模型（如Claude Haiku）会不对称地吸引其他模型改变风格和立场，而GPT-4.1 nano等模型可塑性较强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于开放多智能体场景，但模型间长期交互的动态机制尚不明确，需要理解其是否具有可预测的稳定行为模式。

Method: 使用7种LLM和20个争议话题，进行自玩和混合玩的二元辩论，在表征空间、话语特征和立场维度上追踪轨迹。

Result: 自玩轨迹呈现模型特异性吸引子；混合辩论中吸引子不对称地影响对话伙伴，Claude Haiku是强吸引子，GPT-4.1 nano可塑性最强。

Conclusion: 开放LLM交互可部分从模型特异性吸引子预测，但受结构化和不对称伙伴影响塑造，有助于设计、预测和监控自主智能体系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in open-ended multi-agent settings, but the long-run dynamics of model--model interaction remain poorly understood. We study whether open-ended LLM discussions exhibit attractor-like behavior, i.e. topic-independent stable sets of behaviors which conversations settle into. Across 7 LLMs and 20 controversial topics, we compare self-play and mixed-play dyadic debates, tracking trajectories in representation space, discourse traits, and stances. We find self-play trajectories to be model-specific attractors that draw their conversation partners asymmetrically in mixed-play debates, influencing the other models' stylistic choices and behavior. For example, Claude Haiku is a strong attractor of other models in latent space, corresponding to other models taking on its traits like metacommentary, and models like GPT-4.1 nano are especially malleable. Our results suggest that open-ended LLM interactions are partially predictable from model-specific attractors, but shaped by structured and asymmetric partner influence. Overall, our analysis sheds some light on the complex behavior of open-ended multi-agent interaction, which we hope is helpful in designing, predicting, and monitoring autonomous agentic systems in the real world.

</details>


### [192] [SWE-INTERACT: Reimagining SWE Benchmarks as User-Driven Long-Horizon Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30573)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Anisha Gunjal,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Interact是一个评估编程智能体在多轮交互式用户驱动软件工程任务中的测试平台。研究发现，在单轮SWE任务上表现良好的模型并不能可靠地迁移到多轮用户驱动工作流中，最强模型解决约50%的单轮任务但仅25%的SWE-Interact任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE基准测试通常提供完整需求并评估智能体的自主实现能力，缺乏对真实开发者工作流中多轮交互、用户意图发现和需求迭代场景的评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能测试智能体在模糊指令、渐进需求、用户反馈下的交互式编码能力的评估框架。

Method: 设计了一个用户模拟器，从模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查智能体工作空间，提供针对性反馈、修订和新约束，直至任务目标完全实现。该设置基于大规模真实编程智能体交互研究，构建了一组多轮交互式软件工程任务基准测试。

Result: 强模型在单轮任务上解决约50%，但在SWE-Interact多轮任务上仅解决约25%。最强模型（Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5）在模糊初始指令下表现强劲，能坚持到所有需求被揭示并写出干净代码，但仍存在过度智能体行为、遗忘需求和技术错误。弱模型在模糊情况下表现差、过早放弃、遗忘或忽略指令、频繁重构代码。

Conclusion: SWE-Interact衡量了模型开发中一个正交的真实世界能力轴：交互式目标发现和与用户循环的迭代优化。强单轮任务表现不能可靠迁移到多轮用户驱动工作流。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Interact, a new testbed for evaluating coding agents on multi-turn, interactive, user-driven software engineering tasks. Existing frontier SWE benchmarks typically provide complete requirements upfront and evaluate agents on autonomous implementation. In contrast, SWE-Interact places agents in a realistic developer workflow: a carefully designed user simulator starts with vague or incomplete instructions, progressively reveals requirements, inspects the agent's workspace, and provides targeted feedback, revisions, and new constraints until the full task goal has been handed off. Grounded in large-scale studies of real coding-agent interactions, this setup tests whether agents can discover user intent, adapt to evolving requirements, and build on their own prior work. Across a suite of frontier and open-weight models, we find that strong performance on single-turn SWE tasks does not reliably transfer to multi-turn, user-driven workflows: the best-performing models solve roughly 50% of single-turn baseline tasks but only 25% of the corresponding SWE-Interact tasks. The strongest models in our evaluation, including Opus 4.8 and GPT 5.5, start strong even in the face of vague initial instructions, persevere until all the requirements are surfaced by the user, integrate them better and write clean code. However, they still suffer from over-agentic coding, forgetting requirements and technical mistakes. Weaker models start poorly under ambiguity, give up early, forget or ignore instructions and rework their code more. Overall, SWE-Interact measures an orthogonal, real-world capability axis for frontier model development: interactive goal discovery and iterative refinement with a user in the loop.

</details>


### [193] [The Fundamental Limits of Valid Transport Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30574)
*Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在极小极大框架下形式化了任意有效传输映射的估计问题，指出在标准稳定性假设下，估计任意传输映射与估计最优传输映射的统计难度相当；但在稳定性假设不成立时，次优传输映射可能被更准确地学习。这为理解现代基于传输的生成方法（如扩散模型、流匹配）的统计极限提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成建模方法（扩散模型、归一化流、流匹配）估计的是传输映射或计划，但并未明确针对最优传输（OT）映射。由于生成建模中传输成本本身无关紧要，研究者倾向于选择统计或计算上更易处理的映射。然而，这些方法的样本复杂度分析因目标映射的解析复杂性而困难。作者旨在建立一个严格的极小极大框架来形式化任意有效传输映射的估计问题，从而导出样本复杂度下界，并阐明何时追求次优映射能带来真正的统计优势。

Method: 采用极小极大（minimax）统计决策理论框架，形式化定义"任意有效传输映射"的估计问题。将扩散模型、流匹配等方法学习到的对象视为传输映射或计划，在该框架下分析其样本复杂度。利用OT文献中的标准稳定性假设（如Lipschitz或强凸性条件），推导估计任意传输映射的下界，并与估计OT映射的难度进行比较。同时通过反例展示当稳定性假设不成立时，次优传输映射的估计误差可显著低于OT映射。

Result: 在OT文献的标准稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射的样本复杂度下界与估计OT映射的下界相同，即两者统计难度相当。当稳定性假设失效时，存在反例表明次优传输映射可以比OT映射被更准确地学习（统计误差更小）。该极小极大框架为基于传输的生成方法提供了严格的样本复杂度下界分析工具。

Conclusion: 该极小极大框架为理解基于传输的现代生成方法的统计极限提供了严谨基础，揭示了在强稳定性条件下追求最优传输映射并无统计代价，但在非稳定设定下次优映射具有统计优势。这指导研究者根据实际问题的稳定性条件选择合适的目标映射，并为流匹配和扩散模型等方法的理论分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: Many modern generative modeling methods, including diffusion models, normalizing flows, and flow matching, estimate transport maps or plans between distributions without explicitly targeting an optimal transport (OT) map. In applications like generative modeling, the transport cost itself is irrelevant, and this makes it natural to target maps which are more tractable from either a statistical or computational standpoint. In this short note, we formalize the task of estimating any valid transport map in a rigorous minimax framework. One consequence of this framing is that it yields sample complexity lower bounds for any method whose learned object is evaluated as a transport map or plan, including flow matching and diffusion-based generative models, in settings where direct analysis would be challenging due to the analytic complexity of the methods and their target maps. We observe that, under standard, though strong, stability assumptions from the OT literature, estimating any valid transport map is statistically as hard as estimating the OT map. We complement these results with some examples showing that when these stability assumptions fail, alternative transport maps can be learned substantially more accurately than the OT map. Our minimax framing provides a rigorous foundation for understanding the statistical limits of modern transport-based generative methods and clarifies when targeting sub-optimal maps can provide real statistical advantages.

</details>


### [194] [One-Step Gradient Delay is Not a Barrier for Large-Scale Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30634)
*Philip Zmushko,Egor Petrov,Nursultan Abdullaev,Mikhail Khrushchev,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了异步流水线并行（PipeDream-2BW）因梯度陈旧导致训练不稳定的普遍认知，证明一阶梯度的性能退化主要取决于优化器选择（AdamW退化严重，而Muon具有鲁棒性），并引入基于Error Feedback的修正方法进一步消除延迟影响，在10B参数规模模型上实现了与同步训练相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步流水线并行（如PipeDream-2BW）能消除流水线气泡、提升吞吐量，但由于梯度陈旧性问题，其实际应用受到限制。本文试图挑战"陈旧性必然导致不稳定"的既有认知，探索在特定优化器下能否实现稳定的异步训练。

Method: 1. 对比分析AdamW与Muon在一阶梯度的陈旧条件下的表现差异；2. 提出优化器无关的Error Feedback修正策略以缓解延迟影响；3. 提供Muon在有/无修正下的收敛性理论分析；4. 在10B参数模型上进行大规模实验验证。

Result: AdamW在一步陈旧梯度下性能严重退化，而Muon表现鲁棒；Error Feedback修正能进一步弥合与同步训练的性能差距；10B参数模型实验证实了异步流水线并行在实际大规模训练中的可行性。

Conclusion: 异步流水线并行在合适的优化器（如Muon）和Error Feedback修正下，可以消除流水线气泡而不牺牲模型质量，为大规模LLM训练提供高效实用的方案。

Abstract: Modern large-scale LLM pretraining benefits from utilizing Pipeline Parallelism; however, synchronous implementations leave GPUs idle during pipeline bubbles, wasting computational resources. Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism eliminates these bubbles, maximizing throughput at the cost of gradient staleness. Among asynchronous schedules, PipeDream-2BW is particularly appealing: unlike the original PipeDream schedule, it ensures a constant one-step gradient delay regardless of pipeline depth. However, its adoption remains limited due to the common belief that optimizing under staleness is fundamentally unstable. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that degradation under one-step delay depends strongly on optimizer choice rather than being an intrinsic limitation. We provide the first comprehensive empirical analysis showing that while AdamW, the predominant optimizer at the time when PipeDream-2BW was introduced, indeed suffers from severe degradation, recent methods like Muon exhibit strong robustness under a one-step delay. We introduce an optimizer-agnostic Error Feedback-inspired correction to further mitigate delay effects. We provide supporting theoretical analysis demonstrating convergence for Muon with and without this correction. Extensive evaluation on models up to 10B parameters confirms that our strategies bridge the performance gap with synchronous training, highlighting the practical potential of asynchronous pipeline parallelism at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Concurrent Splay-Based Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28889)
*Vitaly Aksenov,Rene van Bevern,Artem Shilkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于并发二叉搜索树的类Splay旋转设计，通过基于访问计数的双深度阈值控制节点旋转，在倾斜工作负载下减少根节点竞争的同时保持静态最优性，实验表明在多种倾斜负载上能提升并发AVL树的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发有序索引数据结构多关注最坏情况保证，但实际工作负载的访问分布往往非均匀（如Zipf分布）。顺序场景下存在高效的分布自适应数据结构，但使其在并发场景下高效实现较为复杂。Splay树作为最著名的分布自适应结构，虽能提升倾斜负载性能，但将访问节点移至根部的做法在并发环境下会导致根节点附近的严重竞争。

Method: 提出一种基于两个深度阈值的旋转设计：下阈值和上阈值基于节点被访问次数计算出的静态最优性复杂度确定。节点仅在深度超过上阈值时被旋转，且旋转在达到下阈值前停止。该设计旨在保留Splay在倾斜负载上的主要实用优势，同时减少根附近竞争。提出两种变体：基于精确64位计数器和基于6位近似计数器的版本。证明了对应顺序只读树的静态最优性，并在Bronson等人的并发AVL树基础上实现了两种旋转设计。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种倾斜工作负载上能够提升并发AVL树的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过限制旋转范围（不将节点旋转到根）并基于访问计数控制旋转时机，能够在并发场景下有效平衡分布自适应带来的性能收益和并发竞争开销，为构建高效的并发分布自适应数据结构提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Most work on efficient concurrent ordered indices, such as concurrent binary search trees, B-trees, skip lists, etc., has focused on data structures that provide good \emph{worst-case} guarantees. In real workloads, objects are often accessed at different rates, since access distributions may be non-uniform. Many efficient distribution-adaptive data structures exist in the sequential case; however, they are often complicated to make efficient in the concurrent case.
  The most prominent distribution-adaptive data structure is Splay Tree. Its most important advantage is that it does not store any balancing information and provides a reasonable performance improvement on extremely skewed workloads, such as Zipfian workloads. This paper proposes a splay-like rotation design for concurrent binary search trees. Instead of moving an accessed node to the root, rotations use two depth thresholds that are based on the static-optimality complexity computed from the number of accesses to the node: a node is rotated only when it is substantially deeper than the upper threshold, and rotations of the node stop before reaching the lower threshold. This design aims to preserve the main practical benefit of splaying on skewed workloads while reducing contention near the root.
  We present two variants of the rotation design: one using an exact 64-bit access counter per node and one using a 6-bit approximate counter. We prove static optimality for the corresponding sequential read-only tree and evaluate both rotation designs by implementing them on top of the concurrent AVL tree of Bronson et al. Our experiments show that the approach can improve throughput on several skewed workloads.

</details>


### [196] [Five Ways to Build a Concurrent Linked From Coarse-Grain Locking to Lock-Free Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28972)
*Zeeshan Mohammed Rangrej*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种多线程并发链表实现方案（从粗粒度锁到无锁设计），在C++中实现并通过不同读写负载和列表大小进行性能评测，发现最优算法高度依赖使用场景：粗粒度锁和惰性链表在读密集、小键范围场景占优，无锁链表在大键范围和高线程数场景具有竞争力，而细粒度锁因节点级锁开销过大表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多线程环境下并发访问链表存在正确性与效率的复杂性，需要系统性地比较不同并发控制策略（从简单锁到无锁设计）在不同工作负载下的性能表现，以指导实际开发中的算法选择。

Method: 实现了五种C++并发链表：粗粒度锁、细粒度锁、惰性链表、乐观链表和无锁链表；在三种工作负载（读密集、均衡、写密集）和不同链表规模下进行性能基准测试。

Result: 粗粒度锁和惰性链表在读密集、小键范围场景性能最佳；无锁链表在大键范围和高线程数场景表现有竞争力；细粒度锁在所有测试中表现最差，节点级锁开销过高。

Conclusion: 并发链表的最优实现选择高度依赖具体使用场景（读写比例和链表规模），不存在普适的最优方案；细粒度锁的理论优势在实践中难以兑现。

Abstract: Linked lists are one of the most basic data structures in computer science. But when many threads try to use the same linked list at the same time, things get complicated. In this paper, we look at five different ways to make a linked list work correctly and efficiently with multiple threads running at once. We start with the simplest approach -- one big lock for the whole list -- and step by step improve it, ending with a lock-free design that uses no locks at all. We implemented all five versions in C++ and measured how fast each one is across different workloads (read-heavy, balanced, and write-heavy) and different list sizes. Our results show that the right choice of algorithm depends heavily on how the list is used: the coarse-grain and lazy lists win under read-heavy workloads with small key ranges, while the lock-free list becomes competitive when key ranges are large and more threads are running. Fine-grain locking, despite its theoretical appeal, pays a heavy cost from per-node lock overhead and consistently performs the worst in our tests.

</details>


### [197] [Importance-Aware Resource Allocation for Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29052)
*Kaiyi Lei,Yuanzhe Peng,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出iCoTASC框架，利用归因重要性和离线预计算实现轻量级在线调度，解决面向任务的语义通信中资源分配的计算开销大和需重训练的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向任务的语义通信方案要么在线计算开销过大，要么依赖端到端重训练和慢变信道假设，无法在快速衰落和严格时延约束下高效分配稀缺的无线电资源。

Method: 基于归因重要性指导逐维度嵌入选择作为通信控制信号；通过数据驱动的效用函数建模量化带来的语义收益递减；离线预计算每个发射机的效用查找表，实现查表式的轻量级在线调度和低复杂度自适应调整。

Result: 支持分布式系统中实时、信道自适应的语义资源分配，无需对底层任务推理模型进行重训练。

Conclusion: iCoTASC通过混合离线-在线设计，在保持低在线计算复杂度的同时实现了信道自适应的语义资源分配，适用于协作式多设备语义通信系统。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication must allocate scarce radio resources to semantic features under fast fading wireless conditions and strict end-to-end latency budgets. Existing solutions are either optimization-heavy, leading to prohibitive computational overhead during online operation, or rely on end-to-end retraining procedures together with slowly varying channel assumptions. We propose iCoTASC (importance-aware Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication), a hybrid offline-online framework designed for collaborative multi-device semantic communication systems. iCoTASC leverages attribution-based importance to guide per-dimension embedding selection as a practical communication control signal, models diminishing semantic returns of quantization through a data-driven utility function, and precomputes per-transmitter utility lookup tables offline, which together enable lightweight online scheduling via table lookup and low-complexity refinement under time-varying channels. The proposed framework supports real-time, channel-adaptive semantic resource allocation in distributed systems without requiring retraining of the underlying task inference model.

</details>


### [198] [Are There Manufacturer Differences in Hard-Drive Reliability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29078)
*Christoph Siemroth,Yeomyung Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 基于Backblaze硬盘数据集，使用两种持续时间回归模型分析四大硬盘制造商（HGST、Seagate、Toshiba、WD）的中短期故障率差异，发现HGST故障率最低（约为Seagate的41%），WD约为Seagate的52%，Toshiba略高于Seagate，同时记录了年龄、容量、温度和位置对故障率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究四大硬盘制造商在短期至中期内硬盘故障率是否存在显著差异，为数据中心存储采购和可靠性评估提供实证依据。

Method: 使用Backblaze HDD数据集，采用两种不同的持续时间回归模型（duration regression models），控制驱动器年龄、容量、外形尺寸和温度等变量，比较四大制造商的故障率差异。

Result: 在控制相关变量后：HGST故障率最低，约为Seagate的41%；WD约为Seagate的52%；Toshiba故障率略高于Seagate。同时发现了年龄、容量、温度和驱动器位置对故障率的影响效应。

Conclusion: 不同硬盘制造商的故障率存在显著差异，HGST可靠性最优，WD次之，Seagate和Toshiba相对较高；且驱动器年龄、容量、温度及安装位置等物理因素均对故障率有显著影响。

Abstract: Based on the Backblaze hard disk drive (HDD) dataset, we analyze whether the four major HDD manufacturers represented in the dataset -- HGST, Seagate, Toshiba, Western Digital (WD) -- show differences in short- to medium-term HDD failure rates. Using two different duration regression models, we find -- holding constant drive age, capacity, form-factor, and drive temperature -- that Toshiba's failure rate is slightly above Seagate's. HGST HDD failure rates are the lowest, about 41% of Seagate's. WD HDD failure rates are significantly above HGST's, but still only about 52% of Seagate's. We also document the effects of age, capacity, temperature and drive location on failure rates.

</details>


### [199] [KernelFlume: Elastic Core-Attention Scaling for Agentic Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29207)
*Guangyu Xiang,Xueze Kang,Lin Zhang,Wenxiang Lin,Shaohuai Shi,Yuxin Wang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelFlume提出了一种面向LLM解码的解耦架构，将稳定的投影/FFN计算与核心注意力计算分离。权重节点执行密集投影/FFN核函数，无权重注意力节点存储KV分区，可按需弹性扩展。通过路由表动态映射token范围、基于查询优先的注意力调度和层间流水线重叠等技术，在真实测试中相比ServerlessLLM降低32-61%的token输出成本，模拟中成本降低达56-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在面对突发长上下文解码工作负载时，采用按实例弹性扩展（启动新服务实例和完整模型副本）的方式增加KV容量。这种方式存在启动延迟高、内存开销大、批处理碎片化等问题，无法高效应对动态长上下文场景。

Method: 1) 将投影/FFN（权重节点）与核心注意力计算（无权重注意力节点）解耦，注意力节点存储token范围的KV分区；2) 维护路由表在token边界动态映射token范围到注意力节点；3) 使用查询优先的核心注意力调度和层间核流水线，重叠远程注意力通信与本地投影/FFN计算。系统基于UCX通信和CUDA Graph实现低延迟。

Result: 真实GPU测试：A6000上p99 TPOT约74ms，H100上约34ms。相比ServerlessLLM，每百万输出token成本在A6000上降低32%，H100上降低61%。模拟中56-66%成本降低，使用廉价异构注意力节点硬件可达80-85%，并扩展至百万token上下文范围。

Conclusion: KernelFlume通过解耦架构实现了高效的长上下文LLM服务弹性扩展，显著降低了成本并保持了低延迟，尤其适用于智能体、推理模型等动态长上下文工作负载。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly dominated by long and dynamic decode workloads from agents, reasoning models, and extended conversations. When bursty long-context demand exceeds deployed capacity, existing serving systems typically scale out by launching additional serving instances with model replicas. This instance-level elasticity increases KV capacity only by provisioning another full copy of the model, inheriting startup latency, memory overhead, and batch fragmentation.
  We present KernelFlume, a decode-centric architecture that disaggregates the stable projection/FFN path from core-attention computation: weight nodes execute dense projection/FFN kernels, while weightless attention nodes store token-range KV partitions and scale with request-state demand. To make this separation elastic, KernelFlume maintains a routing table that maps token ranges to attention-node endpoints. It updates routes at token boundaries and uses host-visible graph signals to drive pre-registered UCX endpoint communication outside the captured CUDA Graph. To preserve low per-token latency after disaggregation, KernelFlume combines query-first core-attention dispatch with inter-layer kernel pipelining, overlapping remote attention and communication with local projection/FFN work. On real GPU testbeds (intra-node A6000 and cross-node H100), under a dynamic long-context agentic workload serving Llama-3.1-8B, KernelFlume sustains flat p99 TPOTs of ~74 ms on A6000 and ~34 ms on H100, while lowering cost per million output tokens by up to 32% and 61%, respectively, relative to full-instance elastic scaling with ServerlessLLM, a state-of-the-art instance-startup method. Replaying the same trace at larger model scale in simulation projects a 56--66% cost reduction over ServerlessLLM, widening to 80--85% with cheaper heterogeneous attention-node hardware and persisting into the million-token context range.

</details>


### [200] [Fog Computing and Large Language Models: A vision for the mutual beneficiaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29483)
*Satish Narayana Srirama*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了雾计算与大语言模型（LLMs）之间的双向赋能关系：雾计算可解决LLM的云中心化带来的延迟、网络负载和隐私问题，而LLM的代码生成能力可促进雾应用的动态部署。文章综述了当前技术现状与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM传统上依赖云中心部署，面临与早期物联网相同的延迟、网络负载和隐私问题，而这些问题正是雾计算所要解决的。因此需要将LLM优化后部署到雾基础设施上，同时雾计算也能从LLM的代码生成能力中获益。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，通过分析LLM优化技术（参数权重量化、剪枝、低秩适应等）以及雾计算动态应用部署需求，系统地讨论了两者之间的双向赋能关系。

Result: 揭示了LLM与雾计算之间的相互促进作用：LLM通过优化可在雾节点上运行，解决云中心化问题；雾计算可借助LLM的代码生成能力实现应用的动态部署。

Conclusion: 雾计算和LLM是双向受益者，未来研究应聚焦于LLM在雾基础设施上的高效部署以及LLM驱动的雾应用自动化生成。

Abstract: Fog computing utilizes proximal computational resources for sensor data processing and actuation, and addresses the latency, network load, and privacy issues of cloud-centric Internet of Things. On the other hand, Large Language Models (LLMs) are a type of deep learning AI models, which are trained on enormous text data, that perform various natural language processing tasks such as translation, question answering, text summarization, and code generation. LLMs are generally cloud-centric, requiring abundant GPU memory and computing capabilities, again face the same issues that led to fog computing. This pushes the necessity for LLM support in the proximity on fog infrastructure, requiring LLM optimizations such as parameter-weight quantization, pruning, low-rank adaptation etc. Meanwhile, fog computing also gets benefit from LLM's ability for code generation, in the dynamic deployment of fog-based applications. The paper addresses how both fog computing and LLMs can be mutual beneficiaries, discussing the state-of-the-art and future research scope.

</details>


### [201] [Energy-Efficient Multimodal Inference Serving with Tri-serve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29629)
*Ziyang Jia,Sara Rashidi Golrouye,Laxmi Bhuyan,Benjamin Kubwimana,Devashree Tripathy,Zexin Li,Cong Liu,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Tri-serve是一种软件DVFS控制器，通过解决多模态推理服务中的三类功耗低效问题（级间依赖停顿、算术强度与频率的反相关、热节流），在无损延迟和吞吐量的情况下实现22%的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型推理的性能需求不断增长，导致大量能耗。GPU的片上电源管理单元（PMU）自主进行频率决策，但这些硬件管理的频率决策会造成显著的功耗低效。

Method: 提出了Tri-serve，一种基于软件的DVFS控制器，联合考虑三类低效问题：级间依赖停顿、算术强度对频率和功率的影响、高热强度阶段的热节流效应，在商用GPU上实现节能的多模态推理服务。

Result: Tri-serve在不影响延迟或吞吐量的情况下，实现了22%的能效提升。

Conclusion: 通过软件层面的DVFS控制，可以有效弥补硬件PMU在频率决策上的不足，显著提升多模态推理服务的能效。

Abstract: Multimodal model inference creates substantial energy demand with growing performance requirements. Within GPUs, power is autonomously managed by an on-board power management unit (PMU), which makes frequency boosting/throttling decisions. However, we find that these hardware-managed frequency decisions can cause significant power inefficiency.
  This work identifies three classes of power inefficiencies within modern multimodal inference serving: (1) inter-stage dependency stalls run at near maximum frequency despite being idle; (2) anti-correlation between auto-boost frequency and arithmetic intensity (A.I.) results in compute-bound phases (e.g., prefill) running at lower frequency and vice versa; and (3) thermal throttling degrades SM frequency and throughput.
  We propose Tri-serve, a software-based DVFS controller that jointly accounts for three classes of inefficiency -- inter-stage Dependency stalls, the Arithmetic-intensity effect on frequency and power, and the Thermal-throttling effect of high A.I. phases -- to deliver energy-efficient multimodal serving on commodity GPUs. We show that Tri-serve achieves 22% energy efficiency improvement with no latency or throughput impacts.

</details>


### [202] [NI-ORCA: A Parallel Algorithm for Counting the Orbits of Non-Induced Graphlets up to K4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29651)
*Syed Ibtisam Tauhidi,Arindam Karmakar,Thai Son Mai,Hans Vandierendonck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出NI-ORCA，一种并行算法，用于高效计算网络中非诱导图子图（大小不超过4）的轨道计数。该算法扩展了ORCA框架，结合三角形计数、4-团枚举和轨道求解三个阶段，采用阶段特定的并行化策略，在八个真实数据集和合成图上实现高达30倍加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法能高效计算诱导图子图的轨道，但许多实际应用需要非诱导轨道计数，而目前没有方法能提供精确、可扩展且并行的非诱导轨道计数支持。

Method: NI-ORCA扩展ORCA框架用于非诱导轨道计数，通过重新表述线性方程组实现。算法包含三个阶段：三角形计数、4-团枚举和轨道求解。采用线程和顶点局部内存模型及数据结构设计阶段特定的并行化策略，最小化竞争并平衡工作负载。分析了调度策略、块大小和亲和性策略对性能的影响。

Result: 在八个真实数据集和合成Erdős-Rényi图上的实验表明，结合阶段特定数据结构与动态调度及小块大小的混合模式能提供一致的加速比和有效的负载均衡。NI-ORCA相比最先进的顺序算法显著提升性能，最高可达30倍加速比。

Conclusion: NI-ORCA是首个支持精确、可扩展、并行的非诱导图子图轨道计数方法，在性能上大幅超越现有顺序算法，为非诱导轨道计数在实际应用中的大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Counting the orbits of graphlets in a network is a vital tool for understanding the structural roles of vertices in various graph analytics tasks. While existing algorithms efficiently compute orbits of induced graphlets, many real-world applications require non-induced orbit counts. However, no current method offers exact, scalable, and parallel support for non-induced orbit counting. This paper presents NI-ORCA, a parallel algorithm to efficiently compute the orbits of non-induced graphlets up to size four (4-clique). NI-ORCA extends the ORCA framework for non-induced orbit counting by reformulating a system of linear equations. The algorithm consists of three stages: triangle counting, 4-clique enumeration, and orbit solving. We design and implement stage-specific parallelisation strategies using thread and vertex-local memory models and data structures, minimising contention and balancing workload. We further analyse the impact of scheduling policies, chunk sizes, and affinity strategies on performance. Experimental analysis on eight real-world datasets and a series of synthetic Erddos-Renyi graphs demonstrates that a mixed mode combining stage-specific data structure, with dynamic scheduling with small chunk sizes, delivers consistent speedup and effective load balancing. Our results show that NI-ORCA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art sequential algorithms, achieving up to 30x speedups.

</details>


### [203] [Demystifying the Design Space and Best Practices for Heterogeneous LLM Inference and Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29708)
*Zhixin Wang,Zhengbo Wang,Fangcheng Fu,Yinhui Lu,Jinlong Hou,Yijie Chen,Xiaowei Shen,He Liu,Xiangbin Li,Jun Chen,Ruya Gu,Dian Wang,Zhou Tan,Yuan Cheng,Hongzhou Zhang,Xiangjun Huang,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对异构预填充-解码（PD）推理的设计空间进行了系统化分析，识别出四个关键设计轴（加速器、精度、互连、KV驻留）和三个关键边界决策（计算放置、KV表示、KV所有权），并指出精度策略应归属运行时角色而非全局设置、KV传输应关注字节语义而非张量语义、KV生命周期需要显式所有权管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构PD推理部署中，各配置独立决策，缺乏跨配置的全局视角，不清楚哪些决策必须在PD边界联合做出、哪些可以独立做出，因此需要系统化分析设计空间并识别关键交互约束。

Method: 沿四个设计轴（加速器、精度、互连、KV驻留）组织设计空间，通过分析工作负载阶段压力，识别出只有部分交互会形成约束，这些约束通过三个边界决策（计算放置、KV表示、KV所有权）体现。

Result: 精度策略应绑定运行时角色而非全局设置；KV传输引擎应处理字节迁移而非张量语义；KV交接包含跨预填充和解码的生命周期管理（预留、释放、故障恢复），需要显式所有权。

Conclusion: 异构PD推理需要在边界处进行联合决策，精度、KV表示和KV所有权是核心设计约束，提供了基于工业部署观察和运行时源码分析的具体设计指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous prefill-decode (PD) inference is now in production: prefill on cost-efficient or supply-available accelerators, decode on bandwidth-strong ones, and KV state crossing mixed interconnects in mixed numerical formats. Each deployment makes these decisions on its own. What is missing is the picture across configurations-which decisions must be made jointly at the PD boundary, and which can be made independently. We propose a design space organized along four design axes-accelerator, precision, interconnect, and KV residency and the workload regime (stage pressure) they respond to. We show that only a subset of interactions among these factors become binding constraints once PD inference becomes heterogeneous. These interactions surface through three recurring boundary decisions: compute placement, KV representation, and KV ownership. The resulting analysis yields concrete guidance. Precision policy belongs to runtime roles rather than to a single system-wide setting, because the same low-bit format relieves different bottlenecks on each side of the boundary. KV transfer engines move bytes rather than tensor semantics, making representation compatibility an explicit boundary concern whenever producer and consumer differ. The KV handoff also carries a lifecycle-reservation, release, and failure recovery-that spans prefill and decode and requires explicit ownership. Two further interactions remain open. Cross-vendor and interconnect-related claims are stated as design guidance grounded in industrial deployment observations and source-code inspection of the runtimes involved.

</details>


### [204] [SMART-MIG: A Learning Framework for Scalable and Energy-Efficient GPU Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29775)
*Wenqing Yu,Neel Karia,Tanvi Hisaria,Clifford Stein,Olivier Tardieu,Asser Tantawi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SMART-MIG系统，结合均值场多智能体强化学习（MF-MARL）进行大规模MIG重分区，并采用定制启发式算法进行作业调度，在GPU集群中实现能耗与时延效率的联合优化，相比静态分区方案能耗-时延效率提升18%，且能耗仅高出理论下界27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU多实例（MIG）技术允许在GPU分区上运行小模型以提高利用率和降低能耗，但大规模调度中如何同时优化GPU分区决策和作业调度以实现能耗-时延效率，且保持算法复杂度可控，是当前面临的重大挑战。

Method: 采用均值场多智能体强化学习（MF-MARL）进行大规模MIG重分区，结合定制启发式算法进行作业调度；重分区组件的复杂度在作业和GPU数量增加时保持恒定；并建立能耗和时延的理论下界以进行性能基准评估。

Result: SMART-MIG相比静态分区方案能耗-时延效率提升18%，能耗仅比理论下界高出27%，且重分区算法复杂度不随系统规模增长。

Conclusion: SMART-MIG通过MF-MARL与启发式调度相结合，有效解决了大规模MIG系统中能耗与时延的联合优化难题，在可接受的复杂度范围内实现了接近理论最优的性能。

Abstract: The emergence of Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) technology enables us to run smaller machine learning models on partitions of a GPU rather than the entire device, thus improving utilization and reducing energy consumption, albeit with potential performance trade-offs. Meanwhile, the growing energy demands of GPU-equipped data centers motivate the development of online partitioning and scheduling schemes that not only ensure fast job processing but also achieve high energy efficiency. However, achieving energy-tardiness efficiency with manageable algorithmic complexity in large-scale scheduling remains a great challenge, due to the dual objectives of deciding on the GPU partitions and scheduling jobs onto the slices of the heterogeneous partitions. To address this challenge, we propose SMART-MIG, a parallel computing system that combines Mean-Field Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MF-MARL) for large-scale MIG repartitioning with tailored heuristic algorithms for job scheduling. We demonstrate that the complexity of the repartitioning component remains constant even as the number of jobs and GPUs increases. We also establish theoretical lower bounds on energy consumption and tardiness to rigorously benchmark system performance. Finally, extensive experiments show that SMART-MIG improves the energy-tardiness efficiency by $18\%$ compared to its corresponding static-partitioning counterpart, while being only $27\%$ above the theoretical lower bound on energy consumption.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Uniform Experts: Cost-Aware Expert Execution for Efficient Multi-Device MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29982)
*Hui Zang,Pengfei Xia,Hong Liu,Jiajia Chu,Tuo Hao,Minghao Chen,Rui Zhang,Ziyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CAEE框架，通过硬件引导的专家执行优化，在MoE模型中减少8%-18%推理延迟，同时保持准确率下降低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽能实现大规模稀疏激活，但存在两个关键问题：(1)重要性无关成本——低贡献专家占用相同内存和传输成本，导致成本效益比低；(2)系统级不平衡——多设备部署受限于最慢设备，局部优化未必改善端到端延迟。

Method: 提出CAEE（Cost-Aware Expert Execution），一个硬件引导的运行时框架：使用轻量级校准成本模型估算硬件开销，选择性剪枝低重要性高成本专家，并通过低开销补偿机制重新分配其贡献，避免额外数据移动。

Result: 在671B DeepSeek-R1模型上，CAEE在多种部署配置（包括专家卸载和多设备系统上的设备内执行）下，实现端到端推理延迟降低8%-18%，模型准确率下降低于1%。

Conclusion: CAEE通过联合优化token级专家重要性和系统级执行成本，有效缓解了MoE推理中的数据移动瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable language models to achieve unprecedented scale via sparse activation. However, their inference performance is often limited by data movement bottlenecks. Two coupled challenges exacerbate this limtation: (1) Importance-Agnostic Cost: Low-contribution experts incur nearly uniform memory and transfer costs, resulting in a low cost-to-benefit ratio and wasting critical bandwidth; (2) System-Level Imbalance: Multi-device deployments are universally bottlenecked by the slowest device, meaning that local reductions on one device may yield no improvement in end-to-end latency. We propose Cost-Aware Expert Execution (CAEE), a hardware-guided runtime framework that jointly optimizes for token-level expert importance and system-level execution cost. CAEE uses lightweight, calibrated cost models to estimate hardware overhead, selectively prunes low-importance, high-cost experts, and redistributes their contributions via a low-overhead compensation mechanism, avoiding extra data movement. Evaluations on the 671B DeepSeek-R1 model show that CAEE can reduce end-to-end inference latency by 8\%-18\% across diverse deployment settings, including expert offloading and on-device execution on multi-device systems, while maintaining a model accuracy drop of less than 1\%.

</details>


### [206] [FBench: A Flexible Benchmark for CFG-Based What-If Exploration of HPC I/O Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30197)
*Zhaobin Zhu,Chen Wang,Kathryn Mohror,Sarah Neuwirth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出FBench，一种基于上下文无关文法的灵活、代码透明的基准测试工具，用于HPC应用的I/O性能探索与what-if分析，无需重复运行完整应用即可评估不同优化和文件系统配置的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模HPC应用的I/O性能受访问模式、中间件优化和文件系统配置的复杂交互影响，传统方法需要反复运行完整应用进行探索，效率低下且成本高昂。

Method: 从Recorder轨迹中提取上下文无关文法(CFG)，生成简化的全局配置文件或实时重放I/O模式；支持POSIX和MPI-IO接口；允许用户通过JSON配置文件注入优化提示，无需修改代码即可快速实验。

Result: FBench准确复现合成与真实负载的I/O行为；在IOR和HACC-IO中匹配扩展行为和Lustre条带参数敏感性；发现FLASH Sedov中集体I/O写带宽比独立I/O低30倍，切换至突发缓冲区文件系统可提升非集体写带宽约1.5倍；LAMMPS评估中实现高达8倍改进。

Conclusion: FBench能显著减少what-if分析所需时间，为HPC I/O性能探索提供高效、灵活的基准测试方案。

Abstract: The I/O performance of large-scale HPC applications depends on a complex interplay of access patterns, middleware optimizations, and file system configurations. To systematically explore these effects without repeatedly rerunning full applications, we introduce FBench, a flexible and code-transparent benchmarking tool for what-if analysis and I/O performance exploration. FBench leverages context-free grammars (CFGs) derived from Recorder traces to either generate simplified global configuration files for benchmark execution or replay I/O patterns on-the-fly without additional preprocessing. It supports both POSIX and MPI-IO interfaces and allows users to inject optimization hints via JSON configuration files, enabling rapid experimentation with I/O settings without code changes. Our evaluation shows that FBench accurately reproduces I/O behavior for both synthetic and real workloads, capturing access patterns and performance trends across diverse optimizations and file system settings. For IOR and HACC-IO, FBench closely matches scaling behavior and sensitivity to Lustre striping parameters. For FLASH Sedov, it reveals that collective I/O on Lustre can yield up to 30x lower write bandwidth than independent I/O, largely independent of striping, and that switching to a burst buffer file system increases non-collective write bandwidth by about 1.5x without additional tuning. The evaluation with LAMMPS shows that FBench can significantly reduce the time required for what-if analyses and, with simple tuning, enable improvements of up to 8x.

</details>


### [207] [Energy-Aware Scheduling for Serverless LLM Serving on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30391)
*Tianyu Wang,Gourav Rattihalli,Aditya Dhakal,Longfei Shangguan,Dejan Milojicic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Festina是一个面向无服务器LLM推理服务的节能控制平面，通过协同调度请求放置、SM分区和GPU运行点，在满足SLO的前提下实现集群级能耗优化，最高可降低56%能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理已成为主要云工作负载，其能耗快速增长使得集群级能耗优化日益重要。无服务器LLM服务通过弹性共享GPU资源来应对流量波动，但这种共享也带来了能耗优化难题：多个共存模型运行在同一设备级运行点下，而它们在不同执行阶段和负载条件下的资源需求与延迟容忍度各不相同，导致能耗最小化需要跨请求放置、运行时资源适配和工作负载整合进行协同调度。

Method: Festina采用profile-guided、功率感知的控制平面设计。轻量级全局调度器通过离线profile和GPU状态摘要进行常数时间查询，实现SLO安全的能耗感知请求放置；每个GPU上的相位感知本地调度器持续调整任务批处理大小和计算资源以最小化功耗；此外，Festina通过SLO感知的迁移进行能耗感知的工作负载整合，减少GPU静态功耗。

Result: 与四种SOTA LLM服务系统和一种DVFS增强系统相比，Festina在SLO达成率基本持平（2%以内）的前提下，能耗最高降低56%。

Conclusion: Festina证明了通过协同调度请求放置、运行时资源适配和工作负载整合，可以在不牺牲SLO的前提下显著降低无服务器LLM推理集群的能耗，为LLM推理的绿色计算提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As LLM inference becomes a major cloud workload, its growing energy footprint makes cluster-wide energy optimization increasingly important. Serverless LLM serving helps platforms absorb traffic volatility by elastically sharing GPU resources across models, but this sharing also makes energy optimization difficult. Multiple co-resident models run under one device-wide operating point, while their resource demands and latency slack change across execution phases and load conditions. As a result, minimizing energy requires coordinated scheduling across request placement, runtime resource adaptation, and workload consolidation.
  We present Festina, a profiling-guided, power-aware control plane to minimize cluster-wide energy for serverless LLM serving. Unlike common global-local schedulers that focus on throughput or tail latency, Festina makes energy-first decisions by jointly coordinating request placement, SM partitioning, and GPU operating points under TTFT/TBT SLOs. In our system, a lightweight global scheduler performs fast, SLO-safe, energy-aware placement using constant-time lookups from offline profiles and GPU state summaries. On each GPU, a phase-aware local scheduler continuously adapts task batching and compute resources to minimize power consumption. Festina further performs energy-aware workload consolidation to reduce GPUs' static power consumption via SLO-aware migration. Comparison with four SOTA LLM serving systems and one DVFS-augmented system demonstrates that Festina reduces energy consumption by up to 56% while maintaining parity in SLO attainment (within a 2% margin)

</details>


### [208] [Analyzing Linearizability in Relativistic Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30419)
*Kahbod Aeini,Wojciech Golab*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对广义相对论中时间膨胀效应导致的分布式系统时序问题，在Gilbert和Golab提出的相对论线性izability框架下，为复制状态机和ABD读写寄存器构造提供了形式化正确性证明，且证明了比Jayanti定理更强的相对论线性izability性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦相对论表明时间是相对的，分布式系统不能简单地用时序来建模。Gilbert和Golab虽然提出了相对论线性izability的概念和理论工具，但未给出正式的正确性证明，Jayanti近期指出了这一不足。

Method: 利用Gilbert和Golab的理论工具，对复制状态机以及ABD读写寄存器（包含其变体）进行形式化分析，建立相对论线性izability的严格证明。

Result: 成功证明了这些异步算法满足相对论线性izability，并且证明的性质强于Jayanti核心定理所保证的。

Conclusion: Gilbert和Golab的技术可用于为经典分布式算法建立更强的相对论线性izability保证，填补了此前缺乏形式化证明的空白。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of relativity correctly predicted that time is relative, and subject to both kinematic and gravitational dilation. Therefore, executions of distributed systems cannot always be modeled as sequences of events totally ordered according to wall clock time. To address this fundamental problem, Gilbert and Golab formulated a generalization of Herlihy and Wing's linearizability property for shared objects, which they called \emph{relativistic linearizability}, and introduced a collection of theoretical tools to facilitate rigorous analysis. While they conjectured that several widely-studied classically linearizable algorithms are also relativistically linearizable, their work stopped short of presenting formal proofs of correctness, as pointed out recently by Jayanti. In this paper, we explain how Gilbert and Golab's techniques can be used to establish relativistic linearizability for a replicated state machine, as well as variations of the widely studied read/write register construction of Attiya, Bar-Noy and Dolev (ABD). Our results establish a stronger form of relativistic linearizability than Jayanti's central theorem for these asynchronous algorithms.

</details>


### [209] [GPU Parallelization Strategies for Forward and Backward Propagation in Shallow Neural Networks: A CUDA-Based Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30497)
*Rania Zitouni,Nadine Bousdjira,Sarah Hasnaoui,Amel Sadoun,Fatma Salhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对浅层神经网络前向和反向传播中的CUDA优化策略进行了比较研究，评估了三种优化方法：平铺共享内存（+1列填充消除存储体冲突）、预转置权重矩阵（合并全局内存访问）以及融合MatMul+ReLU核函数（消除中间全局内存往返）。在NVIDIA Tesla T4上的实验表明，完全优化的实现在大数据集（25,600样本）上相比基线CUDA版本实现了1.41倍加速，执行时间从21.0秒降至14.8秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索并评估不同的CUDA优化策略对神经网络前向和反向传播性能的影响，特别是针对内存访问模式优化（共享内存、全局内存合并、核函数融合）在GPU加速深度学习原语中的有效性。

Method: 实现了三种堆叠优化：(1) 平铺共享内存并通过+1列填充消除存储体冲突；(2) 预转置权重矩阵以实现合并的全局内存访问；(3) 融合MatMul+ReLU核函数以减少中间全局内存往返。实验在NVIDIA Tesla T4上进行，使用了三种不同规模的数据集进行对比。

Result: 完全优化的实现在大数据集（25,600样本）上相比基线CUDA版本获得1.41倍加速，执行时间从21.0秒降至14.8秒。

Conclusion: 内存访问优化在GPU加速深度学习原语中非常有效，三种优化策略的堆叠能够显著提升前向和反向传播的性能。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of CUDA optimization strategies applied to forward and backward propagation in a shallow neural network. Three stacked optimizations are evaluated: (1) tiled shared memory with bank-conflict elimination via +1-column padding, (2) pre-transposed weight matrices for coalesced global memory access, and (3) a fused MatMul+ReLU kernel that eliminates intermediate global-memory round-trips. Experiments on an NVIDIA Tesla T4 (CUDA 13.0) across three dataset sizes show that the fully optimized implementation achieves a 1.41x speedup over the baseline CUDA version on the large dataset (25,600 samples), reducing execution time from 21.0s to 14.8s. Results are compared against a sequential CPU baseline and an OpenMP parallel implementation, demonstrating the effectiveness of memory-access optimization in GPU-accelerated deep learning primitives.

</details>


### [210] [Spandana: Reconciling Strict SLOs with Low Cost under Fine-Grained Load Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30533)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,Zeyu Xu,Marton Nemeth,Shengda Zhu,Marios Kogias,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spandana通过将SLO执行与成本优化解耦，采用轻量级控制器将请求在VM和FaaS之间动态路由，实现了严格的SLO遵守、76-86%的CPU利用率和比三种SOTA基线低5-44%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务面临亚秒级负载波动，现有自动伸缩、FaaS部署及混合系统无法同时满足严格SLO遵守、低成本和高的资源利用率。

Method: Spandana架构将SLO执行与成本优化解耦：每个应用VM上的轻量级控制器根据请求能否满足SLO将其路由到VM或FaaS；资源分配器通过考虑VM成本、FaaS成本和流量波动性来确定最优VM配置，使VM池保持高利用率。

Result: Spandana保持严格的SLO遵守，实现76-86%的CPU利用率，相比三种SOTA基线降低成本5-44%。

Conclusion: Spandana通过VM与FaaS混合架构的解耦设计，有效解决了严格SLO与低成本高利用率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Cloud-based online services face significant sub-second load fluctuations while needing to meet strict Service Level Objectives (SLOs). Cluster operators often over-provision resources to protect SLOs, sacrificing utilization and cost efficiency. Existing reactive and proactive autoscalers, serverless (FaaS) deployments, and VM/FaaS hybrid systems fail to reconcile strict SLO compliance with low cost and high utilization under fine-grained load fluctuation.
  We introduce Spandana, an architecture that addresses this trade off by decoupling SLO enforcement from cost optimization. A lightweight controller colocated with each application VM enforces SLOs by steering each arriving request between the VM and FaaS. Requests that can meet the SLO stay on the VM; the remaining requests are forwarded to a stock FaaS layer such as AWS Lambda. For cost optimization, Spandana's resource allocator determines the most-efficient VM provisioning by accounting for VM cost, FaaS cost, and traffic volatility, allowing the VM pool to run at high utilization. Our evaluation shows that Spandana maintains strict SLO adherence, achieves 76-86% CPU utilization, and reduces cost by 5-44% over three SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [211] [Data Replication Meets Function Scheduling in the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30563)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Matteo Briscini,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了边缘-云连续体中无服务器计算的函数调度与数据放置联合优化问题，针对强一致性和最终一致性两种模型提出了全局视图贪心算法和聚合视图启发式算法，实验表明数据放置比函数调度对性能影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算在边缘-云连续体中具有吸引力，但其无状态假设在有状态应用（需持久数据）时失效——从远端云存储获取数据会抵消边缘部署的延迟优势。保持数据在近端需要复制，而复制又要求对函数执行位置和一致性模型进行联合决策。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为二元线性规划（BLP）求最优解作为基准；2) 提出全局视图贪心算法（基于完整快照）；3) 提出聚合视图启发式算法（各节点仅基于本地观测需求独立决策）。

Result: 全局视图贪心算法在保持接近最优（相差几个百分点）的同时可扩展到超过10^4个节点；聚合视图启发式算法在客户端移动场景下能维持低且稳定的延迟，而集中式策略因快照过时导致延迟尖峰；在全部实验中，数据放置对结果的影响大于函数调度。

Conclusion: 对于边缘-云连续体中的无服务器计算，数据放置决策比函数调度更关键；去中心化的聚合视图启发式算法在动态环境中表现出更好的适应性和稳定性。

Abstract: Serverless computing is an appealing model for the edge-cloud continuum, but its stateless assumption breaks down once functions need persistent data: fetching state from a distant cloud store erases the latency benefit of running at the edge. Keeping data close means replicating it, and replication forces a placement decision that is coupled with where functions execute and with the consistency each application demands. We study this joint problem of function scheduling and data placement under two consistency models, strong and eventual replication. We first formulate it as a Binary Linear Program that yields the optimal placement for a given system snapshot, and use it as a reference point. Because the solver does not scale past a few hundred nodes, we add two heuristics with progressively less information: a Global-View greedy method that works from the same complete snapshot, and an Aggregated-View heuristic in which each node decides from locally observed demand alone. Across a range of system sizes the Global-View heuristic stays within a few percent of the optimum while scaling to over $10^4$ nodes. The Aggregated-View heuristic sacrifices some solution quality, but adapts continuously to each invocation. Under client mobility, centralized policies suffer from stale snapshots and recurring latency spikes, while the Aggregated-View maintains low and stable client-observed latency. Across all experiments, data placement proves more influential than function scheduling in determining the outcome.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自进化智能体），通过三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、剧本）和严格的保留集筛选机制实现无需权重更新的智能体进化。在四个基准测试中，RSEA在ALFWorld上表现最佳（69.3%），但不同方法各有优劣；无保护的上下文进化存在高风险；RSEA的严格保留集选择确保了递归自进化的单调安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过演化自然语言工件（如反思、工作流、提示优化等）来提升性能而无需更新权重，但这些方法通常只在单一基准上报告成功，缺乏系统的横向对比。作者希望进行公平的苹果对苹果比较，揭示不同方法的真实表现和潜在问题。

Method: 提出RSEA框架，维护三层自然语言状态：命令式策略（strategy）、可复用技能（skills）和程序化剧本（playbook）。每代进化中，RSEA从自身轨迹重写所有三层，并使用严格的keep-better门控机制——仅当候选版本在不相交的保留集上不退化时才提交。

Result: 在ALFWorld、GAIA、(τ)-bench和WebShop四个基准上，与ReAct、Reflexion、GEPA、AWM、ACE、Dynamic Cheatsheet六个基线对比，使用统一本地骨干模型。三大发现：1）没有通用最优方法，RSEA在ALFWorld上单次最优（69.3% vs ReAct 64.6%），重试达79.4%；AWM在强骨干工具任务上最优。2）无保护的上下文进化（Dynamic Cheatsheet）高方差且不安全，在ALFWorld上接近最优（70.7%），但在WebShop上崩溃（0.14 vs ReAct 0.43）。3）RSEA的严格保留集选择使递归自进化具有单调安全性，在所有基准上从未显著低于基座智能体，退化时自动回退到原始ReAct。

Conclusion: 不同进化方法在不同任务上各有优劣，不存在普适最优的工件形式；无保护的上下文进化具有高方差和不可靠性；严格的保留集验证机制是实现安全递归自进化的关键，能确保性能不退化。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [213] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力切片"（capability slice）概念，构建了一个从评测到数据的闭环推理框架，通过细粒度的评测分类学、非指令数据分类学及映射规则，将基准级性能失败转化为可定位、可验证的数据干预。两个案例验证了该框架的有效性：一是诊断出持续预训练导致BBH下降的根因是掩码<EOS>损失而非推理能力削弱；二是将数学推理弱点分解为特定操作组合，通过针对性数据采样将AIME2025/2026 Pass@128从个位数提升至26.67。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型能力是LLM预训练的核心变量，但从未被直接观测：数据塑造能力是前瞻性的，评测揭示能力是回顾性的。实际优化中，工程师先观测到失败，然后必须推断语料库的修正方向。评测端（基准名称、样本正确性）和数据端（数据来源、领域、质量标签）使用不兼容的词汇表，导致这一推断通常依赖直觉而非系统方法。

Method: 提出"能力切片"作为基本分析单元——一组共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束的评测样本，粒度精确到可定位单一弱点，同时足够稳定以支持聚合。围绕该单元构建：评测分类学、非指令数据分类学、映射规则，形成闭环，将基准级失败转化为可测试的数据干预。

Result: 案例一：持续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，诊断发现根因是单个掩码<EOS>损失而非推理能力削弱；修复后BBH恢复至66.44，超过原始检查点。案例二：数学推理弱点通过求解操作分解为特定失败组合，针对性采样使AIME2025 Pass@128从6.67提升至26.67，AIME2026从0.00提升至26.67。

Conclusion: 同一未经修改的闭环在两个方向相反的案例中均得出正确结论，表明评测到数据的推理可以是常规化、可审计、可实验验证的，而非依赖直觉。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [214] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPTNT基准测试，基于合作游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》，评估多模态模型在实时、异步协作中的能力。实验表明，当前最先进的闭源和开源模型均无法在实时条件下拆除任何炸弹，而人类玩家可以。通过控制实验，识别出模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面的关键弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试孤立研究协作所需的各种能力（时间压力、信息不对称、不完美通信），缺乏在真实协作条件下综合评估多模态模型性能的测试平台。

Method: 基于合作游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》构建GPTNT基准测试。两名代理需协作拆除程序生成的炸弹：一方可见并操作炸弹但无说明书，另一方有说明书但不可见/操作炸弹。要求代理实时异步通信，并可选择性移除说明书、伙伴以隔离模型的即时推理与记忆能力。

Result: 所有测试的闭源和开源模型均无法在实时条件下拆除任何炸弹，而人类玩家可完成此任务。模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面表现薄弱。

Conclusion: GPTNT揭示了当前多模态模型在实时协作任务中的严重不足，提供了一个可随着模型进步而演化的动态基准，填补了现有评估体系在协作性能方面的空白。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [215] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IMCBench 是一个基于真实临床图像与合成患者档案构建的多轮医疗对话基准，用于评估多模态大模型在安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个临床维度上的表现。实验表明，Claude Opus 4.6 综合得分最高（3.61），但没有模型在所有维度上占优，且安全分数在恶性和罕见疾病中显著下降；消融实验进一步证明，视觉输入和电子健康记录（EHR）上下文对安全指导均有贡献，准确的临床描述不能保证安全的患者指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准存在碎片化问题：有的支持多轮对话但缺乏图像输入，有的提供多模态输入但仅聚焦于单轮问答。缺乏一个既能结合临床图像又能进行多轮对话的评估基准，无法全面评估模型在真实临床交互场景中的表现。

Method: 构建 IMCBench 基准，将真实的公开临床图像与合成患者档案配对，模拟多轮医患对话。采用 LLM-as-Jury 评分机制，经过临床专家标注校准，从安全性、准确性和不确定性使用三个维度对八种多模态前沿模型（涵盖 Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama 四个系列）进行1-5分的评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 综合得分最高（3.61），其次为 Claude Sonnet 4.6（3.30）和 GPT-5.2（3.29），但无模型在所有维度上占优。安全分数在恶性疾病和罕见疾病中均下降0.27。消融实验显示，移除视觉输入后安全得分平均下降0.18，移除EHR上下文后下降0.23，更强模型能更有效利用视觉特征。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述不能保证安全的患者指导，医疗AI评估需要引入涵盖安全性、准确性和不确定性表达的多维度评估框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [216] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaSteer框架，通过分析推理链中"真实性"的几何结构，利用模式聚类和Fisher-LDA提取纯净的真实性表征，并基于前瞻熵动态干预推理轨迹，在数学和编码任务上有效提升了LLM的推理正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如CoT、"Wait" prompts）虽能让模型"想更多"，但未能有效引导模型走向"真理"；RepE虽能进行内在控制，但尚未应用于动态推理轨迹中。

Method: DynaSteer框架包含三部分：(1) 模式聚类解耦推理流形；(2) Fisher-LDA投影提纯真实性表征；(3) 动态监测前瞻熵，选择性引导推理轨迹并在必要时回滚。

Result: 在多个MATH基准测试上验证了DynaSteer的有效性，并在域外编码任务上证实了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了推理链中真实性的三个关键发现：真实性编码在句子层面且与推理模式纠缠、有效干预需遵循不确定性和衰减效应定位到早期高熵分岔点、朴素引导向量存在噪声风险。DynaSteer通过动态、有选择地干预解决了这些问题。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [217] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VirtueMap框架，通过亚里士多德德性伦理学视角评估LLMs在伦理困境中对五种德性（实践智慧、正义、诚实、勇气、节制）的表达程度，基于95%以上一致性的参考排序，使用归一化Borda对齐评分，在九个LLM家族上达到90.3%的平均排名一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
